## ל (id: BDB04729)

ל, twelfth letter; used as numeral 30 in postB. Heb.



## לְ (id: BDB04730)

לְ **prep.** to, for, in regard to (Moab. Ph. ל, Aram. לְ, ܠ Arab. لِ Eth. ለ፡ As. *la* in *lapân* \= לִפְנֵי, DlHWB 530\), before tone\-syllables usu. לָ (Koii. 276 f.); with suff. לִי; לְ, לְכָה † [Gn 27:37](https://ref.ly/Gen27:37), [2 S 18:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:22), [Is 3:6](https://ref.ly/Isa3:6), לְָ; f. לָךְ, לֵכִי † [2 K 4:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:2), Ct [2:13](https://ref.ly/Song2:13) Kt (prob. N. Pal. dialect : cf. Syr. ܠܟ); לוֺ (15 t., acc. to Mas., written incorrectly לאֹ: v. לאֹ *note*); לָהּ; לָהַ † [Nu 32:42](https://ref.ly/Num32:42), [Zc 5:11](https://ref.ly/Zech5:11), [Ru 2:14](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:14), לָ֫נוּ; לָכֶם לָכֶ֫נָה † Ez [13:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:18) (לָכֶן does not occur); לָהֶם, poet. לָ֫מוֺ (55 t., incl. a few cases where, acc. to many, it stands for לוֺ: cf. Ges§ 103\. 2a, n. Di:[Is 44:15](https://ref.ly/Isa44:15), [53:8](https://ref.ly/Isa53:8)), \[also לָהֵ֫מָּה † [Je 14:16](https://ref.ly/Jer14:16)]; לָהֶן \[לָהֵן (q.v.) † [Ru 1:13](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:13), [1:13](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:13), לָהֵ֫נָּה 5 t., v. הֵמָּה]. **Prep.** denoting direction (not properly *motion,* as אֶל) towards, or reference to; and hence used in many varied applications, in some of which the idea of *direction* predominates, in others that of *reference* (cf. GiesebrechtDie Präp. Lamed, 1876\):—**1\.** very often, with various classes of verbs, *to, towards, for:* viz. **a.** verbs of *looking, listening, attending, waiting,* etc., as האמין, התחֹלל, יחל, הוחיל, נכסף, כלה ([Psalm 84:3](https://ref.ly/Ps84:3) \+ ), אזן, הטה, לב (שׁת, הכין, נטה)נתן, קִוָּה, הקשׁיב, שׁמע (sts.), חמא to thirst *for* ([Ex 17:3](https://ref.ly/Exod17:3), [Psalm 42:3](https://ref.ly/Ps42:3)), השׁתחוה (*to* [Gn 37:10](https://ref.ly/Gen37:10), *towards* [Psalm 99:5](https://ref.ly/Ps99:5)); sts. also with האזין, הביט, נִבַּט, ראה, נפשׁ, נשא (see these verbs; many are also construed with other preps.); [Is 51:6](https://ref.ly/Isa51:6). [Psalm 44:21](https://ref.ly/Ps44:21); pregn. [Is 38:14](https://ref.ly/Isa38:14); השׁתאה ל † [Gn 24:21](https://ref.ly/Gen24:21), החרישׁל [Nu 30:5](https://ref.ly/Num30:5), [30:8](https://ref.ly/Num30:8): sts. without a vb., as Ju [5:9](https://ref.ly/Judg5:9) לִבִּי לְ, [Je 5:3](https://ref.ly/Jer5:3) עיניך ל, [Psalm 33:18](https://ref.ly/Ps33:18) (‖אל), [39:8](https://ref.ly/Ps39:8) תוחלתי לך היא, [120:7](https://ref.ly/Ps120:7) המה למלחמה, [130:6](https://ref.ly/Ps130:6) נפשׁי לאדני (cf. [Is 26:8](https://ref.ly/Isa26:8)), [143:6](https://ref.ly/Ps143:6), [Dn 11:27](https://ref.ly/Dan11:27), [2 Ch 3:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:13), [32:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:2). **b.** with verbs of *saying, calling, singing, vowing, sacrificing,* etc., as אמר, דבר (chiefly with God as subj. \= *promise,* [Gn 24:7](https://ref.ly/Gen24:7), [1 K 5:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:26) \+ , esp. in D כאשׁר דבר לְ [Dt 1:11](https://ref.ly/Deut1:11) (v. Dr), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Deut1:21) etc.; with human subj. [Gn 49:28](https://ref.ly/Gen49:28), Ju [14:7](https://ref.ly/Judg14:7), [1 K 2:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:19) al. (Gie42f.: דבר אל is more common), זבח, זמּר, הודה, הגיד, נדר, נשא יד (in oath) Ez [20:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:5), [20:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:6), [20:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:23), [Psalm 106:26](https://ref.ly/Ps106:26), קִטֵר, הריע, נשׁבע, שָׁר etc. **c.** with vbs. of *giving, leaving, bringing, offering* etc., as הביא, הִפִּיל *allot* ([Jos 13:6](https://ref.ly/Josh13:6)), נתן, הסגיר, עזב [Psalm 16:9](https://ref.ly/Ps16:9) abandon *to* Sheol [Is 18:6](https://ref.ly/Isa18:6), הקריבּ [Lv 17:4](https://ref.ly/Lev17:4), שׁוב \= *to be returned* [Dt 28:31](https://ref.ly/Deut28:31), השׁיב \= *bring back* [22:1](https://ref.ly/Deut22:1), \= *requite* [2 S 16:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:12), שִׁלַּח, etc. **d.** with vbs. of dealing, acting *towards* (whether with friendly or hostile intent), as עשה ל [Gn 19:8](https://ref.ly/Gen19:8) \+ oft., גמל ל [Is 3:9](https://ref.ly/Isa3:9), so with הֵייטִב, הֵרַע, הֵמַר, חָטָא to sin *against* ([Gn 20:9](https://ref.ly/Gen20:9) \+), אָשָׁם to be guilty *towards* ([Lv 5:19](https://ref.ly/Lev5:19)), שַׁקֵּר, כִּחֵשׁ to lie *to,* כִּזֵּב; with vbs. of mocking or laughing *against, at,* as לעג לְ [Psalm 2:4](https://ref.ly/Ps2:4), שחק לְ [37:13](https://ref.ly/Ps37:13), שמח לְ to rejoice *over* [35:19](https://ref.ly/Ps35:19), Ez [35:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:15), caus. שִמַּח לְ [Psalm 30:2](https://ref.ly/Ps30:2), עלץ לְ † [25:2](https://ref.ly/Ps25:2): with other verbs denoting hostility (less common than ב or על), [Gn 27:42](https://ref.ly/Gen27:42) מתנחם לך להרגך, [2 K 5:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:7), [Ex 11:7](https://ref.ly/Exod11:7) (so [Jos 10:21](https://ref.ly/Josh10:21): cf. [Jb 16:9](https://ref.ly/Job16:9)), [Je 25:31](https://ref.ly/Jer25:31), [50:9](https://ref.ly/Jer50:9), [Psalm 7:14](https://ref.ly/Ps7:14), [37:12](https://ref.ly/Ps37:12), [56:3](https://ref.ly/Ps56:3), [106:16](https://ref.ly/Ps106:16) קנא לְ (usu. In good sense, **5 g** *c*)[Jb 20:27](https://ref.ly/Job20:27), [34:37](https://ref.ly/Job34:37). And with adjj., as [Psalm 73:1](https://ref.ly/Ps73:1) טוב ל good *to,* [Gn 13:13](https://ref.ly/Gen13:13) ´רָעִים וְחַטָּאִים לי *towards* ´י [2 S 22:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:24) תָּמִים לוֺ (‖ [Psalm 18:24](https://ref.ly/Ps18:24) עִמּוֺ), [Psalm 89:29](https://ref.ly/Ps89:29) נֶאֱמֶנֶת לוֺ; with subst. (rare) [Ex 32:12](https://ref.ly/Exod32:12), [La 3:60](https://ref.ly/Lam3:60) (syn. v:[61](https://ref.ly/Lam3:61) על). **e.** with words denoting what is pleasurable or the reverse, as נעם לְ [2 S 1:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:26), ערב ל [Ho 9:4](https://ref.ly/Hos9:4), יֵמַר לְ [Is 24:7](https://ref.ly/Isa24:7), טוֺב לְ (adj.) [1 S 1:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:8), נָקֵל לְ [2 K 20:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:10), also הועיל ל סכן ל to be profitable *to;* and with neuter vbs., to denote the subj. of a sensation or emotion, as טוֺב לְ to be well *to* (with), [Dt 5:30](https://ref.ly/Deut5:30), [19:13](https://ref.ly/Deut19:13) \+ , מַר לְ [Ru 1:13](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:13), חַר לְ [1 S 13:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:6) \+ oft., כְּרֹב לְ [Ho 10:1](https://ref.ly/Hos10:1), רָוַח לְ [1 S 16:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:23), חַם לְ to be warm *to,* [1 K 1:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:1), רַע לְ [Psalm 106:32](https://ref.ly/Ps106:32), חָרָה לְ it was hot (\= anger arose) *to* [Gn 4:6](https://ref.ly/Gen4:6) \+ oft., חָֽשְׁכָה לְ [Mi 3:6](https://ref.ly/Mic3:6). And with pass. Vbs., נִסְלַח לוֺ it is forgiven *to him*\=he is forgiven [Lv 4:26](https://ref.ly/Lev4:26) \+ oft.; otherwise rare, נִרְצָה לְ [1:4](https://ref.ly/Lev1:4), נִרְפָּא לָנוּ it is healed *to us* \= we are healed [Is 53:5](https://ref.ly/Isa53:5), הוּנַח ל [La 5:5](https://ref.ly/Lam5:5), יְבֻלַּע לְ [2 S 17:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:16) (v. Dr). **f.** with verbs of *reaching to, touching , attaching* etc., as אסר ל to bind *to,* חבשׁ, דבק [Psalm 44:26](https://ref.ly/Ps44:26), מחא to reach *to* [Is 10:10](https://ref.ly/Isa10:10), [10:14](https://ref.ly/Isa10:14), [Psalm 21:9](https://ref.ly/Ps21:9), הגיע [Ex 4:25](https://ref.ly/Exod4:25), נחמד [Nu 25:5](https://ref.ly/Num25:5), קרוב (adj.) [Ru 2:20](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:20), out of connexion with a vb. (almost \= עַד), [Jos 16:1](https://ref.ly/Josh16:1), [Psalm 59:14](https://ref.ly/Ps59:14), [Jb 28:3](https://ref.ly/Job28:3), [Ne 3:15](https://ref.ly/Neh3:15), [2 Ch 33:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:14), and correl. to מִן (v. מִן **5**). **g.** with vbs. of *motion,*as הלך, בא שָׁב etc. (not so common as אל, or the simple acc. with or without ה *loc.*)—(a)with *places,* rare in early prose, [Jos 1:15](https://ref.ly/Josh1:15), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Josh8:14), Ju [1:34](https://ref.ly/Judg1:34), [20:10](https://ref.ly/Judg20:10) (but v. GFM), [1 S 9:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:12), [20:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:25), [2 K 3:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:27), exc. in partic. phrases, viz. למקומו [Gn 18:33](https://ref.ly/Gen18:33), לדרכו [32:2](https://ref.ly/Gen32:2), לאהליו [1 S 4:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:10) (also with other sfs.: all these \+ oft., esp. with שוב and הלך, or preceded by distrib. אישׁ; (ך)לאהליו, also, without vb., as exclam., [2 S 20:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:1), [1 K 12:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:16)), לארצו [Gn 30:25](https://ref.ly/Gen30:25)\+ , אִישׁ לִירֻשָּׁתוֺ [Dt 3:20](https://ref.ly/Deut3:20), אישׁ לנחלתו Jo 24:28 \+ , אישׁ לעירו [1 S 8:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:22), [Ne 13:10](https://ref.ly/Neh13:10): oft. In late Heb., as [Jb 4:5](https://ref.ly/Job4:5), [1 Ch 4:39](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:39), [4:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:42), [5:26](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:26), [12:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:1), [12:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:9), [22:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:18), [24:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:19), [2 Ch 1:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:3), [8:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:17) \+oft. Chr, [Ezr 2:68](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:68) \+ , [Ne 10:35](https://ref.ly/Neh10:35) ff. [Est 6:4](https://ref.ly/Esth6:4), [Psalm 96:8](https://ref.ly/Ps96:8), [132:7](https://ref.ly/Ps132:7), [146:4](https://ref.ly/Ps146:4): לירושׁלם [Je 3:17](https://ref.ly/Jer3:17) b (om. LXX), [Zc 1:16](https://ref.ly/Zech1:16), and oft. Ch Ezr Ne (as [2 Ch 11:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:14), [19:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:1), [30:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:3), [30:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:11)), לשׁמרון [2 Ch 18:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:2), [28:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:8), [28:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:9), לבבל [Je 51:2](https://ref.ly/Jer51:2), [Ezr 2:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:1), [1 Ch 9:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:1), [2 Ch 36:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:7) (but earlier always ירושׁלם, שׁמרון, בבל or בבלה); and poet. Ju [5:11](https://ref.ly/Judg5:11), [Is 22:1](https://ref.ly/Isa22:1), [23:17](https://ref.ly/Isa23:17), [49:18](https://ref.ly/Isa49:18), [51:14](https://ref.ly/Isa51:14), (pregn.) ימות לשׁחת, [59:20](https://ref.ly/Isa59:20), [60:4](https://ref.ly/Isa60:4), [60:5](https://ref.ly/Isa60:5), [60:7](https://ref.ly/Isa60:7), [65:12](https://ref.ly/Isa65:12), [Je 31:17](https://ref.ly/Jer31:17), [48:15](https://ref.ly/Jer48:15), [50:27](https://ref.ly/Jer50:27), [Mi 1:12](https://ref.ly/Mic1:12), [Zc 9:12](https://ref.ly/Zech9:12), [Psalm 7:8](https://ref.ly/Ps7:8), [68:19](https://ref.ly/Ps68:19), [74:3](https://ref.ly/Ps74:3), Ct [4:16](https://ref.ly/Song4:16), [5:1](https://ref.ly/Song5:1), [6:2](https://ref.ly/Song6:2), [7:13](https://ref.ly/Song7:13), נשׁכימה לכרמים, [Jb 10:19](https://ref.ly/Job10:19), [20:6](https://ref.ly/Job20:6); הוציא לְ [Psalm 18:20](https://ref.ly/Ps18:20), [66:12](https://ref.ly/Ps66:12), לאור [Mi 7:9](https://ref.ly/Mic7:9), [Jb 12:22](https://ref.ly/Job12:22). יוּבַל ל [Ho 10:6](https://ref.ly/Hos10:6) \+ : without a vb., [Is 23:5](https://ref.ly/Isa23:5), [Ho 7:12](https://ref.ly/Hos7:12). Also לָאָרֶץ, with many vbs., both in sense *down to* the earth, [Is 14:12](https://ref.ly/Isa14:12), [21:9](https://ref.ly/Isa21:9), [28:2](https://ref.ly/Isa28:2), [Am 3:14](https://ref.ly/Amos3:14), [5:7](https://ref.ly/Amos5:7), Ez [26:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:11), [Psalm 7:6](https://ref.ly/Ps7:6), \+, c. חִלֵּל (pregn.) [74:7](https://ref.ly/Ps74:7), [89:40](https://ref.ly/Ps89:40), and idiom. c. ישׁב to sit *on* the earth, [Is 3:26](https://ref.ly/Isa3:26), [47:1](https://ref.ly/Isa47:1), [Jb 2:13](https://ref.ly/Job2:13) \+, without vb. [Is 26:9](https://ref.ly/Isa26:9): so לֶעָפָר [Jb 7:21](https://ref.ly/Job7:21), [Psalm 7:6](https://ref.ly/Ps7:6), הוריד לשׁחת Ez [28:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:8). (*b*) with *persons,* not very common, [Dt 32:35](https://ref.ly/Deut32:35), [Is 31:6](https://ref.ly/Isa31:6), [57:9](https://ref.ly/Isa57:9), [Je 3:22](https://ref.ly/Jer3:22), אָתָנוּ לָךְ, [Psalm 45:15](https://ref.ly/Ps45:15), [119:79](https://ref.ly/Ps119:79), [Jb 18:14](https://ref.ly/Job18:14), [1 Ch 12:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:16), [Ne 6:19](https://ref.ly/Neh6:19), לְעַמִּי [Nu 24:14](https://ref.ly/Num24:14), [Ru 1:10](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:10), בא לְ, esp. with pron. לְ, לָהּ etc. (friendly) [2 S 12:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:4), [Zc 9:9](https://ref.ly/Zech9:9), [Am 6:1](https://ref.ly/Amos6:1), (hostile) [2 S 5:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:23), [Je 46:22](https://ref.ly/Jer46:22), [49:9](https://ref.ly/Jer49:9), [50:26](https://ref.ly/Jer50:26), [51:48](https://ref.ly/Jer51:48); [51:53](https://ref.ly/Jer51:53); with a*thing* as subj. [Dt 33:16](https://ref.ly/Deut33:16), (לראשׁ), [2 S 24:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:13), [Is 47:9](https://ref.ly/Isa47:9), [Jb 3:25](https://ref.ly/Job3:25), (cf. [Is 66:4](https://ref.ly/Isa66:4)), [Je 4:12](https://ref.ly/Jer4:12), 22:33, בְּבאֹ לָךְ חֲבָלִים (so [Ho 13:13](https://ref.ly/Hos13:13), [Is 66:7](https://ref.ly/Isa66:7)). And with vbs. of placing (where עַל would be more usu.) [Psalm 21:4](https://ref.ly/Ps21:4) תָּשִׁית לְראֹשׁוֺ עֲטֶרֶת פָּז, [22:16](https://ref.ly/Ps22:16), [66:12](https://ref.ly/Ps66:12), with לְכִסֵּא [Psalm 9:5](https://ref.ly/Ps9:5), [132:11](https://ref.ly/Ps132:11), [132:12](https://ref.ly/Ps132:12), [Jb 36:7](https://ref.ly/Job36:7): cf. נָפַל לְאַפָּיוּ, הִשְׁתַּחֲוָה, [Gn 48:12](https://ref.ly/Gen48:12) \+ (also ´עַל א). **h.** expressing *direction towards* (without contact), לאחור *backwards* [Je 7:24](https://ref.ly/Jer7:24), לַחוּץ *outwards* [Psalm 41:7](https://ref.ly/Ps41:7), למעלה *upwards*, למטה *downwards;* to scatter לכל רוח [Je 49:32](https://ref.ly/Jer49:32) cf. v:[36](https://ref.ly/Jer49:36), Ez [5:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:10) \+, (שׁמים לארבע רוחות Ez [42:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:20), [Dn 8:8](https://ref.ly/Dan8:8), [11:4](https://ref.ly/Dan11:4), [1 Ch 9:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:24): of the points of the compass (without vb.) …לִפְאַת *towards* the quarter of (the N., S., etc.) [Ex 26:18](https://ref.ly/Exod26:18) \+ oft. P (so Ez [47:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:15)), לדרום למזרח etc. (late: earlier ממזרח, or מזרחה etc.) Ez [40:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:23), [41:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:11), [41:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:14), [42:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:4), [Ne 3:26](https://ref.ly/Neh3:26), [1 Ch 5:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:9), [6:63](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:63), [7:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:28), [12:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:15), [26:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:16)–[18](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:18), [2 Ch 31:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:14), למדבר [2 Ch 20:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:24); also (peculiarly) [1 S 14:40](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:40), [1 K 20:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:38), [2 K 11:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:11). **i.** expressing *addition* (rare); [Is 28:10](https://ref.ly/Isa28:10), [28:13](https://ref.ly/Isa28:13) צַו לָצַו קַו לָקַו, [56:8](https://ref.ly/Isa56:8) (resuming על), [Ec 7:27](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:27) אחת לאחת (adding) one *to* another, [Ezr 8:24](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:24), [Ne 11:17](https://ref.ly/Neh11:17) (על is more usual in this sense).

 **2\.** Expressing *locality, at, near*, idiom. in the phrases לִפְנֵי \= *before* (sts. after vbs. of motion, as [1 K 1:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:23), but very oft. otherwise), לְעֵינֵי *in the sight of*, לְיַד, לִימִין, לִשְמאֹל (only [Ec 10:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:2)), לפתח *at the entrance (of)*, [Gn 4:7](https://ref.ly/Gen4:7), [Nu 11:10](https://ref.ly/Num11:10) \+; in other, rarer connexions, [Nu 20:24](https://ref.ly/Num20:24) …לְמֵי (usu. על), Ju [5:16](https://ref.ly/Judg5:16) (‖ v:[15](https://ref.ly/Judg5:15) ב), לְחוֺף † [Gn 49:13](https://ref.ly/Gen49:13), [49:13](https://ref.ly/Gen49:13), Ju [5:17](https://ref.ly/Judg5:17), לְפִי † [Psalm 141:7](https://ref.ly/Ps141:7), [Pr 8:3](https://ref.ly/Prov8:3), [Ho 5:1](https://ref.ly/Hos5:1) לְמִחְפָּה, [2 Ch 35:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:15). לִפְנִימָה \= *within,* [1 K 6:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:30), Ez [40:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:16). **3\.** To denote the *object* of a vb.—**a.** with the Hif., mostly of intrans. vbs., properly (as it seems) a *dat. commodi,* as הֵנִיחַ לְ to *give rest to,* הִרְחִיב to *give width to,* הֵצַר לְ, הֵצִיק לְ, exceptionally also with other words, as הִרְגִּיוּ, הוֺכִיחַ, הִצְדִּיק to *give righteousness to*, [Is 53:11](https://ref.ly/Isa53:11), הֶחֱיָה [Gn 45:7](https://ref.ly/Gen45:7), הֵבִין *give understanding to* (late), הִצְלִיחַ (do.), הִרְבָּה [Ho 10:1](https://ref.ly/Hos10:1), הִשְגִּיא [Jb 12:23](https://ref.ly/Job12:23), הִפְתָּה [Gn 9:27](https://ref.ly/Gen9:27) *give breadth to.* **b.** with other vbs., sporadically early (if the text be sound), but mostly late, in conseq. of Aram. influence (in Aram. the accus. being constantly denoted by ל), as אהב [Lv 19:18](https://ref.ly/Lev19:18), [19:34](https://ref.ly/Lev19:34), [2 Ch 19:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:2), הרג [2 S 3:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:30), [Jb 5:2](https://ref.ly/Job5:2), בוּז (mostly), בזה [2 S 6:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:16), sts. also זכר *to remember,* עבד *to serve* (work or do service *for*), עזר ([2 S 8:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:5), and esp. late), דרשׁ (esp. Chr), הִלֵּל (only Chr Ezr), רפא (prob. the dat. comm.), שִחֵת [1 S 23:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:10), [Nu 32:15](https://ref.ly/Num32:15), נִדָּה [Am 6:3](https://ref.ly/Amos6:3), גִּדֵּל [Psalm. 34:4](https://ref.ly/Ps34:4), פִּתַּח [116:16](https://ref.ly/Ps116:16), כִּבֵּד [86:9](https://ref.ly/Ps86:9), [Dn 11:38](https://ref.ly/Dan11:38), חִזַּק [1 Ch 26:27](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:27), [29:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:12), בֵּרַח [29:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:20) [Ne 11:2](https://ref.ly/Neh11:2), חֵרֵף [2 Ch 32:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:17); see also [1 S 22:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:7), [2 K 8:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:6), [Je 16:6](https://ref.ly/Jer16:6), [40:2](https://ref.ly/Jer40:2), [Jon 4:6](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:6), [Psalm 69:6](https://ref.ly/Ps69:6), [73:18](https://ref.ly/Ps73:18), [135:11](https://ref.ly/Ps135:11), [136:19](https://ref.ly/Ps136:19), [136:20](https://ref.ly/Ps136:20), [Pr 17:26](https://ref.ly/Prov17:26), [Jb 12:23](https://ref.ly/Job12:23) b, [La 4:5](https://ref.ly/Lam4:5), [1 Ch 16:37](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:37), [18:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:6), (הושׁיע, altered fr. [2 S 8:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:6): so [Psalm 116:6](https://ref.ly/Ps116:6)), [25:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:1), [29:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:22), [29:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:22), [2 Ch 5:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:11), [6:42](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:42), [17:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:7), [24:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:5), [34:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:13), (usu. על), [Ezr 8:16](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:16); at the end of an enumeration, [1 Ch 28:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:1) b, [2 Ch 24:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:12) b, [26:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:14) b, [28:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:23); marking the defin. obj. in appos., [1 Ch 29:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:18), [2 Ch 2:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:12), [23:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:1), [Psalm 135:11](https://ref.ly/Ps135:11), [136:19](https://ref.ly/Ps136:19), [136:20](https://ref.ly/Ps136:20), ( \= earlier את, [Gn 26:34](https://ref.ly/Gen26:34), Ju [3:15](https://ref.ly/Judg3:15), [Is 7:6](https://ref.ly/Isa7:6), [8:2](https://ref.ly/Isa8:2)); after a sf. (in Syr. fashion), [1 Ch 5:26](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:26), ´וַיַּגְלֵם ל, [23:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:6), [2 Ch 25:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:5), [25:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:10), [28:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:15), cf. [Ne 9:32](https://ref.ly/Neh9:32); defining anom. the sf. of a *noun,* [Nu 29:18](https://ref.ly/Num29:18), [29:21](https://ref.ly/Num29:21), [29:24](https://ref.ly/Num29:24) etc. [1 Ch 7:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:5) הִתְיַחֲשָם לַכֹּל, [2 Ch 31:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:16), [31:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:18), [Ezr 9:1](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:1), [10:14](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:14). (But in sentences of the type אֵין דּוֺרֵשׁ לְנַפְשִׁי [Psalm 142:5](https://ref.ly/Ps142:5) b, [72:12](https://ref.ly/Ps72:12), [Is 51:18](https://ref.ly/Isa51:18), [Je 14:16](https://ref.ly/Jer14:16), [49:5](https://ref.ly/Jer49:5), [La 1:7](https://ref.ly/Lam1:7), [1:9](https://ref.ly/Lam1:9), [1:17](https://ref.ly/Lam1:17), [1:21](https://ref.ly/Lam1:21), the ל belongs prob. to אין: cf. the ‖ types אֵין לִי מַכִּיר [Psalm 142:5](https://ref.ly/Ps142:5) a, [Dt 28:31](https://ref.ly/Deut28:31), [Je 50:32](https://ref.ly/Jer50:32), [La 1:2](https://ref.ly/Lam1:2), דֹּרֵשׁ אֵין לָהּ [Je 30:17](https://ref.ly/Jer30:17), [La 4:4](https://ref.ly/Lam4:4).) Cf. Ges§ 117 n. **4\.** *Into* (εἰς), of a transition into a new state or condition, or into a new character or office:—**a.** [Gn 2:22](https://ref.ly/Gen2:22) וַיִּבֶן אֶת־הַצֵּלָע לְאִשָּׁה *into* a woman, [12:2](https://ref.ly/Gen12:2) וְאֶעֶשְ לְגוֺי גָּדוֺל *into* a great nation, and very oft. with this and similar verbs, as [Ex 26:7](https://ref.ly/Exod26:7), [Is 44:17](https://ref.ly/Isa44:17), [44:19](https://ref.ly/Isa44:19), שָם [Gn 46:3](https://ref.ly/Gen46:3), [Is 5:20](https://ref.ly/Isa5:20) make bitter *into* sweet etc., [28:17](https://ref.ly/Isa28:17), נָתַן [42:6](https://ref.ly/Isa42:6), also in such phrases as לְשַׁמָּהשָם to make *into* a desolation [Is 13:9](https://ref.ly/Isa13:9), [Je 4:7](https://ref.ly/Jer4:7) etc. [19:8](https://ref.ly/Jer19:8); הפך ל to change *into* [Ex 7:15](https://ref.ly/Exod7:15), [Dt 23:6](https://ref.ly/Deut23:6) \+, to cut or divide *into* [Gn 32:8](https://ref.ly/Gen32:8), Ju [19:29](https://ref.ly/Judg19:29), [Is 11:15](https://ref.ly/Isa11:15) \+, שָרַף לְ to burn *into* [Am 2:1](https://ref.ly/Amos2:1), [Dt 9:21](https://ref.ly/Deut9:21) דַּק לְעָפָר פעל [Psalm 7:14](https://ref.ly/Ps7:14) maketh *into* (or *to be*) flaming ones; היה ל *to become,* in many diff. connexions, as [Gn 2:7](https://ref.ly/Gen2:7) ויהי האדם לנפשׁ חיה *became a* living soul (see היה **II. 2 e,** p. 226 a); (לנגיד)משׁח למלך to anoint *so as to be* king, as king (Germ. ‘zum Konig’: cf. Old Engl. *to,* as Ju [17:13](https://ref.ly/Judg17:13) and ‘We have Abraham *to* our father’), [1 S 9:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:16), [15:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:1), etc., חִוָּה לְ to appoint *as* [13:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:14), [25:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:30); שׁת לְ [Psalm 45:17](https://ref.ly/Ps45:17); even more freely, as דִּבֶּר עָלַי לְמֶלֶח [1 K 14:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:2), cf. [2 S 3:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:17), [1 Ch 29:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:23); חשׁב ל to count *for* (or *as*) [Gn 38:15](https://ref.ly/Gen38:15) \+ oft.; [Ex 21:7](https://ref.ly/Exod21:7) when a man sells his daughter לְאָמָה *for, as,* a female slave, [Dt 6:8](https://ref.ly/Deut6:8) to bind לְאוֺת *for, as,* a sign, הִתְיַצֵּב לְשָטָן (יָצָא) *so as to be* an adversary [Nu 22:22](https://ref.ly/Num22:22), [22:32](https://ref.ly/Num22:32), לְאֹרֵב (הקים)קום [1 S 22:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:8), [22:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:13), עמד לְ [Is 11:10](https://ref.ly/Isa11:10), [Dn 11:1](https://ref.ly/Dan11:1), יצא לַחָפְשִׁי to go forth *into the state of* one free [Ex 21:2](https://ref.ly/Exod21:2) (cf. v:[26](https://ref.ly/Exod21:26); v:[27](https://ref.ly/Exod21:27) after שׁלח), [2 K 25:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:12), [Is 14:2](https://ref.ly/Isa14:2), [Je 34:11](https://ref.ly/Jer34:11), [Psalm 48:4](https://ref.ly/Ps48:4), נודע לְ hath made himself known *as,* [87:4](https://ref.ly/Ps87:4) הזכיר לְ to mention *as,* Ez [13:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:20); poet. [Jb 39:16](https://ref.ly/Job39:16) הִקְשִׁיחַ בָּנֶיהָ לְלאֹ־לָהּ treats her young ones hardly (turning them) *into* none of hers: without a vb. (poet., or late prose) [Mi 1:14](https://ref.ly/Mic1:14), [Na 1:7](https://ref.ly/Nah1:7), Hb [1:11](https://ref.ly/Hab1:11) זוֺ כֹחוֺ לֵאלֽהוֺ, [Zc 4:7](https://ref.ly/Zech4:7), [La 4:3](https://ref.ly/Lam4:3), [Jb 13:12](https://ref.ly/Job13:12), [Hg 1:9](https://ref.ly/Hag1:9), [1 Ch 21:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:12), [26:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:29), [28:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:18) b, [2 Ch 23:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:4). **b.** this usage is also combined idiomatically, with great freq., with a 2nd לְ, of reference (**5 a** *d*), giving rise to such phrases as [Gn 1:29](https://ref.ly/Gen1:29) לָכֶם יִהְיֶה לְאָכְלָה to you it shall be *for food* (see היה **II. 2 f,** p. 226 b), [45:8](https://ref.ly/Gen45:8) וַיְשִימֵנִי לְאָב לְפַרְעֹה, [47:26](https://ref.ly/Gen47:26), [Dt 28:9](https://ref.ly/Deut28:9), [28:25](https://ref.ly/Deut28:25), Ju [1:33](https://ref.ly/Judg1:33), [1 S 2:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:28), [Is 21:4](https://ref.ly/Isa21:4), [28:18](https://ref.ly/Isa28:18) b, וִהְיִיתֶם לוֺ לְמִרְמָס, [49:5](https://ref.ly/Isa49:5), יֹצְרִי מִבֶּטֶן לְעֶבֶד לוֺ, [63:8](https://ref.ly/Isa63:8), [63:10](https://ref.ly/Isa63:10), וַיֵּהָפֵח לָהֶם לְאוֺיֵב ([Jb 30:21](https://ref.ly/Job30:21)), [Je 15:4](https://ref.ly/Jer15:4), [15:20](https://ref.ly/Jer15:20), [20:4](https://ref.ly/Jer20:4), [21:9](https://ref.ly/Jer21:9), Hb [2:7](https://ref.ly/Hab2:7), [Psalm 33:12](https://ref.ly/Ps33:12), [94:22](https://ref.ly/Ps94:22), [132:13](https://ref.ly/Ps132:13), [139:22](https://ref.ly/Ps139:22), [Jb 13:24](https://ref.ly/Job13:24), ותחשׁבני לאויב לך, [16:12](https://ref.ly/Job16:12) etc. **5\.** *With reference to,* viz. **a.** defining those in reference to whom a predicate is affirmed, hence oft. \= *belonging to, of:* (*a*) [Dt 23:3](https://ref.ly/Deut23:3) דור עשירי לא יבא לו, v:[4](https://ref.ly/Deut23:4), v:[9](https://ref.ly/Deut23:9), [La 1:10](https://ref.ly/Lam1:10); הכרית לְ [1 S 2:33](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:33), [1 K 14:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:10) \+; השׁבית לְ [Je 48:35](https://ref.ly/Jer48:35); לאֹ יִכָּרֵת אִישׁ לְ [1 K 2:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:4), [8:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:25) \+; [1 S 25:34](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:34) אִם נוֺתַר לְ, [Gn 17:10](https://ref.ly/Gen17:10) המול לכם כל בשר, [34:15](https://ref.ly/Gen34:15), [34:22](https://ref.ly/Gen34:22), [Ex 12:48](https://ref.ly/Exod12:48), [1 S 11:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:2); [1 K 14:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:13); ישׁב על כסא לְ [2 K 10:30](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:30), [15:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:12), [Je 13:13](https://ref.ly/Jer13:13), [22:4](https://ref.ly/Jer22:4), [Psalm 132:12](https://ref.ly/Ps132:12) cf. v:[11](https://ref.ly/Ps132:11); ראה בנים לְ [Gn 50:23](https://ref.ly/Gen50:23), [Psalm 128:6](https://ref.ly/Ps128:6); אבד לְ to perish *belonging to* [Dt 22:14](https://ref.ly/Deut22:14), [1 S 13:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:22); [Gn 23:16](https://ref.ly/Gen23:16) money עֹבֵר לַסֹּחֵר current *to* ( \= with) the merchant, [Nu 9:10](https://ref.ly/Num9:10), [Am 9:1](https://ref.ly/Amos9:1), [Is 33:14](https://ref.ly/Isa33:14), [Jb 12:6](https://ref.ly/Job12:6): note further the pron. in [Ex 10:5](https://ref.ly/Exod10:5) הַצֹּמֵחַ לכם מן השדה, [12:2](https://ref.ly/Exod12:2), [12:5](https://ref.ly/Exod12:5), [26:33](https://ref.ly/Exod26:33), [Lv 11:29](https://ref.ly/Lev11:29), וְזֶה לָכֶם הַטָּמֵא (cf. v:[4](https://ref.ly/Lev11:4)–[8](https://ref.ly/Lev11:8)), [19:23](https://ref.ly/Lev19:23), [25:30](https://ref.ly/Lev25:30), [26:5](https://ref.ly/Lev26:5), [26:26](https://ref.ly/Lev26:26), בְּשִׁבְרִי לָכֶם מַטֵּה לֶחֶם (Ez [14:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:13)), [Nu 28:19](https://ref.ly/Num28:19), [32:21](https://ref.ly/Num32:21), [34:4](https://ref.ly/Num34:4), [Dt 28:66](https://ref.ly/Deut28:66), [Jos 2:6](https://ref.ly/Josh2:6) הָעֲרֻכוֺת לָהּ עַל הַגָּג, Ju [16:9](https://ref.ly/Judg16:9), [19:14](https://ref.ly/Judg19:14), (cf. זָרַח לְ [Gn 32:32](https://ref.ly/Gen32:32) \+), [1 S 5:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:9), [2 S 15:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:30), [2 K 4:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:27) b נַפְשָׁהּ מָרָה־לָהּ (cf. [Is 15:4](https://ref.ly/Isa15:4), [Je 4:19](https://ref.ly/Jer4:19)), [Is 23:7](https://ref.ly/Isa23:7), [Je 2:21](https://ref.ly/Jer2:21), [Mi 2:4](https://ref.ly/Mic2:4) איך ימישׁ לי, Ez [16:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:14), [29:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:7), [Psalm 110:3](https://ref.ly/Ps110:3); also [40:7](https://ref.ly/Ps40:7) ears hast thou digged *to* (or *for*) me, [51:12](https://ref.ly/Ps51:12) (cf. [1 S 10:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:9)), [Is 50:4](https://ref.ly/Isa50:4), [50:5](https://ref.ly/Isa50:5) יעיר לי אזן. (*b*) in such phrases as [Nu 1:4](https://ref.ly/Num1:4) אִישׁ אִישׁ לְמַטֶּה a man *for* (or *of*) a tribe, [7:11](https://ref.ly/Num7:11), [31:4](https://ref.ly/Num31:4), [Dt 1:23](https://ref.ly/Deut1:23), [Jos 3:12](https://ref.ly/Josh3:12), [18:4](https://ref.ly/Josh18:4), Ju [20:10](https://ref.ly/Judg20:10) ten men לַמֵּאָה *of* 100, 100 *of* 1000 etc.; רִאשׁוֺן לְ \= first *of* [Ex 12:2](https://ref.ly/Exod12:2), [2 S 19:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:21). (*c*) spec. of relationship, to define a man's family or tribe, esp. in genealogies, [Nu 1:6](https://ref.ly/Num1:6) לראובן אליצור, v:[7](https://ref.ly/Num1:7), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Num1:8) etc., v:[22](https://ref.ly/Num1:22), v:[24](https://ref.ly/Num1:24) etc., [3:21](https://ref.ly/Num3:21), [3:27](https://ref.ly/Num3:27), [1 Ch 24:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:20), [24:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:21) etc., [26:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:23), [26:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:25) etc. \+ oft.; in the opp. order [Ex 31:2](https://ref.ly/Exod31:2), [Lv 24:11](https://ref.ly/Lev24:11), [Nu 17:23](https://ref.ly/Num17:23) אהרן לבית לוי [1 K 15:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:27) etc., cf. [2 S 3:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:2), [3:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:3), [3:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:5), also [9:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:3) a, [Gn 20:18](https://ref.ly/Gen20:18), [46:26](https://ref.ly/Gen46:26), [46:27](https://ref.ly/Gen46:27); similarly הנשׁארים לְ [2 K 10:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:11), [10:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:17), הַמֵּת לְ [1 K 14:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:11), [16:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:4) \+. (*d*) denoting *relation* (to be *to* or *towards* one in a particular regard or capacity) [Ex 19:5](https://ref.ly/Exod19:5) והייתם לי סְגֻלָּה ye shall be *to me* a special possession, [22:30](https://ref.ly/Exod22:30) אנשׁי קדשׁ תהיון לי, [1 S 18:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:18), [2 S 19:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:29), [1 K 5:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:15), [2 K 19:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:15), [Je 12:9](https://ref.ly/Jer12:9), [15:8](https://ref.ly/Jer15:8), [22:6](https://ref.ly/Jer22:6) גלעד אתה לי, [51:20](https://ref.ly/Jer51:20), [Is 54:9](https://ref.ly/Isa54:9), Ez [24:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:19), [Psalm. 12:5](https://ref.ly/Ps12:5) מִי אָדוֺן לָנוּ, [35:14](https://ref.ly/Ps35:14) כְּרֵעַ כְּאָח לִי, [99:8](https://ref.ly/Ps99:8), [Jb 24:17](https://ref.ly/Job24:17), [30:29](https://ref.ly/Job30:29), [Ne 6:18](https://ref.ly/Neh6:18); with a ptcp. [Nu 10:25](https://ref.ly/Num10:25), [25:18](https://ref.ly/Num25:18), [35:23](https://ref.ly/Num35:23), [Dt 4:22](https://ref.ly/Deut4:22), [19:4](https://ref.ly/Deut19:4), [19:6](https://ref.ly/Deut19:6), [Is 11:9](https://ref.ly/Isa11:9), [14:2](https://ref.ly/Isa14:2) (Dr§ 135:7 *Obs*.); מלך לְ [Nu 22:4](https://ref.ly/Num22:4); רַב לָכֶם it is (too) much *to* you, מְעַט לְ (too) little *to*…; in the phr. (מָה)מִי אֵלֶּה לְ who (what) are these *to* thee ?\= *what meanest thou* by these things? [Gn 33:5](https://ref.ly/Gen33:5), [33:8](https://ref.ly/Gen33:8), [2 S 16:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:2), Ez [37:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:18), cf. [Ex 12:26](https://ref.ly/Exod12:26), [Jos 4:6](https://ref.ly/Josh4:6), Ez [12:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:22); חלילה לי away be it *to* (or *for*) me ! למה לי to what purpose *to* me is…? [Gn 27:46](https://ref.ly/Gen27:46), [Is 1:11](https://ref.ly/Isa1:11), [Je 6:20](https://ref.ly/Jer6:20), [Jb 30:2](https://ref.ly/Job30:2): oft. also in such phrases as מָגֵן לְ a shield *to* [Gn 15:1](https://ref.ly/Gen15:1), [Psalm. 18:31](https://ref.ly/Ps18:31), a strength *to* [Psalm. 28:8](https://ref.ly/Ps28:8), an abomination *to* [Gn 43:32](https://ref.ly/Gen43:32), [Is 1:13](https://ref.ly/Isa1:13) \+, a grief *to* [Pr 10:1](https://ref.ly/Prov10:1), [17:21](https://ref.ly/Prov17:21); cf. [Je 15:10](https://ref.ly/Jer15:10), [Mal 2:9](https://ref.ly/Mal2:9), [Psalm. 89:28](https://ref.ly/Ps89:28) etc.: note also [Jon 3:3](https://ref.ly/Jonah3:3) לאלהים עיר גדולה a city great *to* God (i.e. in his estimation: cf. [Acts 7:20](https://ref.ly/Acts7:20) ἀστεῖος τῷ θεῷ, and לפני [Gn 10:9](https://ref.ly/Gen10:9)), [Est 10:3](https://ref.ly/Esth10:3). And with כְּ Ju [17:11](https://ref.ly/Judg17:11), [2 S 12:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:3), וַתְּהִי לוֺ כְּבַת, [Ex 22:24](https://ref.ly/Exod22:24) (cf. **4 b**), [Am 9:7](https://ref.ly/Amos9:7) כבני כֻשִׁיים אַתֶּם לי [Ho 11:4](https://ref.ly/Hos11:4), [Is 29:2](https://ref.ly/Isa29:2), [Jb 33:6](https://ref.ly/Job33:6) הֵן־אֲנִי לָאֵל כְּפִיְ lo, I am *to* God as thou art, etc. **b.** denoting possession, *belonging to;*—(*a*) as predicate, in הָיָה לְ (cf. Lat. *est mihi*), יֵשׁ לְ,אֵין לְ constantly (see these words); also alone, as [Gn 31:16](https://ref.ly/Gen31:16), [31:43](https://ref.ly/Gen31:43) לִי הוּא it is *mine,* [48:5](https://ref.ly/Gen48:5) לי הם, [Ex 19:5](https://ref.ly/Exod19:5) b כי לי כל הארץ, [1 K 20:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:3); [20:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:4), [Is 43:1](https://ref.ly/Isa43:1) לי אתה, [44:5](https://ref.ly/Isa44:5) אני ´לי, Ez [29:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:3), [Psalm. 47:10](https://ref.ly/Ps47:10), [50:10](https://ref.ly/Ps50:10), [50:12](https://ref.ly/Ps50:12), [Jb 12:13](https://ref.ly/Job12:13), [12:16](https://ref.ly/Job12:16), Ct [2:16](https://ref.ly/Song2:16), [6:3](https://ref.ly/Song6:3); [1 S 1:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:2) ולו שׁתי נשׁיס֫ and *he had* two wives, [25:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:7), [25:36](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:36), Ju [3:16](https://ref.ly/Judg3:16), [17:5](https://ref.ly/Judg17:5), [Jb 22:8](https://ref.ly/Job22:8), [2 S 17:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:18), [Ho 6:10](https://ref.ly/Hos6:10), \+ oft.; with לאֹ, [1 K 22:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:17), [Is 53:2](https://ref.ly/Isa53:2) לא הדר לו, [Je 5:10](https://ref.ly/Jer5:10) \+ ; with a neut. adj. (rare) [Is 63:2](https://ref.ly/Isa63:2), [Je 30:10](https://ref.ly/Jer30:10) אָנוּשׁ לְשִׁבְרֵח; note also such phrases as [2 K 10:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:19) זבח גדול לי לבעל, [Is 2:12](https://ref.ly/Isa2:12) ´כִּי יוֺם לי ´על וג for ´י *hath* a day against etc., [22:5](https://ref.ly/Isa22:5), [28:2](https://ref.ly/Isa28:2) ´הִנֵּה חָזָק וְאַמִּץ לי ´י *hath* a strong and mighty one (sc. at his disposal), [34:2](https://ref.ly/Isa34:2) ´על וג´קצף לי, v:[6](https://ref.ly/Isa34:6) b. [34:8](https://ref.ly/Isa34:8); [Ho 4:1](https://ref.ly/Hos4:1)…עם ´כִּי רִיב לי, [12:3](https://ref.ly/Hos12:3), [Mi 6:2](https://ref.ly/Mic6:2): מַה־לִּי וָלָח what is there *to* me and *to* thee (?) (i.e. what have we to do with each other (?)), v. שׁלוס לך מָה peace be *to thee !* Of that which *pertains to* one as a right, [Lv 25:31](https://ref.ly/Lev25:31), [25:48](https://ref.ly/Lev25:48), [Dt 1:17](https://ref.ly/Deut1:17) כי המשׁפט לאלהיס הוא, [21:17](https://ref.ly/Deut21:17), [1 S 17:47](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:47), [Je 10:23](https://ref.ly/Jer10:23), [32:7](https://ref.ly/Jer32:7), [32:8](https://ref.ly/Jer32:8), Ez [21:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:32), [Psalm 3:9](https://ref.ly/Ps3:9) הישׁועה ´לי, [Jon 2:10](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:10); with an inf. [1 S 23:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:20) וְלָנוּ הַסְגִּירוֺ and it shall be *for us* (or *our place*) to deliver him, [Mi 3:1](https://ref.ly/Mic3:1) הלא לכם לדעת, [Ezr 4:3](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:3), [2 Ch 13:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:5), [20:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:17), [26:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:18), cf. [Psalm 50:16](https://ref.ly/Ps50:16) מַהלּלְּ לְ. (*b*) here also belongs the so\-called *Lamed auctoris,* [Is 38:9](https://ref.ly/Isa38:9) מִכְתָּב לְחִזְקִיָּהוּ a writing *belonging to, of,* or *by* H., Hb [3:1](https://ref.ly/Hab3:1), [Psalm 3:1](https://ref.ly/Ps3:1) and oft. מזמור לדוד a Psalm *of* or *by* D. (but possibly denoting orig., at least in some cases, a Psalm *belonging to* a collection known as David's: so certainly in לבני קרח [Psalm 42:1](https://ref.ly/Ps42:1) al., and prob. also in לאסף [Psalm 50:1](https://ref.ly/Ps50:1) al.); so לדוד מזמור [24:1](https://ref.ly/Ps24:1) \+, לדוד alone [10:1](https://ref.ly/Ps10:1) m [14:1](https://ref.ly/Ps14:1) \+. Comp. on Ph. coins לצדנם *of* the Sidonians, i.e. belonging to them, לצור (\= Gk. Σιδονιων, Τύρου). Heb. idiom also uses the ל of possession where we should write the simple name, as Ez [38:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:16) (written on a stick) ליהּודה, v:[17](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:17) ליוסף, in English ‘Judah,’ ‘Joseph,’ [Is 8:1](https://ref.ly/Isa8:1) למהר־שׁלל־חשׁ־בז ‘Maher\-shalal\-hash\-baz.’ **c.** as periph. for the *st. c.:*—(*a*) אשׁר לְ, as [Ex 29:29](https://ref.ly/Exod29:29)m [39:1](https://ref.ly/Exod39:1), [39:39](https://ref.ly/Exod39:39), [Lv 7:20](https://ref.ly/Lev7:20), [7:21](https://ref.ly/Lev7:21), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Lev16:6), [16:15](https://ref.ly/Lev16:15) (see further exx. sub אשׁר **7,** p. 82 f.); so שֶׁלִּי † Ct [1:6](https://ref.ly/Song1:6), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Song8:12), שֶׁלָּנוּ † [2 K 6:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:11). (*b*) without אשׁר—(*a*) where it is desired to keep the first noun indeterm., [1 S 16:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:18) ראיתי בן לישׁי a son *to* or *of* Jesse, [22:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:20), [Gn 41:12](https://ref.ly/Gen41:12), [Nu 1:4](https://ref.ly/Num1:4), [7:24](https://ref.ly/Num7:24), [1 K 2:39](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:39) שׁני עבדים לשׁמעי, [18:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:22), [2 K 3:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:11), [Ru 2:1](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:1) etc.; (β) where the genit. is a *compound* term, to avoid a series of nouns in the *st. c.,* [Nu 1:4](https://ref.ly/Num1:4) ראש לבית אבותיו, [7:24](https://ref.ly/Num7:24), [7:30](https://ref.ly/Num7:30), [7:36](https://ref.ly/Num7:36) etc., [1:21](https://ref.ly/Num1:21) פקֻדיהם למטה ראובן v:[23](https://ref.ly/Num1:23); v:[25](https://ref.ly/Num1:25) etc., [2:9](https://ref.ly/Num2:9); [2:16](https://ref.ly/Num2:16) etc., [Jos 21:38](https://ref.ly/Josh21:38); [1 Ch 9:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:23) ´הַשְּׁעָרִים לְבֵית י [27:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:3) הראשׁ לְ, [2 Ch 19:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:11), [Ne 10:39](https://ref.ly/Neh10:39) etc., occas. also besides, as [1 S 14:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:16) הצופים לשׁאול, [Ex 31:7](https://ref.ly/Exod31:7) (usu. ארון העדות); (γ) where the regens is a pr. name, or a compound term, which does not readily admit of being placed in the *st. c.,* as (יהודה) דברי הימים למלכי ישראל [1 K 14:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:19), [14:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:29) \+ oft., [1 K 5:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:30) שרי הנצבים לְ, [2 K 11:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:4) שרי המאות לְ;ראשׁי האבות לְ [Nu 36:1](https://ref.ly/Num36:1), [Jos 19:51](https://ref.ly/Josh19:51), [1 Ch 8:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:13) \+ oft. Ch Ne Ezr; in dates, as באחד לחדשׁ [Gn 8:5](https://ref.ly/Gen8:5), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Gen8:14), [Ex 12:3](https://ref.ly/Exod12:3), [12:6](https://ref.ly/Exod12:6), [Gn 7:11](https://ref.ly/Gen7:11) לחיי נח …בשׁנת, [16:3](https://ref.ly/Gen16:3), [Ex 19:1](https://ref.ly/Exod19:1)…בחדשׁ השׁלישׁי לצאת בשׁנת שׁתים לאסא [1 K 15:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:25), [15:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:28), [16:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:8) (all \+ oft.); other cases, [Ex 20:5](https://ref.ly/Exod20:5), [20:6](https://ref.ly/Exod20:6), [Lv 13:48](https://ref.ly/Lev13:48), [Nu 16:22](https://ref.ly/Num16:22), ( \= [27:16](https://ref.ly/Num27:16)) [18:15](https://ref.ly/Num18:15), Ju [20:10](https://ref.ly/Judg20:10), [2 K 5:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:9), Ez [45:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:19), [Ru 2:3](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:3), [1 Ch 4:43](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:43), [9:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:19), [9:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:21), [26:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:19), [2 Ch 22:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:10), [23:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:4); (δ) with a neg., [Gn 15:13](https://ref.ly/Gen15:13) כארץ לא לכם, [Je 5:19](https://ref.ly/Jer5:19), [Pr 26:17](https://ref.ly/Prov26:17), Hb [1:6](https://ref.ly/Hab1:6), poet. even alone, [2:6](https://ref.ly/Hab2:6) who increaseth לאֹ לוֺ (that which is) not *his,* [Jb 18:15](https://ref.ly/Job18:15) בְּלִי לוֺ, [39:16](https://ref.ly/Job39:16) לְלאֹלָהּ as (**4**) those which are not *hers;* (ε) poet., [Is 16:2](https://ref.ly/Isa16:2), [26:7](https://ref.ly/Isa26:7) ארח לצדיק [Je 47:3](https://ref.ly/Jer47:3), [Ho 9:6](https://ref.ly/Hos9:6), [Psalm 37:16](https://ref.ly/Ps37:16), [49:14](https://ref.ly/Ps49:14), [55:19](https://ref.ly/Ps55:19), (Hi De Ch), [58:5](https://ref.ly/Ps58:5), [73:6](https://ref.ly/Ps73:6), [105:36](https://ref.ly/Ps105:36), [116:15](https://ref.ly/Ps116:15) המותה לחסידיו, [123:4](https://ref.ly/Ps123:4), [Jon 2:3](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:3), [Ec 5:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:11); cf. also מלך לְ [Jos 12:18](https://ref.ly/Josh12:18) (but v. LXX Di), [2 K 19:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:13) (cf. Aram. [Ezr 5:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra5:11)): v. further Ew§ 292, Ges§ 129, Giesebr:§ 19\. **c.** attached to advbs., esp. those compounded with מִן, it forms preps., as מִקֶּדֶם לְ [Gn 3:24](https://ref.ly/Gen3:24) lit. off the front *with reference to* (or *of*) \= *in front of:* so מִבֵּית לְ \= *within,* מִחוּץ לְ \= *without,* מִמַּעַל לְ, מֵעַל לְ, מֵעֵבֶר לְ, מֵהָֽלְאָה לְ, מִצְּפוֺן לְ, סָבִֹיב לְ (all oft.); more rarely, מֵאַחֲרֵי לְ, מִ֫בַּעַד לְ, מִבֵּינוֺת לְ, אֶל בֵּינוֺת לְ, מִזֶּה וּמִזֶּה לְ [Ex 38:15](https://ref.ly/Exod38:15), הֵנָּה לְ, [Dn 12:5](https://ref.ly/Dan12:5); [12:5](https://ref.ly/Dan12:5), מִזְרָח לְ, מִימִין לְ, מִנֶּגֶד לְ, מִסָּבִיב לְ, תַּחַת לְ, poet. נֶגְדָּה־נָּא לְ, † [Psalm. 116:14](https://ref.ly/Ps116:14), [116:18](https://ref.ly/Ps116:18). See בית, חוץ, etc.; and cf. Ju [7:1](https://ref.ly/Judg7:1), [7:8](https://ref.ly/Judg7:8). **d.** construed with *passive* verbs, the ל of reference notifies the *agent,* as בָּרוּח לְ blessed *by,* [Gn 14:17](https://ref.ly/Gen14:17) \+ oft.; otherwise not very common, [Gn 14:17](https://ref.ly/Gen14:17) \+ oft.; otherwise not very common, [Gn 31:15](https://ref.ly/Gen31:15) נֶחְשַׁב לְ to be reckoned *by* (so [Is 40:17](https://ref.ly/Isa40:17)), [Ex 12:16](https://ref.ly/Exod12:16) אַח אֲשֶׁר יֵאָכֵל לְכָל־נֶפֶשׁ חוּא לְבַדּוֺ, יֵעָשֶה לָכם, [1 S 2:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:3), [25:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:7), [2 S 19:43](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:43), [Je 8:3](https://ref.ly/Jer8:3) נִבְחַר לְ ([Pr 21:3](https://ref.ly/Prov21:3)), [29:22](https://ref.ly/Prov29:22), [Psalm 73:10](https://ref.ly/Ps73:10), [111:2](https://ref.ly/Ps111:2), [Pr 13:13](https://ref.ly/Prov13:13) יֵחָבֶל־לוֺ is pledged *by it,* [14:20](https://ref.ly/Prov14:20), [Ne 6:1](https://ref.ly/Neh6:1), [6:7](https://ref.ly/Neh6:7) נִשְׁמַע לְ, [13:26](https://ref.ly/Neh13:26) אָהוּב לְ, [Est 4:3](https://ref.ly/Esth4:3), [5:12](https://ref.ly/Esth5:12), [Ec 5:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:12) שָׁמוּר לְ. So with נִרְאָה [Ex 13:7](https://ref.ly/Exod13:7) ( \= [Dt 16:4](https://ref.ly/Deut16:4)), נוֺדַע [1 S 6:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:3), Ez [36:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:32), [Ne 4:9](https://ref.ly/Neh4:9) (but usu. with these words לְ is rather the dat. comm.. *be known, appear, to),* נעתר [Gn 25:21](https://ref.ly/Gen25:21) \+, נדרשׁ and נמצא [Is 65:1](https://ref.ly/Isa65:1) \+ to let oneself be *entreated, sought, found, by,* נוֺסַר † [Lv 26:23](https://ref.ly/Lev26:23), נענה † Ez [14:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:4), [14:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:7) (?). (Comp. in Syr. Nö§ 247, esp. with pass. ptcp.:§ 279 (so Talm., Luz:§ 90\), which in Mand. and New Syr. even unites with the ל to form a new tense, v. NöM § 263; NS § 104\.) Analogously [Gn 38:15](https://ref.ly/Gen38:15) וַתַּהַר לוֺ and was pregnant *by,* v:[18](https://ref.ly/Gen38:18) הָרָה לְ (adj.) pregnant *by* (lit. *to*). **e.** *regarding, in respect of,* viz. (*a*) with verbs of *speaking, commanding, hearing,* etc.; *concerning, about* (syn. עַל, which is more usu.); so with מר:אָ [Gn 20:13](https://ref.ly/Gen20:13), [Dt 33:12](https://ref.ly/Deut33:12), [33:13](https://ref.ly/Deut33:13) \+ Ju [9:54](https://ref.ly/Judg9:54), [Is 41:7](https://ref.ly/Isa41:7), [Psalm 3:3](https://ref.ly/Ps3:3), [41:6](https://ref.ly/Ps41:6) \+, דִּבֶּר Ez [44:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:5), סִפֵּר [Psalm 22:31](https://ref.ly/Ps22:31), דרשׁ [Dt 12:30](https://ref.ly/Deut12:30), [2 S 11:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:3), חָלַם [Gn 42:9](https://ref.ly/Gen42:9), הִטִּיף [Mi 2:6](https://ref.ly/Mic2:6), חִוָּה [Nu 8:20](https://ref.ly/Num8:20), [Psalm 91:11](https://ref.ly/Ps91:11) \+, שָׁמַע [Gn 17:20](https://ref.ly/Gen17:20), and oft. in the adjunct…לאשׁר …לכל אשׁר [Gn 27:8](https://ref.ly/Gen27:8), [Jos 1:18](https://ref.ly/Josh1:18), [22:2](https://ref.ly/Josh22:2) \+; שׁאל [Gn 26:7](https://ref.ly/Gen26:7) \+, esp. in phr. לְשָׁלוֺם ´שׁאל לפ to ask *about* any one *with ref. to* (his) welfare; in the phr. לַדָּבָר הַזֶּה *in regard to* this thing (idiom.), [Gn 19:21](https://ref.ly/Gen19:21), [1 S 30:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:24) \+, Ju [21:5](https://ref.ly/Judg21:5), [21:7](https://ref.ly/Judg21:7) לנשׁיס, [1 K 20:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:7); without a vb., [Lv 7:37](https://ref.ly/Lev7:37), [14:54](https://ref.ly/Lev14:54), [Dt 33:7](https://ref.ly/Deut33:7), and in titles [Je 23:9](https://ref.ly/Jer23:9), [46:2](https://ref.ly/Jer46:2), [48:1](https://ref.ly/Jer48:1), [49:1](https://ref.ly/Jer49:1), [49:7](https://ref.ly/Jer49:7), [49:23](https://ref.ly/Jer49:23), [49:28](https://ref.ly/Jer49:28). (*b*) limiting the application of a term, esp. with כְּ to denote the *tertium comparationis,* as [Gn 41:19](https://ref.ly/Gen41:19) לָרֹעַ …לאֹ רָאִיתִי כָהֵנָּה *as regards, in respect of* (in our idiom, simply *in* or *for*) badness, [Ex 24:10](https://ref.ly/Exod24:10) כְּעֶצֶם הַשָּׁמַיִם לָטֹהַר *in brightness,* [Dt 34:11](https://ref.ly/Deut34:11), [34:12](https://ref.ly/Deut34:12), Ez [3:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:3) (rd. לְמֹתֶק) [Pr 25:3](https://ref.ly/Prov25:3), [1 Ch 24:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:4); with an inf., [Gn 3:22](https://ref.ly/Gen3:22) היה כאחד ממנו לדעת *in respect of* knowing etc., [34:15](https://ref.ly/Gen34:15), [Is 21:1](https://ref.ly/Isa21:1) כַּסּוּפוֺת לַחֲלוֺף as whirlwinds *in respect of* sweeping through, [Jos 10:14](https://ref.ly/Josh10:14), [2 S 14:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:17), [14:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:25), Ez [38:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:9), [38:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:16), [Pr 26:2](https://ref.ly/Prov26:2) כצפור לָנוּד כדרור לָעוּף, [1 Ch 12:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:9) כחבאיס לְמַהֵר; with לָרֹב *in* multitude, [Dt 1:10](https://ref.ly/Deut1:10) ככוכבי השׁמיס לרב Ju [7:12](https://ref.ly/Judg7:12), [7:12](https://ref.ly/Judg7:12) \+ oft.; less freq. in comparisons with מִן, [1 K 10:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:23) לְעשֶׁר וּלְחָכְמָה …מִכֹּל …וַיִּגְדַּל, Ct [1:2](https://ref.ly/Song1:2), [Jb 30:1](https://ref.ly/Job30:1) חעיריס ממני לימיס (cf. the accus. [Dt 24:5](https://ref.ly/Deut24:5) (peculiar); [Ec 9:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:4) בִּי לְכֶלֶב חַי הוא ´טוב וג; cf. [Psalm 17:4](https://ref.ly/Ps17:4) (on [16:3](https://ref.ly/Ps16:3) v. Comm.). In Chr sts. used peculiarly as a periphr., [1 Ch 28:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:1) b, [28:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:21) לכל נדיב *as regards* every liberal man \= every liberal man (cf.Ke), [29:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:5) a, [29:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:6) b; cf. [Ezr 6:7](https://ref.ly/Ezra6:7) (Aram.), [7:28](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:28). **f.** in connexion with terms designating a cause or occasion, *with referenceto* or *in view of* (Germ. *auf…hin*) becomes nearly equivalent to *on account of, through* (not common): so to cut oneself לָנֶפֶשׁ [Lv 19:28](https://ref.ly/Lev19:28) *on account of* a (dead) person, [Dt 14:1](https://ref.ly/Deut14:1), [Je 16:6](https://ref.ly/Jer16:6) b, [Lv 11:24](https://ref.ly/Lev11:24) לְאֵלֶּה תִּטַּמָּאוּ *on account of* these ye shall become unclean, [21:1](https://ref.ly/Lev21:1), [21:2](https://ref.ly/Lev21:2), [21:3](https://ref.ly/Lev21:3) \+, Ez [20:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:31) נִטְמָא לְ, [Nu 5:2](https://ref.ly/Num5:2) כָּל־טָמֵא לָנֶפֶשׁ [Nu 9:6](https://ref.ly/Num9:6), [9:7](https://ref.ly/Num9:7), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Num9:10), cf. [2 Ch 23:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:19); ´לְשֵׁם י *in view of* (i.e. determined by), *because of* ´י's name, Jo 9:9, [Je 3:17](https://ref.ly/Jer3:17), [Is 55:5](https://ref.ly/Isa55:5) (‖לְמַעַן), Ez [36:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:22) (do.); [Gn 4:23](https://ref.ly/Gen4:23) a I have slain a man לְפִצְעִי *because of* my wound, v:[23](https://ref.ly/Gen4:23) b, [Ex 4:26](https://ref.ly/Exod4:26) לַמּוּלוֺת, [Nu 35:33](https://ref.ly/Num35:33) לַדָּם לָכֵן \= *therefore* (syn. עַל כֵּן), constantly (v. כֵּן); [Job 30:24](https://ref.ly/Job30:24) (si vera l.) לָהֶן: of the cause of an emotion, [Is 15:5](https://ref.ly/Isa15:5) לִבִּי לְמוֺאָב יִזְעַק *because of* Moab [16:7](https://ref.ly/Isa16:7), [16:11](https://ref.ly/Isa16:11), [Je 31:20](https://ref.ly/Jer31:20) הָמוּ מֵעַי לוֺ (עַלּ Ct [5:4](https://ref.ly/Song5:4)), [Ho 10:5](https://ref.ly/Hos10:5), לְזאֹת [Jb 37:1](https://ref.ly/Job37:1). Cf. [Nu 16:34](https://ref.ly/Num16:34) נסו לקולם fled *at* the sound of them, Ez [27:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:28), Hb [3:16](https://ref.ly/Hab3:16), [Psalm 42:8](https://ref.ly/Ps42:8). **g.** marking the aim, object, or consequence of an action or thing, *in view of, for, unto:* (*a*) [Gn 1:16](https://ref.ly/Gen1:16) לממשׁלת היום *for* the rule of the day, [22:7](https://ref.ly/Gen22:7) where is the sheep לְעוֺלָה? [42:25](https://ref.ly/Gen42:25) provision לַדָּרֶךְ *for* the way; [Ex 20:7](https://ref.ly/Exod20:7) לַשָּׁוְא i.e. *for* a vain or frivolous purpose, similarly לָרִיק and לַשֶּׁקֶר; [Lv 1:3](https://ref.ly/Lev1:3) \+ לִרְצֹּנוֺ *for* his acceptance; [Nu 21:23](https://ref.ly/Num21:23) and oft. יצא למלחמה *for* battle; יָשַׁב לְ to sit (wait) *for,* [Ex 24:14](https://ref.ly/Exod24:14), [Hos 3:3](https://ref.ly/Hos3:3), [Je 3:2](https://ref.ly/Jer3:2); [1 S 8:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:16) to use לִמְלַאכְתּוֺ *for* his business; [2 S 15:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:2) \+ בָּא לַמִּשְׁפָּט *for* judgment; [Psalm 69:22](https://ref.ly/Ps69:22) לִצְמָאִי *for* (i.e. to quench) my thirst, [Ne 9:15](https://ref.ly/Neh9:15); [Ex 29:36](https://ref.ly/Exod29:36) \+ לַיּוֺם *for* each day; [Is 4:3](https://ref.ly/Isa4:3) כָּל־כָּתוכ לַחַיִּים *for* life; [Ho 9:4](https://ref.ly/Hos9:4) לַחְמָם לְנַפְשָׁם; לרעה and לטובה [Je 21:10](https://ref.ly/Jer21:10) \+; [Is 58:4](https://ref.ly/Isa58:4), [Psalm 63:10](https://ref.ly/Ps63:10) לְשׁוֺאָה יבקשׁו נפשׁי: in the sense of *to secure, compass,* [Gn 41:55](https://ref.ly/Gen41:55) cried to Ph. לַלָּחֶם *for* bread, [1 S 2:36](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:36), [Am 8:11](https://ref.ly/Amos8:11), [Jb 15:23](https://ref.ly/Job15:23), [Is 10:3](https://ref.ly/Isa10:3): so in לִמַעַן *for* the purpose of; and with an inf. oft. (v. **7 a**). (*b*) corresponding to the Lat. *dat. commodi,* (*a*) with vbs., [Gn 2:18](https://ref.ly/Gen2:18) אעשה לו I will make *for* him, etc., v:[20](https://ref.ly/Gen2:20), [3:21](https://ref.ly/Gen3:21), etc., absol. עָשָה לְ [1 S 14:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:6), [Is 64:3](https://ref.ly/Isa64:3), פָּעַל לְ [Psalm 68:29](https://ref.ly/Ps68:29); מָחָא לְ [Gn 8:9](https://ref.ly/Gen8:9); לָקַח לְ [24:3](https://ref.ly/Gen24:3), [24:4](https://ref.ly/Gen24:4) \+ oft.; סָפַד לְ, נוּד [Je 16:5](https://ref.ly/Jer16:5), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Jer16:6), בָּכָה לְ [22:10](https://ref.ly/Jer22:10), etc.; oft. with prons. and imv., [Nu 11:16](https://ref.ly/Num11:16) אֶסְפָה־לִּי gather *me* 70 men, [22:6](https://ref.ly/Num22:6) אָרָה־לִּי curse *me* this people, [23:1](https://ref.ly/Num23:1) בְּנֵה לִי, [1 K 1:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:28) קראו לי לבת־שׁבע call *me* B., [3:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:24), [13:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:13), [17:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:10), Ct [2:15](https://ref.ly/Song2:15) אֶחֱזוּ לָנוּ catch *us* the foxes, [Is 49:20](https://ref.ly/Isa49:20) גְּשָׁה־לִּי retire *for* me, that I may dwell, [2 S 18:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:5) לְאַט לִי לַנַּעַר (act) gently (**5 i** *b*) *for my sake* towards the young man, [2 K 4:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:24) אַל־תַּעֲצָר־לִי לִרְכֹּב AV slacken *me* not the riding; (β) with substs., e.g. in such phrases as פסךְ ´הוא לי [Ex 12:11](https://ref.ly/Exod12:11) a passover is it *unto* ´י, [13:6](https://ref.ly/Exod13:6) ´חַג לי, [16:25](https://ref.ly/Exod16:25) ´שַׁבָּת לי, [Is 23:18](https://ref.ly/Isa23:18) \+ ´קֹדֶשׁ לי, [Lv 1:9](https://ref.ly/Lev1:9) and oft. ´אִשֵּה רֵיחַ נִיחוֺחַ לי, [1 S 1:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:3) ´כהנים לי, etc.; (γ) also as a *dat. incommodi,* as to lie in wait, lay snares, dig a pit, etc., *for* any one, Ju [9:25](https://ref.ly/Judg9:25), [16:2](https://ref.ly/Judg16:2), [Psalm 35:7](https://ref.ly/Ps35:7), [57:7](https://ref.ly/Ps57:7) etc.; with vbs. of withholding or removing (rare), Ju [17:2](https://ref.ly/Judg17:2) לֻקַּח לָח [1 S 21:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:6) (cf. ܒܨܪܠ) [Psalm 40:11](https://ref.ly/Ps40:11), [84:12](https://ref.ly/Ps84:12), [Jb 12:20](https://ref.ly/Job12:20); note also the phr. לְ (הַמַּעְבָּרוֺת)לָכַד הַמַּיִם Ju [3:28](https://ref.ly/Judg3:28) (RV), [7:24](https://ref.ly/Judg7:24), [12:5](https://ref.ly/Judg12:5): זכר ל, in both senses, to remember *for* (in one's favour) [Je 2:2](https://ref.ly/Jer2:2) \+ , *against* [Psalm 137:7](https://ref.ly/Ps137:7) \+, cf. גער לְ [Mal 3:11](https://ref.ly/Mal3:11) and [2:3](https://ref.ly/Mal2:3). (*c*) more distinctly *on behalf of,* as with קִנֵּא to be jealous *for,* [Nu 11:29](https://ref.ly/Num11:29) \+, נִלְחַם [Dt 3:22](https://ref.ly/Deut3:22) \+, שָׁמֶר [7:12](https://ref.ly/Deut7:12) \+, רָב לְ to contend *for* Ju [6:31](https://ref.ly/Judg6:31), יָרֵא [Jos 9:24](https://ref.ly/Josh9:24), הִתְפַּלֵּל [1 S 2:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:25) \+, דִּבֶּר to speak *for* one [2 K 4:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:13), [Jb 13:7](https://ref.ly/Job13:7) תְּדַבְּרוּ עַוְלָה הַלְאֵל will ye speak wickedness *on God's behalf ?* שָׁאַל to ask [1 S 22:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:13) \+, עָבַר to pass over *for* ( \= to pardon) [Am 7:8](https://ref.ly/Amos7:8); [Dt 30:12](https://ref.ly/Deut30:12) מי יעלה לנו, v:[13](https://ref.ly/Deut30:13), Ju [1:1](https://ref.ly/Judg1:1), [20:18](https://ref.ly/Judg20:18), [Is 6:8](https://ref.ly/Isa6:8) מִי יֵלֶח־לָנוּ; see also [Ex 2:19](https://ref.ly/Exod2:19), [4:16](https://ref.ly/Exod4:16) a, [Nu 35:31](https://ref.ly/Num35:31), , [Dt 23:6](https://ref.ly/Deut23:6), [Jos 18:6](https://ref.ly/Josh18:6), Ju [5:13](https://ref.ly/Judg5:13), [7:4](https://ref.ly/Judg7:4), [7:20](https://ref.ly/Judg7:20), [2 S 15:34](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:34) b, [Is 33:21](https://ref.ly/Isa33:21), [Pr 16:26](https://ref.ly/Prov16:26), [31:8](https://ref.ly/Prov31:8), etc.; [Psalm 94:16](https://ref.ly/Ps94:16) מִי יָקוּם לִי עִם מְרֵעִים; ´היה ל to be *on one's side,* [Hos 1:9](https://ref.ly/Hos1:9) ואנכי לא אהיה לכם, [Psalm 124:1](https://ref.ly/Ps124:1), [124:2](https://ref.ly/Ps124:2), and without היה [Gn 31:42](https://ref.ly/Gen31:42), [Ex 32:36](https://ref.ly/Exod32:36), אֵלַי ´מִי ל who is on ´י's side ? (let him come) to me ! [Jos 5:13](https://ref.ly/Josh5:13) b, [2 S 20:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:11), [2 K 10:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:6) אִם לִי אַתֶּם (syn. אִתִּי [9:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:32)), [Psalm 56:10](https://ref.ly/Ps56:10) לי ´זה ידעתי כי י, [118:6](https://ref.ly/Ps118:6), [118:7](https://ref.ly/Ps118:7) לִי ´י. **h.** used reflexively (the ‘ethical’ dative, or dative of feeling), throwing the action back upon the subj., and expressing with some pathos the interest, or satisfaction, or completeness, with which it is (or is to be) accomplished, esp. (but not exclusively) with imv. and I Pers. impf. (Cf. Ew§ 315 a. Very common in Syriac, esp. *b:* No:§ 224\.) **i.** of reference to a norm or standard, *according to, after, by:*—(*a*) [Gn 1:11](https://ref.ly/Gen1:11) \+ oft. P לְמִינוֺ *acc. to* its kinds, [8:19](https://ref.ly/Gen8:19) \+ לְמִשְׁפְּחֹתֵיהֶם *acc. to* their families, [10:5](https://ref.ly/Gen10:5) נִישׁ לִלְשֹׁנוֺ, v:[31](https://ref.ly/Gen10:31); v:[32](https://ref.ly/Gen10:32), [Ex 30:12](https://ref.ly/Exod30:12) \+ לִפְפְקֻדֵיהֶם *acc. to* them that are numbered of them, [Nu 1:2](https://ref.ly/Num1:2) לְבֵית אֲבוֺתָם *by* their fathers' houses, v:[2](https://ref.ly/Num1:2) לְגֻלְגְּלֽתָם v:[3](https://ref.ly/Num1:3) לְצִבְאֹתָם, v:[20](https://ref.ly/Num1:20) \+ oft., esp. in enumerations and classifications; [Gn 13:3](https://ref.ly/Gen13:3) Abram went לְמַסָּעָיו *by* his journeyings (stages), so לְמַסְעֵיהֶם [Ex 17:1](https://ref.ly/Exod17:1) \+; [Gn 13:17](https://ref.ly/Gen13:17) go through the land לְאָרְכָּהּ וּלְרָחְבָּהּ *acc. to* (i.e. to the full extent of) its length and breadth (cf. Hb [1:6](https://ref.ly/Hab1:6)); [41:47](https://ref.ly/Gen41:47) לִקְמָצִים *by* handfuls, [Nu 24:2](https://ref.ly/Num24:2) \+ לִשְׁבָטָיו *by* its tribes, [1 S 29:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:2) עברים למאות ולאלפים *by* hundreds and thousands, [2 S 18:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:4), לְעָרֶיהָ [Nu 32:33](https://ref.ly/Num32:33), [Jos 18:9](https://ref.ly/Josh18:9); Ju [19:19](https://ref.ly/Judg19:19) לַעֲצָמֶיהָ *acc. to* her bones (i.e. limb by limb), Ez [24:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:6) לִנְתָחֶיהָ piece by piece; [Psalm 140:12](https://ref.ly/Ps140:12) to hunt לְמַדְחֵפוֺת thrust\-*wise,* with thrust upon thrust, [Is 27:12](https://ref.ly/Isa27:12) לְאַחַד אֶחָד (Ges Ew) *by* one, one (i.e. one by one); hence, esp. with plurals, it acquires sts. a *distributive* force, as לִבְקָרִים [Is 33:2](https://ref.ly/Isa33:2) *by* mornings \= *every* morning (cf. **6**), so לַבְּקָרִים [Psalm 73:14](https://ref.ly/Ps73:14), [101:8](https://ref.ly/Ps101:8) \+, לִרְגָעִים [Is 27:3](https://ref.ly/Isa27:3) \+ *every* moment, לֶחֳדָשִׁים [Is 47:13](https://ref.ly/Isa47:13) *every* month, Ez [47:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:12), [1 K 10:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:22) אַחַת לְשָׁלוֺשׁ שָׁנִים once *every* three years, [Am 4:4](https://ref.ly/Amos4:4) לשׁלשׁת ימים *every* three days (but v. We), [1 Ch 9:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:25); in Chr לשׁער ושׁער לעיר ועיר, [2 Ch 8:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:14), [19:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:5), [26:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:11). (*b*) denoting the *principle,* with regard to which an act is done, לְמִסְפַּר *acc. to* the number of…[Dt 32:8](https://ref.ly/Deut32:8), Ju [21:23](https://ref.ly/Judg21:23) \+, [Is 11:3](https://ref.ly/Isa11:3) to judge לְמַרְאֵה עֵינָיו לְמִשְׁמַע אָזְנָיו *acc. to* that which his eyes see, his ears hear (cf. [Lv 13:12](https://ref.ly/Lev13:12), [Jb 42:5](https://ref.ly/Job42:5)), [28:26](https://ref.ly/Isa28:26), [32:1](https://ref.ly/Isa32:1) a king will regin לְצֶדֶק *acc. to* justice (‖לְמשׁפט), [42:3](https://ref.ly/Isa42:3) לֶאֱמֶת \= *faithfully,* [Je 9:2](https://ref.ly/Jer9:2) לֶאֱמוּנָה \= *honestly,* [15:15](https://ref.ly/Jer15:15), [30:11](https://ref.ly/Jer30:11) (\= [46:28](https://ref.ly/Jer46:28)) וְיִסַּרְתִּיְ לַמִּשְׁפָּט (synon. [10:24](https://ref.ly/Jer10:24) בְּמשׁפט), [Ho 2:12](https://ref.ly/Hos2:12) (‖לְפִי), [Jo 2:23](https://ref.ly/Joel2:23) לִצֶדָקָה; [Gn 38:24](https://ref.ly/Gen38:24) pregnant לִזְנוּנִים \= *unchastely,* [Nu 15:24](https://ref.ly/Num15:24) לשׁגגה *by* error (elsewhere בשׁגגה), [2 Ch 30:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:3), [35:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:8), Ct [7:10](https://ref.ly/Song7:10) flowing down למישׁרים *straightly* ([Pr 23:31](https://ref.ly/Prov23:31) ´ב), לָרֹב [Jb 26:3](https://ref.ly/Job26:3), [2 Ch 14:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:14), poet. לְמַכְבִּיר [Jb 36:31](https://ref.ly/Job36:31) *in* abundance, לְאַט \= *gently* [2 S 18:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:5) \+; [Ex 16:3](https://ref.ly/Exod16:3), [Psalm 78:25](https://ref.ly/Ps78:25) לָשבַע *acc. to* satiety; לְרֶגֶל *acc. to* the foot (pace) of [Gn 33:14](https://ref.ly/Gen33:14) \+ (v. רֶגֶל); [1 S 23:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:20) לְכָל־אַוַּת נַפְשְׁ ([Dt 12:15](https://ref.ly/Deut12:15) al. ´בְּ), [2 S 15:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:11) לְתֻמָּם *acc. to* their simplicity, i.e. unsuspectingly (so [1 K 22:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:34)), [1 K 9:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:11) לְכָל־חֶפְחוֺ, [Is 54:16](https://ref.ly/Isa54:16), Ez [22:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:6) לִזְרֹעוֺ, [Jb 12:5](https://ref.ly/Job12:5), [Psalm 119:91](https://ref.ly/Ps119:91), [119:154](https://ref.ly/Ps119:154) לְאִמְרִֽתְ (‖´כְּ v:[58](https://ref.ly/Ps119:58); v:[116](https://ref.ly/Ps119:116); v:[170](https://ref.ly/Ps119:170)), [Ec 1:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:10) long ago לְעולמים *acc. to* (measured by) the ages etc. (v. Hi): so also in the phr. לְפִי חֶרֶב *acc. to* a sword's mouth, i.e. as the sword would devour, without quarter, [Jos 6:21](https://ref.ly/Josh6:21) \+ oft.; …לְפִי itself also, in various fig. applications, has the force of *acc. to,* [Gn 47:12](https://ref.ly/Gen47:12), etc. (v. פֶּה); and in לְאֵל יָדְְֽ (אֵין)יֵשׁ it is (not) *acc. to* the power of thy hand (v. p. 43\). Similarly [Dt 11:11](https://ref.ly/Deut11:11) לִמְטַר הַשָּׁמַיִם *after the manner of* the rain of heaven, i.e. as the rain permits (opp. to the artificial irrigation of v:[10](https://ref.ly/Deut11:10)), Ju [21:12](https://ref.ly/Judg21:12) \+ לְמִשְׁכַּב זָכָר, Ez [12:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:12) לַעַיִן i.e. *as* the eye sees it. **j.** designating a condition or state: לָבֶטַח *in a state of* confidence \= confidently, [Lv 25:18](https://ref.ly/Lev25:18) \+ oft.; לְבָדָד, לְבַד, *in a state of* separation ( \= a part), so לְבַדּוֺ (v. pp. 94, 95\); לְשָׁלוֺם [Gn 44:17](https://ref.ly/Gen44:17) \+, לְפֶתַע suddenly † [Is 29:5](https://ref.ly/Isa29:5), [30:13](https://ref.ly/Isa30:13); לִבְלִי *in a condition of* no…\= without, [Is 5:14](https://ref.ly/Isa5:14) \+ (v. בלי), so …לְאֵין (late, v. p. 35\), לְלאֹ † [2 Ch 15:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:3); further [Is 1:5](https://ref.ly/Isa1:5) לָחֳלִי *in a state of* sickness, [50:11](https://ref.ly/Isa50:11) לְמַעֲצֵבָה, [Psalm 45:15](https://ref.ly/Ps45:15) לִרְקָמוֺת, [Ezr 2:63](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:63) \= [Ne 7:65](https://ref.ly/Neh7:65) a priest לאורים ולתמים *having relation to* (i.e. *with*) U. and Th., [2 Ch 20:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:21) לְהַדְרַת קֹדֶשׁ \= *in* holy adornment (cf. ´בְּ [Psalm 29:2](https://ref.ly/Ps29:2), [96:9](https://ref.ly/Ps96:9)). And of a concomitant circumstance (Germ. *bei*), *in presence of, at,* [Jb 29:3](https://ref.ly/Job29:3) לְאוֺרוֺ, Hb [3:11](https://ref.ly/Hab3:11), …לקול [Jb 21:12](https://ref.ly/Job21:12), [Ezr 3:13](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:13). **6\.** Of time: **a.** *towards, against,* sts. with collat. idea of *in view of,* much rarer than בְּ, but expressing concurrence (*at*) rather than duration (*in*): [Gn 3:8](https://ref.ly/Gen3:8) לְרוּחַ הַיּוֺם *at* the breeze of the day, לְעֵת in various connexions, as לְעֵת עֶרֶב [Gn 8:11](https://ref.ly/Gen8:11) \+ (v. עֵת); לְעִתִּים מְזֻמָּנִים † [Ezr 10:14](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:14), [Ne 10:35](https://ref.ly/Neh10:35); לְעִתּוֺת בַּצָּרָה † [Psalm 9:10](https://ref.ly/Ps9:10), [10:1](https://ref.ly/Ps10:1); …לְיוֺם *at, on* the day of, [Psalm 81:4](https://ref.ly/Ps81:4), [Pr 7:20](https://ref.ly/Prov7:20) \+, …מה תעשו לְיוֺם [Is 10:3](https://ref.ly/Isa10:3), [Ho 9:5](https://ref.ly/Hos9:5) (cf. [Je 5:31](https://ref.ly/Jer5:31)); …לַיָּמִים אֲשֶׁר † Ez [22:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:14); …לַיּוֺם אֲשֶׁר † [Mal 3:17](https://ref.ly/Mal3:17); לתשׁובת השׁנה [2 S 11:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:1) \+; לתקופת הימים † [1 S 1:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:20), לתקופת השׁנה † [2 Ch 24:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:23) (ex [34:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:22) without לְ), (יָמִים)לְקֵץ שָׁנִים (late) [2 Ch 18:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:2), [Ne 13:6](https://ref.ly/Neh13:6), [Dn 11:6](https://ref.ly/Dan11:6), [11:13](https://ref.ly/Dan11:13) (in early Heb. מִקֵּץ יָמִים); …לשׁנת † [2 Ch 15:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:10); לַבֹּקֶר [Psalm 30:6](https://ref.ly/Ps30:6), [49:15](https://ref.ly/Ps49:15) \+ ([Ex 34:2](https://ref.ly/Exod34:2) after הֱיֵה נָכוֺן \= *against, for;* cf. [19:11](https://ref.ly/Exod19:11), [Pr 21:31](https://ref.ly/Prov21:31)); (לַבְּקָרִים [Is 33:2](https://ref.ly/Isa33:2), v. **5 i**); לערב [Gn 49:27](https://ref.ly/Gen49:27) (‖בבקר) \+; לְמָחָר † [Ex 8:6](https://ref.ly/Exod8:6) (in answer to v:[5](https://ref.ly/Exod8:5) לְמָתַי), v:[19](https://ref.ly/Exod8:19), [Est 5:12](https://ref.ly/Esth5:12) ([Nu 11:18](https://ref.ly/Num11:18), [Jos 7:13](https://ref.ly/Josh7:13) after הִתְקַדְּשׁוּ \= *against*), לַמָּחֳרָת [Jon 4:7](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:7) (cf. [1 Ch 29:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:21)); לָאוֺר [Jb 24:14](https://ref.ly/Job24:14); לַמּוֺעֵד, …לְמוֺעֵד, [Gn 17:21](https://ref.ly/Gen17:21), [Ex 23:15](https://ref.ly/Exod23:15) \+; לְפָנִים and לִפְנֵי *before* (oft.); לְאָחוֺר *hereafter,* † [Is 41:23](https://ref.ly/Isa41:23), [42:23](https://ref.ly/Isa42:23); [Psalm 32:6](https://ref.ly/Ps32:6) b; with inf. (rare), in the phr. (עֶרֶב)בֹּקֶר(הַ)לִפְנוֺת [Gn 24:63](https://ref.ly/Gen24:63) \+, [2 S 18:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:29), [Is 7:15](https://ref.ly/Isa7:15) לְדַעְתּוֺ *when* he knoweth. **b.** to denote the *close* of a period (rare), [Gn 7:4](https://ref.ly/Gen7:4) לימים עוד שׁבעה, v:[10](https://ref.ly/Gen7:10), [Ex 19:15](https://ref.ly/Exod19:15), [2 S 13:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:23), [Am 4:4](https://ref.ly/Amos4:4) לשׁלשׁת ימים (We); [Ezr 10:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:8), [10:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:9), [Ne 6:15](https://ref.ly/Neh6:15), [Dn 12:7](https://ref.ly/Dan12:7) (cf. עַד [7:25](https://ref.ly/Dan7:25)) [2 Ch 21:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:19) (so Syr.: v. PS ܠ 5\). **c.** *towards, to,* [Ex 34:25](https://ref.ly/Exod34:25) לא ילין לַבֹּקֶר (usu. עַד, as [23:18](https://ref.ly/Exod23:18)), [Dt 16:4](https://ref.ly/Deut16:4), [1 S 13:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:8) (after נוֺחַל), [Am 4:7](https://ref.ly/Amos4:7) בעוד שׁלשׁה חדשׁים לַקָּצִיר *to* the harvest; oft. in the expressions לְעוֺלָם, לָנֶצַח, לְדוֺר וָדוֺר, לְדֹר דֹּר; rather differently in מִיּוֺם לְיוֺם [Psalm 96:2](https://ref.ly/Ps96:2) (‖ [1 Ch 16:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:23) אל), [Est 3:7](https://ref.ly/Esth3:7) (i.e. passing from day to day), cf. [2 S 14:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:26) (Gie30 f.). **d.** *for, during,* [Is 63:18](https://ref.ly/Isa63:18) לַמִּצְעָר (si vera l.), [2 Ch 11:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:17) לשׁנים שׁלושׁ, [29:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:17). **7\.** With an *inf.* (Ges§ 114, 2\), ל denotes **a.** most commonly the end or purpose of an action ( \= the Lat. Gerund with *ad,* e.g. *ad faciendum,* to do): [Gn 1:17](https://ref.ly/Gen1:17) and he placed them in the firmament וּלְהַבְדִּיל…לְהָאִיר וְלִמְשֹׁל *to* give light…, and *to* rule…, and *to* divide, etc., [2:15](https://ref.ly/Gen2:15) set him in the garden לְעָבְדָהּ וּלְשָׁמְרָהּ *to* till it, and *to* keep it, v:[9](https://ref.ly/Gen2:9) brought them to Adam לִרְאוֺת *to* see, etc., \+ very oft.; [Gn 19:20](https://ref.ly/Gen19:20) קְרֹבָה לָנוּס שָׁמָּה near *for* fleeing thither, [Ec 3:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:2) עֵת לָלֶדֶת a time *for* bringing forth. The neg. is expressed by לְבִלְתִּי, q.v. **b.** *with reference to,* limiting or qualifying the idea expressed by the principal vb., and so resolvable sts. into *so as to, to,* sts. into *in respect of, in:*—(*a*) *so as to, to,* [Dt 8:6](https://ref.ly/Deut8:6) and keep the commands of ´לָלֶכֶת בִּדְרָכָיו וּלְיִרְאָה אֹתוֺ י *to* walk in his ways, and *to* fear him, [10:15](https://ref.ly/Deut10:15), [11:22](https://ref.ly/Deut11:22), [19:9](https://ref.ly/Deut19:9), [1 K 2:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:3), [2:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:4), [11:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:2), [1 S 20:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:20), [20:36](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:36), [Jo 2:26](https://ref.ly/Joel2:26) אֲשֶׁר עָשָה עִמָּכֶם לְהַפְלִיא *so as to* do wondrously, Ez [5:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:6); Ju [5:18](https://ref.ly/Judg5:18) עַם חֵרֵף נַפְשׁוֺ לָמוּת *so as to* die, *for* dying \[not *‘unto* death’], [16:16](https://ref.ly/Judg16:16), [2 K 20:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:1) חָלָה לָמוּת; [Gn 2:3](https://ref.ly/Gen2:3) לַעֲשוֺת *so as to* make (or *in* making) which, he created; and in the very freq. לֵאמֹר, introducing the words spoken, *so as to say* \= *saying* (Germ. *indem* er sagte), [Gn 1:22](https://ref.ly/Gen1:22), etc. (*b*) *in respect of, in* (cf. **5 e** (*b*)) [Gn 34:7](https://ref.ly/Gen34:7), [1 S 12:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:17) your evil is great that ye have done לִשְׁאוֺל לכם מלך *in* asking for yourselves a king, v29, [14:33](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:33) the people sin against J. לאכל על־הדם *in* eating with the blood, [19:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:5), [2 S 19:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:7), [2 K 4:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:24), [Je 44:18](https://ref.ly/Jer44:18), [Psalm 36:3](https://ref.ly/Ps36:3), [63:3](https://ref.ly/Ps63:3), [78:18](https://ref.ly/Ps78:18), [101:8](https://ref.ly/Ps101:8), [103:20](https://ref.ly/Ps103:20), [Ne 13:18](https://ref.ly/Neh13:18). And with the *tert. compar.,* above **5 e** (*b*). Esp. with verbs expressing what with us would be denoted by an adv. adjunct, but in Heb. idiom forms the principal idea, as [1 S 1:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:12) הִרְבְּתָה לְהִתְפַּלֵּל lit. did much *in respect of* praying ( \= prayed long *or* much), [Is 55:7](https://ref.ly/Isa55:7) כִּי יַרְבֶּה לִסְלוֺחַ \+; [2 K 2:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:10) הִקְשִׁיתָ לִשְׁאוֺל thou hast done hardly *in respect of* asking ( \= asked a hard thing), [1 K 14:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:9) הֵרַע לַעֲשוֺת; so with הקריב [Gn 12:11](https://ref.ly/Gen12:11), מִהַר [27:20](https://ref.ly/Gen27:20), הרחיק [Ex 8:24](https://ref.ly/Exod8:24) הֶעְפִּיל [Nu 14:44](https://ref.ly/Num14:44), הֵהִין [Dt 1:41](https://ref.ly/Deut1:41), בּשֵׁשׁ Ju [5:28](https://ref.ly/Judg5:28), הפליא [13:19](https://ref.ly/Judg13:19), [2 Ch 26:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:15) (with *pass.* vb.), שׁוּב [1 K 13:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:17), [Ezr 9:14](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:14), היטיב [Je 1:12](https://ref.ly/Jer1:12) \+ (without לְ [1 S 16:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:17)), העמיק [Is 29:15](https://ref.ly/Isa29:15) \+, קֵרֵב Ez [36:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:9), הגדיל [Jo 2:20](https://ref.ly/Joel2:20) \+, קִדַּמְתִּי לִבְרֹחַ [Jon 4:2](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:2), הגביהּ [Psalm 113:5](https://ref.ly/Ps113:5), השׁפיל v:[6](https://ref.ly/Ps113:6); [Gn 31:27](https://ref.ly/Gen31:27) נַחְבֵּאתָ לִבְרֹחַ hast hidden thyself *in regard to* fleeing \= hast fled secretly, [2 S 19:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:4) וַיִּתְגַּנֵּב לָבוֺא \= come in stealthily. (*c.*) by an extension of (*b*), the inf. with לְ so forms the complement of a verb that, if the verb be trans., it becomes virtually its *object:* so very oft. with such verbs as הוסיף *to add* [Gn 4:2](https://ref.ly/Gen4:2), [4:12](https://ref.ly/Gen4:12), הֵחֵל *to begin* [6:1](https://ref.ly/Gen6:1), חדל [11:8](https://ref.ly/Gen11:8), יכל [13:6](https://ref.ly/Gen13:6), מִהַר [18:7](https://ref.ly/Gen18:7), נתן *to permit* [20:6](https://ref.ly/Gen20:6), אבה [24:5](https://ref.ly/Gen24:5), בקשׁ [Ex 2:15](https://ref.ly/Exod2:15), מֵאֵן [7:14](https://ref.ly/Exod7:14), למד [Dt 14:23](https://ref.ly/Deut14:23), חפץ [25:8](https://ref.ly/Deut25:8), ידע [1 K 5:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:20) (these all occur also without לְ); הואיל *to undertake, consent,* [Gn 18:17](https://ref.ly/Gen18:17); [18:31](https://ref.ly/Gen18:31), כִּלָּה *to finish,* תָּמַם [Dt 2:16](https://ref.ly/Deut2:16) (to come to an end *in respect of*), קִוָּה [Is 5:2](https://ref.ly/Isa5:2); also חִוָּה [Gn 50:2](https://ref.ly/Gen50:2), אָמַר [Ex 2:14](https://ref.ly/Exod2:14), דִּמָּה [Nu 33:56](https://ref.ly/Num33:56), חשׁב [1 S 18:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:25), יעץ [Psalm 62:5](https://ref.ly/Ps62:5), לִמֵּד [Je 12:16](https://ref.ly/Jer12:16), אָהֵב [Ho 12:8](https://ref.ly/Hos12:8): [Dt 10:12](https://ref.ly/Deut10:12) what doth ´י ask of thee כי אם ליראה except *to* fear etc. ? (cf. [Mi 6:8](https://ref.ly/Mic6:8) after דרשׁ without ל). (*d*) as the *subj.* of a sentence (rare): [Is 10:7](https://ref.ly/Isa10:7) להשׁמיד בלבבו, [1 Ch 29:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:12); with טוב [1 S 15:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:22), [Psalm 118:8](https://ref.ly/Ps118:8), [118:9](https://ref.ly/Ps118:9), [Ec 7:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:2), [7:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:5), [Pr 21:9](https://ref.ly/Prov21:9) (usu. without לְ, as v:[19](https://ref.ly/Prov21:19), [25:24](https://ref.ly/Prov25:24), [Ex 14:12](https://ref.ly/Exod14:12)); cf. [Ex 8:22](https://ref.ly/Exod8:22) לא נכון לעשות כן; [2 S 18:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:11) וְעָלַי לָתֵת, [Ne 13:13](https://ref.ly/Neh13:13), [Ezr 10:12](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:12), [Mi 3:1](https://ref.ly/Mic3:1) הלא לכם לדעת, [Ezr 4:3](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:3), [2 Ch 13:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:5), [20:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:17), [26:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:18). (*e*) with יֵשׁ, אֵין (late), and (more rarely) לאֹ, in sense of *it is* (*not*) *possible to*…, or (sts.) *there is no need to*…: see יֵשׁ **2 c** *c* (p. 442\); אַיִן **5** (p. 34 b), adding [Hag 1:6](https://ref.ly/Hag1:6), [Est 8:8](https://ref.ly/Esth8:8), [2 Ch 22:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:9); לאֹ **1 a** *b* (p. 518\): and cf. Dr§ 202 Ges§ 114 l Dav:§ 94 b, 95 b. (*f*) with הָיָה, to express the idea of *destination,* as [Nu 24:22](https://ref.ly/Num24:22) וקין יהיה לְבָעֵר shall be *for consuming,* [Dt 31:17](https://ref.ly/Deut31:17), [Is 5:5](https://ref.ly/Isa5:5), [6:13](https://ref.ly/Isa6:13), [37:26](https://ref.ly/Isa37:26), Ez [30:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:16), [Psalm 109:13](https://ref.ly/Ps109:13) \+. Cf. מֶה לַעֲשוֺת *what is* (*was*) *to be done*? [Is 5:4](https://ref.ly/Isa5:4), [2 K 4:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:13), [2 Ch 25:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:9) \+ (Dr§ 203\). (*g*) expressing (acc. to the context) *tendency, intention,* or *obligation* (the ‘periphrastic’ future):—[Ho 9:13](https://ref.ly/Hos9:13) וְאֶפְרַיִם לְהוֺצִיא אֶל הוֺרֵג בָּנָיו *is for* bringing forth (\= *must* bring forth), [Is 10:32](https://ref.ly/Isa10:32) עוד היום בנֹב לעמד *is he for* tarrying (*must he* tarry), [38:20](https://ref.ly/Isa38:20) להושׁיעני ´י, ´י *is* (*ready*) *to* save me, [44:14](https://ref.ly/Isa44:14) (si vera l.), [Jer 51:49](https://ref.ly/Jer51:49), Hb [1:17](https://ref.ly/Hab1:17), [Psalm 32:9](https://ref.ly/Ps32:9), [49:15](https://ref.ly/Ps49:15) חוּרָם לְבַלּוֺת שְׁאוֺל \= *must* Sheol waste away, [62:10](https://ref.ly/Ps62:10) בְּמאֹזְנַיִם לַעֲלוֺת, [Pr 18:24](https://ref.ly/Prov18:24), [19:8](https://ref.ly/Prov19:8) שׁומר תבונה למצא טוב *will be finding* prosperity, [20:25](https://ref.ly/Prov20:25), [Jb 30:6](https://ref.ly/Job30:6), [1 Ch 22:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:5) (לִבְנוֺת), [Ec 3:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:15): of past time, [Gn 15:12](https://ref.ly/Gen15:12) ויהי השׁמשׁ לבוא *was about to* go down, [Jos 2:5](https://ref.ly/Josh2:5), [1 S 14:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:21) b (txt. dub.: Dr§ 206 *Obs.*), [2 Ch 26:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:5) (strangely) ויהי לדרשׁ אלהים RV *set himself to* seek; usu. without היה, [2 S 4:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:10) אֲשֶׁר לְתִתִּי לוֺ to whom *it was for my* giving (*I ought to have* given), [2 K 13:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:19) לְהַכּוֺת *percutiendum erat,* [1 Ch 9:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:25), and more freely [2 Ch 11:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:22) כי להמליבו for (he was) *for making* him king, [12:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr12:12) ולא להשׁחית and *was no longer for* destroying him, [36:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:19) (?): in a question, [Gn 30:15](https://ref.ly/Gen30:15) וְלָקַחַת and *art thou for* taking ? [Est 7:8](https://ref.ly/Esth7:8), [2 Ch 19:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:2) הֲלָרָשָׁע לַעְזֹּר *wilt thou help* the wicked ? Cf. Dr§ 204, Ges158; 114 *h\-k*, Dav:§ 94\. (*h*) with וְ, in contin. (mostly) of a finite vb. or ptcp., [Ex 32:29](https://ref.ly/Exod32:29) וְלָתֵת …מַלְאוּ יֶדְכֶם and *be for placing* etc. [Lv 10:10](https://ref.ly/Lev10:10) f. (?), [1 S 8:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:12) וְלָשוּם …יִקַּח, [Je 19:12](https://ref.ly/Jer19:12) …אעשה וְלָתֵת, [44:14](https://ref.ly/Jer44:14), [Ho 12:3](https://ref.ly/Hos12:3), [Psalm 25:14](https://ref.ly/Ps25:14), [109:16](https://ref.ly/Ps109:16), [Jb 34:8](https://ref.ly/Job34:8), [Ec 7:25](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:25), [9:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:1) (si vera l.), [Dn 12:11](https://ref.ly/Dan12:11), [Neh 8:13](https://ref.ly/Neh8:13), [1 Ch 10:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:13), [2 Ch 2:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:8), [7:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:17), [8:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:13), [30:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:9), Ez [13:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:22); [Am 8:4](https://ref.ly/Amos8:4) ´הַשֹּׁאֲפִים אֶבְיוֺן וְלַשְׁבִּית וג *and* (that are) *for making* the poor to cease, [Is 44:28](https://ref.ly/Isa44:28) וְלֵאמֹר …הָאוֺמֵר, [56:6](https://ref.ly/Isa56:6), [Psalm 104:21](https://ref.ly/Ps104:21), [Je 17:10](https://ref.ly/Jer17:10), [44:19](https://ref.ly/Jer44:19), [1 Ch 6:34](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:34) (cf. Dr§ 206 Dav:§ 96 R. 4\).—On לְמִן, v. מִן. *Note.*—[1 K 6:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:19) לְתִתֵּן שָׁם, the supposition that לְ is a conj. (\= למען) is too alien to Heb. usage to be justified by the Ar. لِ for كِكَي, and the view that תִתֵּן here and [17:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:14) is an anom. form for תֵת (Ew§ 238 c Köi. 305\) is against analogy: rd. with Ol:§ 224 d, Ges§ 67 A. 3, Klo, לָתֵת (as [17:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:14) Qr). 

> \[Str3926] † לְמוֺ poet. for לְ (v. מוֺ) [Jb 27:14](https://ref.ly/Job27:14), [29:21](https://ref.ly/Job29:21), [38:40](https://ref.ly/Job38:40), [40:4](https://ref.ly/Job40:4).
