## לאֹ (id: BDB04732)

\[Str3808] לאֹ or לוֺא **adv.** not (Ar. لَا, Aram. לָא, ܠܴܐ, Sab. לא, Ass. *lâ*; not in Eth.: cf. Köii. 1\. 236 WalkerAJSL 1896, 237 ff.)—לוֺא, acc. to Mass. (FrMM 248\), 35 t., besides בְּלוֺא 6 t., and הֲלוֺא, the orthogr. of which varies much (*ib.*:p. 251\), e.g. in S always הֲלוֺא, in Chr always הֲלאֹ, on the whole הֲלוֺא 141 t., הֲלאֹ 128 t.; twice, acc. to Mass., written לו (Qr לאֹ), [1 S 2:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:16), [20:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:2), once לה [Dt 3:11](https://ref.ly/Deut3:11) Kt:—*not*—denying objectively, like οὐ (not μὴ \= אַל):—**1\.** in predication: **a.** with a *verb;* so most freq., and nearly always (*a*) with the finite tenses, whether pf. ([Gn 2:5](https://ref.ly/Gen2:5) b, [4:5](https://ref.ly/Gen4:5) etc.) or impf. ([3:4](https://ref.ly/Gen3:4), [8:21](https://ref.ly/Gen8:21), [8:22](https://ref.ly/Gen8:22), etc.); in short circumst. clauses, as [Gn 44:4](https://ref.ly/Gen44:4) לא הרחיקו, [Is 40:20](https://ref.ly/Isa40:20) לאֹ יִמּוֺט, and with a final force [41:7](https://ref.ly/Isa41:7), [Ex 28:32](https://ref.ly/Exod28:32) (v. Ges§ 156\. 3 R. b, c Dr§ 162\). Governing two closely connected verbs (Dr§ 115, לא) [Ex 28:43](https://ref.ly/Exod28:43) וְלאֹ יִשְאוּ עָוֺן וָמֵתוּ, [Lv 19:12](https://ref.ly/Lev19:12), [19:29](https://ref.ly/Lev19:29) b, [Dt 7:25](https://ref.ly/Deut7:25), [7:26](https://ref.ly/Deut7:26), [19:10](https://ref.ly/Deut19:10), [22:1](https://ref.ly/Deut22:1) al.; and two parallel clauses (Ges§ 152\. 3\) [Is 23:4](https://ref.ly/Isa23:4) b, [Psalm 9:19](https://ref.ly/Ps9:19), [44:19](https://ref.ly/Ps44:19), [Jb 3:1](https://ref.ly/Job3:1) al. With the impf., esp. with 2 ps., לאֹ often expresses (not, like אַל, a deprecation, *do not*…, *let not*…, but) a *prohibition,* as [Gn 2:17](https://ref.ly/Gen2:17) לאֹ תאֹכַל מִמֶּנּוּ thou *shall not* eat of it, [3:1](https://ref.ly/Gen3:1), [3:3](https://ref.ly/Gen3:3), [Ex 20:3](https://ref.ly/Exod20:3) לאֹ־יִהְיֶה לְ there *shall not* be to thee, etc., v:[13](https://ref.ly/Exod20:13) לא תגנב, etc. With the coh. and juss. moods (which are negatived regularly by אַל), it occurs only exceptionally (Ges§ 109\. 1 b R. 1\), [Gn 24:8](https://ref.ly/Gen24:8), [1 S 14:36](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:36), [2 S 17:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:12), [18:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:14), [1 K 2:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:6), Ez [48:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:14). (*b*) with the inf. (which is negatived by בִּלְתִּי, q.v.), only once, in בְּלאֹ (**4 a**), and with לְ, in the sense of *cannot,* or *must not;* † Ju [1:19](https://ref.ly/Judg1:19) כי לא להורישׁ for it *was not* (possible) *to* dispossess, etc. [Am 6:10](https://ref.ly/Amos6:10) לא ´להזכיר בשׁם י, [1 Ch 5:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:1), [15:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:2) (Dr§ 202\. 2\); cf. Aram. לָא [Dan 6:9](https://ref.ly/Dan6:9), [Ezr 6:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra6:8). On its use with the ptcp., see **b** *c.* (*c*) לא always negatives properly the word immed. following: hence, in a verbal sentence, where this is not the verb, some special stress rests upon it, [Gn 32:29](https://ref.ly/Gen32:29) לאֹ יַעֲקֹב ´יֵאָמֵר עוֺד וג *not Jacob* shall thy name be called any more, but Israel, [45:8](https://ref.ly/Gen45:8) לאֹ אַתֶּם שְׁלַחְתֶּם אֹתִי *Not ye* (in our idiom: It is not ye who) have sent me hither, but God, [Ex 16:8](https://ref.ly/Exod16:8), [1 S 8:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:7) כִּי לאֹ ´אֹתְ מָאָסוּ כִּי אֹתִי וג, [Psalm 115:17](https://ref.ly/Ps115:17); without a foll. correcting clause, [Gn 38:9](https://ref.ly/Gen38:9), [Nu 16:29](https://ref.ly/Num16:29) :שְׁלָחָנִי ´לאֹ י *Not* ´י (but another) hath sent me, [Dt 32:27](https://ref.ly/Deut32:27) לאֹ פָּעַל זאֹת ´י, [1 Ch 17:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:4), [Dt 8:9](https://ref.ly/Deut8:9), [Is 28:28](https://ref.ly/Isa28:28) …לא לנצח *not for ever* (but only for a while)…(so [57:16](https://ref.ly/Isa57:16), [Psalm 9:19](https://ref.ly/Ps9:19), [49:18](https://ref.ly/Ps49:18), [103:9](https://ref.ly/Ps103:9); but [Is 13:20](https://ref.ly/Isa13:20) לא תשׁב לנצח is, will *not be inhabited* for ever), [Is 43:22](https://ref.ly/Isa43:22), [Jb 13:16](https://ref.ly/Job13:16), [32:9](https://ref.ly/Job32:9); hence rhetorically, insinuating something very different, not named, [2 K 6:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:10) *not* once, and *not* twice (but repeatedly), [Ezr 10:13](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:13), [Je 4:11](https://ref.ly/Jer4:11) a wind :לאֹ לְזָרוֺת וְלוֺא לְהָבַֽר *not* to winnow, and *not* to cleanse (but to exterminate), [Is 45:13](https://ref.ly/Isa45:13), [48:1](https://ref.ly/Isa48:1) b, [Jos 24:12](https://ref.ly/Josh24:12), [Dn 11:20](https://ref.ly/Dan11:20), [11:29](https://ref.ly/Dan11:29), [Jb 34:20](https://ref.ly/Job34:20) לאֹ בְיָד (but by a Divine agency: cf. [Dn 2:34](https://ref.ly/Dan2:34); also [Jb 20:26](https://ref.ly/Job20:26) אֵשׁ לאֹ נֻפָּךְ fire *not* blown upon \[but kindled from heaven], [Lam 4:6](https://ref.ly/Lam4:6); and אין [Is 47:14](https://ref.ly/Isa47:14)). (*d*) standing alone: (*a*) אִם־לאֹ *if not*, [Gn 18:21](https://ref.ly/Gen18:21) :וְאִם־לאֹ אֵדָֽעָה, 29:49, [42:16](https://ref.ly/Gen42:16), [Jb 9:24](https://ref.ly/Job9:24), [24:25](https://ref.ly/Job24:25); (β) אִם־לא…הֲ, *or not*, [Gn 24:21](https://ref.ly/Gen24:21) waiting to know דַּרְכּוֺ אִם־לאֹ ´הַהִצְלִיחַ י, [27:21](https://ref.ly/Gen27:21), [37:32](https://ref.ly/Gen37:32), [Ex 16:4](https://ref.ly/Exod16:4), [Nu 11:23](https://ref.ly/Num11:23), [Dt 8:2](https://ref.ly/Deut8:2), Ju [2:22](https://ref.ly/Judg2:22) (cf. אִם אַיִן, אַיִן **2 d** β, δ). In answer to a question or request, to deny, or decline, *Nay, No:* Ju [12:5](https://ref.ly/Judg12:5) ויאמר לא, [Hg 2:12](https://ref.ly/Hag2:12); לא אדני [Gn 23:11](https://ref.ly/Gen23:11), [42:10](https://ref.ly/Gen42:10), [1 S 1:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:15) \+; oft. sq. כי, *No: for*…\= *No: but*…, [Gn 18:15](https://ref.ly/Gen18:15) ויאמר לאֹ כִּי צָחָקְתְּ, [19:2](https://ref.ly/Gen19:2), [Jos 5:14](https://ref.ly/Josh5:14), [24:21](https://ref.ly/Josh24:21), [1 S 2:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:16), Qr (v. Dr), [10:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:19) (LXX MSS), [2 S 16:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:18), [24:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:24), [1 K 3:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:22), [3:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:22) \+; [Jb 23:6](https://ref.ly/Job23:6) (strangely). (Cf., in deprecation, אַל.) (*e*) with an interrog. force, which however does not lie in לא as such, but (as in other cases) in the contrast with a preceding clause, or in the tone of voice (cf. וְ **1** *f;* Ew§ 324 a Ges§ 150\. 1 Dr:[1 S 11:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:12)): [Jon 4:11](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:11) ´וַאֲנִי לאֹ אָחוּס וג…אַתָּה חַסְתָּ, [Jb 2:10](https://ref.ly/Job2:10); [22:11](https://ref.ly/Job22:11); [Ex 8:22](https://ref.ly/Exod8:22); [2 K 5:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:26), [Je 49:9](https://ref.ly/Jer49:9) (‖ Ob:[5](https://ref.ly/Obad1:5) הֲלוֺא), [Mal 2:15](https://ref.ly/Mal2:15), [La 3:38](https://ref.ly/Lam3:38); and in passages, exeg. or text. doubtful (v. Comm.), [1 S 20:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:14), [2 S 23:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:5) (but v. Bu), [Ho 10:9](https://ref.ly/Hos10:9) (Ew We), [11:5](https://ref.ly/Hos11:5) (Ew), [Jb 14:16](https://ref.ly/Job14:16) b (but LXX Ew Di תַעֲבֹר), [La 1:12](https://ref.ly/Lam1:12) (Ew Ke), [3:36](https://ref.ly/Lam3:36) (Ke Bä). **b.** with *adjs.* and *subst.:* (*a*) [Gn 2:18](https://ref.ly/Gen2:18) לאֹ טוֺב הֱֶיוֺת הָאָדָם לְבַדֹּו *not good* is man's being alone, [Ex 18:17](https://ref.ly/Exod18:17) \+ oft. (*b*) [Ex 4:16](https://ref.ly/Exod4:16) לאֹ אִישׁ דְּבָרִים אָנֹכִי, [Am 7:14](https://ref.ly/Amos7:14) לא נביא אנכי, [Nu 23:9](https://ref.ly/Num23:9) לאֹ אִישׁ אֵל וִיכַזֵּב, [Dt 17:15](https://ref.ly/Deut17:15) (v. אשׁר **2\.** *b*) [20:20](https://ref.ly/Deut20:20), [32:47](https://ref.ly/Deut32:47), [1 S 15:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:29), [2 S 18:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:20) לאֹ אִישׁ בְּשרָה אַתָּה הַיּוֺם, [21:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:2), [1 K 22:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:33), [2 K 6:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:19) לא זִה הדרך, [Mi 2:10](https://ref.ly/Mic2:10), [Is 27:11](https://ref.ly/Isa27:11), [Ho 8:6](https://ref.ly/Hos8:6); והמה לא אלהים [Je 2:11](https://ref.ly/Jer2:11), [16:20](https://ref.ly/Jer16:20), [2 K 19:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:18), [1 K 19:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:11), [19:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:11) לאֹ ´בָרַעַשׁ י, [Dt 30:12](https://ref.ly/Deut30:12), [30:13](https://ref.ly/Deut30:13), [Jb 15:9](https://ref.ly/Job15:9), [28:14](https://ref.ly/Job28:14) לאֹ בִי הִיא (‖אֵין עִמָּדִי)s, [Psalm 74:9](https://ref.ly/Ps74:9), [Je 5:10](https://ref.ly/Jer5:10) המה ´לוא לי, [10:16](https://ref.ly/Jer10:16); [Dt 32:21](https://ref.ly/Deut32:21) בָּנִים לאֹ אֵמוּן בָּם, [Je 10:14](https://ref.ly/Jer10:14), Hb [1:14](https://ref.ly/Hab1:14), [Jb 16:17](https://ref.ly/Job16:17), [38:26](https://ref.ly/Job38:26) מִדְבָּר לאֹ אָדָם בּוֺ; [1 K 22:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:17) לאֹ אֲדֹנִים לָאֵלֶּה, [Je 49:31](https://ref.ly/Jer49:31), [Psalm 22:3](https://ref.ly/Ps22:3) וְלאֹ דוּמִיָּה לִי, [Jb 18:17](https://ref.ly/Job18:17), [18:19](https://ref.ly/Job18:19), [29:12](https://ref.ly/Job29:12) ולא עֹזֵר לו, [30:13](https://ref.ly/Job30:13), [33:9](https://ref.ly/Job33:9); [Je 2:19](https://ref.ly/Jer2:19) וְלאֹ פַחְדָּתִי אֵלַיִךְ and (that) my terror reached not unto thee, [Jb 21:9](https://ref.ly/Job21:9); abs. [Gn 29:7](https://ref.ly/Gen29:7) לאֹ־עֵתִ הֵאָסֵף הַמִּקְנֶה ([Hg 1:2](https://ref.ly/Hag1:2)), [Nu 20:5](https://ref.ly/Num20:5), [2 K 4:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:23) לא חדשׁ ולא שׁבת, [Is 44:9](https://ref.ly/Isa44:9), [Je 5:12](https://ref.ly/Jer5:12) ויאמרו לוא הוא, [Jb 9:32](https://ref.ly/Job9:32), [22:16](https://ref.ly/Job22:16), [36:26](https://ref.ly/Job36:26), [41:2](https://ref.ly/Job41:2); [Pr 19:7](https://ref.ly/Prov19:7) (si vera l.) מְרַדֵּף אֲמָרִים לאֹ־הֵמָּה words which *are not*, which *are nought.* (*c*) with the ptcp. לאֹ is rare, a finite vb. being usu. preferred ([Ex 34:7](https://ref.ly/Exod34:7) וְנַקִּה לאֹ יְנַקֶּה: Ew§ 320 c Dr§ 162\): [2 S 3:34](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:34) יָדֶיְ לאֹ אֲסֻרוֺת, Ez [4:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:14), [22:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:24), [Dt 28:61](https://ref.ly/Deut28:61), [Psalm 38:15](https://ref.ly/Ps38:15) כְּאִישׁ אֲשֶׁר לאֹ שֹׁמֵעַ who is *not hearing*, [Jb 12:3](https://ref.ly/Job12:3), [13:2](https://ref.ly/Job13:2) לאֹ נֹפֵל אָנֹכִי מִכֶּם, [Zp 3:5](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:5) (very anom.); [1 K 10:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:21) לא is prob. text. err. In וְהוּא לאֹ שנֵא לוֺ or לא שנא הוא לו, [Dt 4:42](https://ref.ly/Deut4:42), [19:4](https://ref.ly/Deut19:4), [19:6](https://ref.ly/Deut19:6), [Jos 20:5](https://ref.ly/Josh20:5) (cf. אֹיֵב [Nu 35:23](https://ref.ly/Num35:23)), שנֵא is best construed as a subst., he being a *not\-hater* to him afore\-time.—In most of the cases under *b, c,* אין could have been employed; but the negation by לאֹ is more pointed and forcible.

 **2\.** Not in predication: **a.** coupled to an adj. to negative it, like the Gk. *a²\-,* but usu. by way of litotes: [Ho 13:13](https://ref.ly/Hos13:13) בן לא־חכם an *unwise* son, [Psalm 36:5](https://ref.ly/Ps36:5) דֶּרֶח לאֹ טוֺב a way *not good*, [43:1](https://ref.ly/Ps43:1) גוי לא חסיד, [Pr 16:29](https://ref.ly/Prov16:29), [30:25](https://ref.ly/Prov30:25), [30:26](https://ref.ly/Prov30:26), Ez [20:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:25), [2 Ch 30:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:17) : [Is 16:14](https://ref.ly/Isa16:14) לוֺא כַבִּיר, cf. [10:7](https://ref.ly/Isa10:7) לאֹ מְעַט. **b.** with a ptcp. [Je 2:2](https://ref.ly/Jer2:2) ארץ לא זרועה, [18:15](https://ref.ly/Jer18:15) (the finite verb is more common: see [Is 62:12](https://ref.ly/Isa62:12) עִיר לאֹ נֱעֶזָ֫בָה (cf. [54:11](https://ref.ly/Isa54:11)), [Je 6:8](https://ref.ly/Jer6:8) אֶרֶץ לוֺא נוֺשָׁ֫בָה, [15:18](https://ref.ly/Jer15:18), [22:6](https://ref.ly/Jer22:6), [31:18](https://ref.ly/Jer31:18), [Zp 2:1](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:1)). **c.** † [Gn 15:13](https://ref.ly/Gen15:13) בארץ לא להם, [Je 5:19](https://ref.ly/Jer5:19), [Hab 1:6](https://ref.ly/Hab1:6) מִשְׁכָּנוֺת לאֹ־לוֺ, [Pr 26:17](https://ref.ly/Prov26:17) רִיב לאֹ־לוֺ. **d.** with a subst., in poetry, forming a kind of compound, expressing pointedly its antithesis or negation (Germ. *un\-* is sts. used similarly): [Dt 32:5](https://ref.ly/Deut32:5)(?), [32:17](https://ref.ly/Deut32:17), [32:21](https://ref.ly/Deut32:21) a they made me jealous בְּלאֹ אֵל with a *not\-God* (with what in no respect deserved the name of God), vb בְּלאֹ עָם i.e. with an unorganized horde, [Am 6:13](https://ref.ly/Amos6:13) הַשְּמֵחִים לְלאֹ בָבָר i.e. at a thing wh. *is not*, an unreality (of their boasted strength), [Is 10:15](https://ref.ly/Isa10:15) כְּהָרִים מַטֶּה לאֹ עֵץ like a rod's lifting up *what is no wood* (but the agent wielding it), [31:8](https://ref.ly/Isa31:8) חרב לא אישׁ, חרב לא אדם, [55:2](https://ref.ly/Isa55:2) בלוא לחם for *what is not bread*, בלוא לשבעה for *what is not* for satiety, [Je 5:7](https://ref.ly/Jer5:7) וַיּשָּֽׁבְעוּ בְּלאֹ אֱלֽהִים by *not\-gods*, in late prose [2 Ch 13:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:9) כֹּהֵן לְלאֹ אֱלֽהִים; [Psalm 44:13](https://ref.ly/Ps44:13) תִּמְכֹּר עַמְּ בְּלאֹ־הוֺן for *no\-value* (i.e. cheaply), [Pr 13:23](https://ref.ly/Prov13:23), [Jb 10:12](https://ref.ly/Job10:12) צלמות וְלאֹ סְדָרִים darkness and *disorder;* so לאֹ־עַמִּי [Ho 1:9](https://ref.ly/Hos1:9), [2:25](https://ref.ly/Hos2:23): still more pregnantly [Jb 26:2](https://ref.ly/Job26:2) a מֶה־עָזַרְתָּ לְלאֹ־כֹחַ (poet. for לאשׁר אין לו כח) the *powerless*, v:[2b](https://ref.ly/Job26:2); v:[3a](https://ref.ly/Job26:3) (Ew§ 286 g Ges§ 152\. 1 n.), [39:16](https://ref.ly/Job39:16) הִקְשִׁיחַ בָּנֶיהָ לְלאֹ לָהּ useth hardly her young ones (making them) into *none of hers;* and even Hb [2:6](https://ref.ly/Hab2:6) הַמַּרְבֶּה לאֹ־לוֺ *what is not his own* (cf. [Jb 18:15](https://ref.ly/Job18:15) מִבְּלִי לוֺ). Cf. with a verb, and ellipse of אשׁר, [Is 65:1](https://ref.ly/Isa65:1) לְלאֹ שָׁאָלוּ to those who have not asked, vb [Je 2:8](https://ref.ly/Jer2:8) אַחֲרִי לאֹ יוֺעִילוּ, v:[11b](https://ref.ly/Jer2:11); also לאֹ רֻחָ֫מָה [Ho 1:6](https://ref.ly/Hos1:6), [1:8](https://ref.ly/Hos1:8), [2:25](https://ref.ly/Hos2:23), and prob. [Jb 31:31](https://ref.ly/Job31:31) לאֹ נִשְבָּע (pf. in p.) one *not satisfied.* **e.** in circ. clauses (Dr§ 164\), poet. and rare: qualifying a subst., [2 S 23:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:4) בֹּקֶר לאֹ עָבוֺת a morning *without clouds*, [Job 12:24](https://ref.ly/Job12:24) בְּתֹהוּ לאֹ דֶרֶח in a *pathless* waste, [38:26a](https://ref.ly/Job38:26); and a verb [Jb 34:24](https://ref.ly/Job34:24) יָרֹעַ כַּבִּרִים לאֹ חֵקֶר *without inquiry*, [Psalm 59:4](https://ref.ly/Ps59:4) לא פשׁעי ולא חטאתי (cf. v:[4](https://ref.ly/Ps59:4) בלי עון), in late prose, twice, [1 Ch 2:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:30), [2:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:32) וימת לא בנים (אֵין and בְּלִי, q.v., are more usual in such cases). **3\.** Once (acc. to many MSS), as a subst., [Jb 6:21](https://ref.ly/Job6:21) כִּי־עַתָּה הֱיִיתֶם לאֹ for now are ye become *nothing,* Hi De Kö (cf. [Dn 4:32](https://ref.ly/Dan4:32) (Aram.) כְּלָה חֲשִׁיבִין, Targum here כְּלָא הֲוֵיתוּן, and אַל [Jb 24:25](https://ref.ly/Job24:25)); but reading fluctuates (Orientals לאֹ, Qr לו, Westerns, Baer (v. pp. 37, 56\) לוֺ \[‘now are ye become *that,*’ viz. the נַחַל אַכְזָב of v:[15](https://ref.ly/Job24:15)]; but even לוֺ yields a forced sense; and text is prob. wrong: Mich Ew Ol Sgf Bu לִי…כֵּן (LXX SyrVer also rd. לִי); Bö Di לְאָיִן…כִּי. Cf. Koii. 1\. 236 f. **4\.** With prefixes:—**a.** † בְּלאֹ:31 (chiefly poet. or late), acc. to the varying signif. of בְּ: (*a*) usu. *with not* \= *without,* [Je 22:13](https://ref.ly/Jer22:13) בונה ביתו בל־א צדק *without* justice (‖בלא משׁפט; so Ez [22:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:29), [Pr 16:8](https://ref.ly/Prov16:8)), [Is 55:1](https://ref.ly/Isa55:1), [55:1](https://ref.ly/Isa55:1); [Pr 19:2](https://ref.ly/Prov19:2), [Jb 8:11](https://ref.ly/Job8:11) הֲיִגְאֶה־גֹּמֶא בְּלאֹ בִצָּה *without* mire ‖כלי מים, [30:28](https://ref.ly/Job30:28) בְּלאֹ חַמָּה (\=not *through* the sun), [La 1:6](https://ref.ly/Lam1:6) וילכו בלא־כח, [Nu 35:22](https://ref.ly/Num35:22) בְּלאֹ צְדִיָּה …בְּלאֹ אֵיבָה, v:[23](https://ref.ly/Num35:23) (sq. inf.) בְּלאֹ רְאוֺת, [2 Ch 21:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:20), [Ec 10:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:11); [Psalm 17:1](https://ref.ly/Ps17:1) תְּפִלָּתִי בְּלאֹ שִפְתֵי מִרְמָה; used more freely in Chr, [1 Ch 12:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:18) :בְּלאֹ חָמָס בְּכַפָּֽי, v:[34](https://ref.ly/Num35:34) בְּלאֹ לֵב וָלֵב, [2 Ch 30:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:18) בְּלאֹ כַכָּתוּב. With ellipse of rel., [La 4:14](https://ref.ly/Lam4:14) בְּלאֹ יוּכְלוּ יִגְּעוּ *without* (that) men are able to touch, etc. (*b*) of time, *in not,* i.e. *outside of,* [Lv 15:25](https://ref.ly/Lev15:25) בלא עֶת־נִדָּתָח֔, *before* [Jb 15:32](https://ref.ly/Job15:32) בלא־יומו, [Ec 7:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:17) :בְּלאֹ עִתֶּךְ. (*c*) where לאֹ belongs to the foll. word, and is only accidentally preceded by ב (v. supr. **2 d**), [Dt 32:21](https://ref.ly/Deut32:21), [32:21](https://ref.ly/Deut32:21), [Je 5:7](https://ref.ly/Jer5:7), [Pr 13:23](https://ref.ly/Prov13:23) בלא משׁפט through *injustice;* with בְּ *pretii,* [Psalm 44:13](https://ref.ly/Ps44:13), [Is 55:2](https://ref.ly/Isa55:2), [55:2](https://ref.ly/Isa55:2), [Je 2:11](https://ref.ly/Jer2:11) בְּלוֺא יוֺעִיל *for* (that which) *profiteth not.* **b.** הֲלאֹ *nonne ?* [Gn 4:7](https://ref.ly/Gen4:7) \+ oft. Inviting, as it does, an affirmative answer, it is often used, (*a*) esp. in conversation, for pointing to a fact in such a way as to arouse the interest of the person addressed, or to win his assent: [Gn 13:19](https://ref.ly/Gen13:19) *Is not* the whole land before thee ? [19:20](https://ref.ly/Gen19:20), [20:5](https://ref.ly/Gen20:5), [27:36](https://ref.ly/Gen27:36), [29:25](https://ref.ly/Gen29:25), [Ex 4:11](https://ref.ly/Exod4:11) Who maketh dumb or deaf, etc. *Do not* I ? [33:16](https://ref.ly/Exod33:16), Ju [4:6](https://ref.ly/Judg4:6), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Judg4:14), [8:2](https://ref.ly/Judg8:2), [9:28](https://ref.ly/Judg9:28), [9:38](https://ref.ly/Judg9:38), [1 S 9:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:20), [9:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:21), [15:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:17) etc.; with a vb. in 1 ps., [Jos 1:9](https://ref.ly/Josh1:9) הלא צויתיך, Ju [6:14](https://ref.ly/Judg6:14) הלא שׁלחתיך, [1 S 20:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:30), [2 S 19:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:23), [Ru 2:9](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:9): similarly in a poet. or rhet. style, Ju [5:30](https://ref.ly/Judg5:30) הלא ימצאו יחלקו שׁלל, [Is 8:19](https://ref.ly/Isa8:19), [10:8](https://ref.ly/Isa10:8), [10:9](https://ref.ly/Isa10:9), [10:11](https://ref.ly/Isa10:11), [28:25](https://ref.ly/Isa28:25), [29:17](https://ref.ly/Isa29:17), [40:21](https://ref.ly/Isa40:21), [40:23](https://ref.ly/Isa40:23), [42:24](https://ref.ly/Isa42:24), [43:19](https://ref.ly/Isa43:19) etc., [Jb 4:6](https://ref.ly/Job4:6), [4:21](https://ref.ly/Job4:21), [7:1](https://ref.ly/Job7:1), [10:10](https://ref.ly/Job10:10), [10:20](https://ref.ly/Job10:20), etc. (β) it has a tendency to become little more than an affirm. particle, declaring with some rhetor. emph. what is, or might be, well known: [Dt 3:11](https://ref.ly/Deut3:11) הֲלֽה הִיא בְרַבַּת בְּנֵי עַמּוֺן, [11:30](https://ref.ly/Deut11:30), [1 S 21:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:12) הלוא ´זה דוד וג (cf. [29:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:3), [29:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:5), [2 S 11:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:3)), [23:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:19), [26:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:1), [2 S 15:35](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:35); it is thus nearly \= הִנֵּה (LXX sts. represents it by ιδου, as [Jos 1:9](https://ref.ly/Josh1:9), Ju [6:14](https://ref.ly/Judg6:14), [Ru 2:9](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:9), [2 S 15:35](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:35)); so esp. in the phrase of the compiler of K, And the rest of the acts of…, כְּתוּבִים (הֵמָּה)הֲלאֹ הֵם *are* they *not* written in, etc. ? [1 K 11:41](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:41), [14:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:29) \+ oft. (with which there interchanges הִנָּם כְּתוּבִים [1 K 14:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:19), [2 K 15:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:11), [15:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:26), [15:31](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:31), which is gen. used by the Chr, [2 Ch 16:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:11), [20:34](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:34), etc.), [Jos 10:13](https://ref.ly/Josh10:13) (cf. [2 S 1:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:18) הִנֵּה), [1 K 8:53](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:53) LXX, [Est 10:2](https://ref.ly/Esth10:2); [Psalm 56:14](https://ref.ly/Ps56:14) (strangely: contr. [116:8](https://ref.ly/Ps116:8)).—הֲלאֹ הִנֵּה † Hb [2:13](https://ref.ly/Hab2:13), [2 Ch 25:26](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:26) (הִנָּם).—On Ju [14:15](https://ref.ly/Judg14:15), see הֲ **1** *end.* **c.** † וָ֔ל֕אֹ *and not*\=*and if not,* [2 S 13:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:26), [2 K 5:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:17). Comp. וָּיֵשׁ. **d.** † כְּלוֺא Ob:[16](https://ref.ly/Obad1:16) והיו כלוא היו, poet. for כאשׁר, *as though* they had not been. **e.** † לְלאֹ *without,* lit. *in the condition of no*…[2 Ch 15:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:3) (comp. לְאֵין, also in Chr). Elsewhere לא belongs to the foll. word, [Am 6:13](https://ref.ly/Amos6:13), [2 Ch 13:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:9), [Is 65:1](https://ref.ly/Isa65:1), [65:1](https://ref.ly/Isa65:1), [Jb 26:2](https://ref.ly/Job26:2), [26:3](https://ref.ly/Job26:3), [39:16](https://ref.ly/Job39:16) (v. supr. **2 d**). *Note.*—Fifteen times, acc. to Mas. (v. De:Psalm 100. 3 FrMM 247 StrProl. Cr. 84\), לא is written by error for לוֺ, viz. [Ex 21:8](https://ref.ly/Exod21:8), [Lv 11:21](https://ref.ly/Lev11:21), [25:30](https://ref.ly/Lev25:30), [1 S 2:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:3), [2 S 16:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:18), [2 K 8:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:10), [Is 9:2](https://ref.ly/Isa9:2), [63:9](https://ref.ly/Isa63:9), [Psalm 100:3](https://ref.ly/Ps100:3), [139:16](https://ref.ly/Ps139:16), [Jb 13:15](https://ref.ly/Job13:15), [41:4](https://ref.ly/Job41:4), [Pr 19:7](https://ref.ly/Prov19:7), [26:2](https://ref.ly/Prov26:2), [Ezr 4:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:2) (always with Qr לוֺ). The passages must be considered each upon its own merits: in some לוֺ yields a preferable sense; but this is not the case in all. There is the same קרי (rightly) on [Is 49:5](https://ref.ly/Isa49:5), [1 Ch 11:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:20); but these were not considered to rest upon equal authority, and are hence not reckoned with the fifteen.—In Ju [21:22](https://ref.ly/Judg21:22) (v. GFM), [1 S 13:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:13), [20:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:14), [20:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:14), and in [Jb 9:33](https://ref.ly/Job9:33) לאֹ יֵשׁ, rd. prob. לֻא for לאֹ. 

> \[Str3810] † לאֹ דְבָר, לוֺ דְבָר **n.pr.loc.** in Gilead, near, perh. east of, Mahanaim (q.v.);—´לאֹ ד [2 S 17:37](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:37) (LXX Λωδαβαρ, Λαδαβαρ); Gr We rd. also לאֹ דְבָר [Am 6:13](https://ref.ly/Amos6:13) \= same city (but v. Dr); \= ´לוֺ ד [2 S 9:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:4); [9:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:5) (LXX Λαδαβαρ); perh. also intended in מִמַּחֲנַיִם עַד־גְּבוּל לִדְבִר [Jos 13:26](https://ref.ly/Josh13:26) (cf. Di Bla; LXX Δαιβων, A Δαβειρ, LXXL Δεβηρ); v. II. דְּבִיר, p. 184 supr.

> \[Str3818] לאֹ עַמִּי **n.pr.m.** (*not my people:* see לאֹ **2 d**) symb. name of Hosea's son, [Ho 1:9](https://ref.ly/Hos1:9), cf. [2:25](https://ref.ly/Hos2:23) (v. also [2:1](https://ref.ly/Hos2:1); [2:3](https://ref.ly/Hos2:3)).

> \[Str3819] לאֹ רֻחָ֫מָה **n.pr.f.** (*uncompassionated:* Ges§ 152 a, N) symb. name of Hosea's daughter, [Ho 1:6](https://ref.ly/Hos1:6), [1:8](https://ref.ly/Hos1:8), cf. [2:25](https://ref.ly/Hos2:23) (v. also [2:3](https://ref.ly/Hos2:3)).

> \[Str3863] לֻא [2 S 18:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:12) Kt (Qr לוּ) if: v. לוּ

## לאב (id: BDB04737)

לאב (√ of foll., cf. Ar. لَابَ (med. و) *be thirsty,* لُوبَةٌ, لَابَةٌ *a stony tract* of land (v. WetzstReisebericht 99\)).

> \[Str8514] † \[תַּלְאֻבָה] **n.f.** drought;—only pl. intens., בַּמִּדְבָּר בְּאֶרֶץ תַּלְאֻבוֺת [Ho 13:5](https://ref.ly/Hos13:5).

* **Associated Passages:** Hosea 13:5


## לָאָה (id: BDB04739)

\[Str3811] † \[לָאָה] **vb.** be weary, impatient (cf. Ar. لَأَى *be slow, hesitating* (Frey); مَلْأَنٌ *after difficulty,* لَأَكَ: *with difficulty* (Lane3007); Aram. לְאִי, بَعْدَ لَأْىٍ, لَأْيًا);—**Qal** *Impf.* 2 ms. תִּלְאֶה [Jb 4:2](https://ref.ly/Job4:2); וַתֵָּלֶא [Jb 4:5](https://ref.ly/Job4:5); 3 pl. וַיִּלְאוּ [Gn 19:11](https://ref.ly/Gen19:11); —*be weary* (in vain endeavour) sq. Inf. [Gn 19:11](https://ref.ly/Gen19:11); *be weary, impatient* at attempted consolation abs. [Jb 4:2](https://ref.ly/Job4:2); at calamity abs. [Jb 4:5](https://ref.ly/Job4:5) (‖בָּהַל). **Niph.** *Pf.* נִלְאָה [Pr 26:15](https://ref.ly/Prov26:15) \+ 2 t.; 2 fs. נִלְאֵית [Is 47:13](https://ref.ly/Isa47:13); נִלְאֵיתִי [Is 1:14](https://ref.ly/Isa1:14) \+ 3 t., etc.;—sq. inf. *be weary* of doing a thing, [Ex 7:18](https://ref.ly/Exod7:18) (JE), *make oneself weary* in doing something [Pr 26:15](https://ref.ly/Prov26:15) (of sluggard's laziness), *weary* (i.e. strenuously exert) *oneself* to [Je 9:4](https://ref.ly/Jer9:4), [20:9](https://ref.ly/Jer20:9); abs. of eager and hopeless prayer על־הַבָּמָה ´נִל Moab, [Is 16:12](https://ref.ly/Isa16:12) (‖נִרְאָה, בָּא אֶל־מִקְדָּשׁוֺ לְהִתְפַּלֵּל); of vain consultations, (sq. בְּ) Babylon [Is 47:13](https://ref.ly/Isa47:13); subj. ´י, sq. inf. [Is 1:14](https://ref.ly/Isa1:14); [Je 6:11](https://ref.ly/Jer6:11), [15:6](https://ref.ly/Jer15:6), always of exhausted patience; fig. of parched soil (abs.) [Psalm 68:10](https://ref.ly/Ps68:10). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. הֶלְאָנִי [Jb 16:7](https://ref.ly/Job16:7); 3 fs. הֶלְאָת Ez [24:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:12) (del. LXX Co), 1 s. sf. הֶלְאֵתִיְ [Mi 6:3](https://ref.ly/Mic6:3); *Impf.* וַיַּלְאוְּ [Je 12:5](https://ref.ly/Jer12:5); תַּלְאוּ [Is 7:13](https://ref.ly/Isa7:13); *Inf.cstr.* הַלְאוֺת [Is 7:13](https://ref.ly/Isa7:13); —*weary, make weary, exhaust,* sq. acc. [Jb 16:7](https://ref.ly/Job16:7) (subj. God); *exhaust patience of,* obj. God [Is 7:13](https://ref.ly/Isa7:13); obj. men [Is 7:13](https://ref.ly/Isa7:13), [Je 12:5](https://ref.ly/Jer12:5), [Mi 6:3](https://ref.ly/Mic6:3).

521:a. \[לָאָה] end: transpose ‘[Mi 6:3](https://ref.ly/Mic6:3)’ to follow ‘[Je 12:5](https://ref.ly/Jer12:5).’

> \[Str8513] † תְּלָאָה **n.f.** weariness, hardship;—´ת [Ex 18:8](https://ref.ly/Exod18:8) \+ 4 t.; of distress of Isr. in Egypt [Nu 20:14](https://ref.ly/Num20:14); in wilderness [Ex 18:8](https://ref.ly/Exod18:8) (both JE); from Assyr., Bab., etc. [Ne 9:32](https://ref.ly/Neh9:32); cf. [La 3:5](https://ref.ly/Lam3:5) (‖ראֹשׁ, gall); [Mal 1:13](https://ref.ly/Mal1:13).

> \[Str3812] לֵאָה:34 **n.pr.f.** Leah, elder daughter of Laban, and wife of Jacob (perh. \= *wild\-cow,* Ar. ܠܠܐ, (ܠܠܝ) \[cf. רָחֵל *ewe*], DlProl. 80 RSK 219 and (doubtfully) NöZMG xl. 1886, 167; also GrayHeb. Names, 96; others as HptGGN 1883, 100 comp. As. *li’at,* in sense *mistress;* on poss. relation of לֵאָה to לִוֵי v. לִוֵי);—mother of Reuben, Simeon, Levi, Judah, Issachar, Zebulun and Dinah; [Gn 29:16](https://ref.ly/Gen29:16), [29:17](https://ref.ly/Gen29:17), [29:23](https://ref.ly/Gen29:23), [29:24](https://ref.ly/Gen29:24), [29:25](https://ref.ly/Gen29:25), [29:30](https://ref.ly/Gen29:30), [29:31](https://ref.ly/Gen29:31), [29:32](https://ref.ly/Gen29:32) (cf. vv:[33](https://ref.ly/Gen29:33), vv:[34](https://ref.ly/Gen29:34), vv:[35](https://ref.ly/Gen29:35)) [30:9](https://ref.ly/Gen30:9) \+ 15 t. Gn 31, 32, [33:1](https://ref.ly/Gen33:1), [33:2](https://ref.ly/Gen33:2), [33:7](https://ref.ly/Gen33:7), [34:1](https://ref.ly/Gen34:1), [35:23](https://ref.ly/Gen35:23), [35:26](https://ref.ly/Gen35:26), [46:15](https://ref.ly/Gen46:15), [46:18](https://ref.ly/Gen46:18), [49:31](https://ref.ly/Gen49:31), [Ru 4:11](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:11); LXX Λ(ε)ια.

## לָאַט (id: BDB04742)

\[Str3813] † לָאַט **vb.** cover, only **Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. אֶת־פָּנָיו ´וְהַמֶּלֶח ל [2 S 19:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:5), rd. perh. לָאט, fr. לוּט We Dr Bu.

> \[Str3814] לָאט Ju [4:21](https://ref.ly/Judg4:21), etc., v. לוּט

> \[Str328] לְאַט לָאַט *gently,* v. אַט sub אטט supr.

## לאך (id: BDB04745)

לאך (√ of מַלְאָח מְלָאכָה, cf. Ar. لَأَي, لَأَاةٌ *send,* لَأَكَ *messenger,* Eth. أَلْأَكَ *send a messenger,* v. ተልእከ፡ *be sent, wait on, minister;* መልአክ፡ \= Heb. מַלְאָח; cf. Ph. מלאך *messenger;* NH מַלְאָח Aram. מַלְאֲכָא, as in Heb.)

> \[Str4397] מַלְאָךְ:214 **n.m.** messenger;—´מ [Ex 23:20](https://ref.ly/Exod23:20) \+ 48 t.; cstr. מַלְאַךְ [Gn 16:7](https://ref.ly/Gen16:7) \+ 69 t.; sfs. מַלְאָכִי [Ex 23:23](https://ref.ly/Exod23:23) \+ 3 t.; מַלְאָכוֺ [Gn 24:7](https://ref.ly/Gen24:7), [24:40](https://ref.ly/Gen24:40); pl. מַלְאָכִים [Gn 32:4](https://ref.ly/Gen32:4) \+ 63 t. \+ מלאכים [2 S 11:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:1) Kt ( Qr מלכים); cstr. מַלְאֲכֵי [Gn 28:12](https://ref.ly/Gen28:12) \+ 11 t.; מַלְאָכֶיְ [Nu 24:12](https://ref.ly/Num24:12), [2 K 19:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:23); מַלְאָכֵכֵה [Na 2:14](https://ref.ly/Nah2:14) error for מַלְאָכֵכִי \= מַלְאָכַיְכִי Ges§ 91, (2\) Anm. 2 or \< for מַלְאָכֵךְ by dittogr. fr. foll. הוֺי Köii. 1\. 571\). מַלְאָכָיו [2 Ch 36:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:15) \+ 8 t.;—**1\.** *messenger,* **a.** one sent with a message [Gn 32:4](https://ref.ly/Gen32:4) \+ 8 t. JE; [Dt 2:26](https://ref.ly/Deut2:26), (not in P); Ju [6:35](https://ref.ly/Judg6:35) \+ 8 t.; [1 S 6:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:21) \+ 51 t. S K, [1 Ch 14:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr14:1), [19:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:2), [19:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:16), [2 Ch 18:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:12), [35:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:21), [Ne 6:3](https://ref.ly/Neh6:3), [Jb 1:14](https://ref.ly/Job1:14), [Pr 13:17](https://ref.ly/Prov13:17), [17:11](https://ref.ly/Prov17:11), [Is 14:32](https://ref.ly/Isa14:32), [18:2](https://ref.ly/Isa18:2), [30:4](https://ref.ly/Isa30:4), [37:9](https://ref.ly/Isa37:9), [37:14](https://ref.ly/Isa37:14), [Je 27:3](https://ref.ly/Jer27:3), Ez [17:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:15), [23:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:16), [23:40](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:40), [30:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:9), [Na 2:14](https://ref.ly/Nah2:14); מלאכי שׁלום, messengers of peace [Is 33:7](https://ref.ly/Isa33:7). **b.** a prophet [Is 42:19](https://ref.ly/Isa42:19), [44:26](https://ref.ly/Isa44:26), [2 Ch 36:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:15), [36:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:16), [Hg 1:13](https://ref.ly/Hag1:13); the herald of the advent מלאכי [Mal 3:1](https://ref.ly/Mal3:1). **c.** priest [Mal 2:7](https://ref.ly/Mal2:7), prob. [Ec 5:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:5) (RV *angel*). **d.** ‖מֵלִיץ [Jb 33:23](https://ref.ly/Job33:23), a messenger from God acting as an interpreter and declaring what is right (*angel* of RV too specific). **e.** fig. רָעִים ´מ *messengers of evil* [Psalm. 78:49](https://ref.ly/Ps78:49); מות ´מ *messenger of death* [Pr 16:14](https://ref.ly/Prov16:14); ‖רוחות *winds his messengers* [Psalm. 104:4](https://ref.ly/Ps104:4). **2\.** *angel,* as messenger of God, מלאכים with God in theophanies [Gn 19:1](https://ref.ly/Gen19:1), [19:15](https://ref.ly/Gen19:15), [28:12](https://ref.ly/Gen28:12), [32:2](https://ref.ly/Gen32:2) (JE), praising him; [Psalm 103:20](https://ref.ly/Ps103:20), [148:2](https://ref.ly/Ps148:2); in his sight not without error [Jb 4:18](https://ref.ly/Job4:18) charged with the care of the pious [Psalm 91:11](https://ref.ly/Ps91:11); [Is 5:29](https://ref.ly/Isa5:29) (שָֽׁאֲגָה in sim.; ‖כְּפִירִים); fig. of wicked [Jb 4:11](https://ref.ly/Job4:11) (\+ אַרְיֵה, שָׁחַל, כְּפִיר, לַיִשׁ v:[10](https://ref.ly/Job4:10); v:[11](https://ref.ly/Job4:11)); sim. of ´י, devouring (אָכַל) in judgment [Ho 13:8](https://ref.ly/Hos13:8); fig. of locusts ´מְתַלְּעוֺת ל [Jo 1:6](https://ref.ly/Joel1:6) (‖שִׁנֵּי אַרְיֵה).

> \[Str3822] † לְבָאוֺת **n.pr.loc.** a city in S. Judah, [Jos 15:32](https://ref.ly/Josh15:32), LXX Δαβως, ALXXL Δαβωθ \= ´בֵּית ל (q. v. p. III supr.) [Jos 19:6](https://ref.ly/Josh19:6), LXX Βαθαρωθ, LXXL Βηθλεβαωθ. Site unknown.

## לבב (id: BDB04760)

† לבב (mng. dub.; DlPr. 88 ff. finds orig. mng. in As. *labâbu, in unruhiger Bewegung sein;*—√ of לֵבָב, לֵב; cf. NH *id.,* As. *libbu,* Aram. לִבָּא ܠܶܒܳܐ Eth. ልብ፡ Ar. لُبٌّ Sab. לב, Sab. Denkm.:p. 13, No. 1, 1\. 7\);—the literary usage of לֵב and לֵבָב is: **1\.** earliest poetry, J and E chiefly, Eph doc. of Ju S K chiefly, Am Ho Zc 9–11 Is 15 use לֵב. **2\.** לֵבָב first appears in Is and certain strata of E and Eph doc. of Ju S K, and is continued in Zp Na (prob.) D H Dt editors and some Psalm Psalm **3\.** Je Ez Jb prefer לֵב but use occasionally לֵבָב. **4\.** Is:2; Is:3 La and exilic Psalm Psalm Use לֵב. **5\.** [Is 13–14:23](https://ref.ly/Isa13:1-Isa14:23), Je 50–51, Hg Zc 1–8, Jo Jon Psalm 25, 90, 104, use לבב. **6\.** Mal Ob Zc 12–14 Memorials of Ezr and Ne Pr Ru Ct and many Psalm Psalm of Persian period use לֵב. **7\.** Chr and Dn use לֵבָב. **8\.** Ec Est and latest Psalm Psalm use לֵב. Exceptions will be noted and suspected passages indicated by ? under לבב and לב which are treated apart. See Br. 'Study of the use of לב and לבב in the O.T.' in *semitic Studies in Memory of Dr. Kohut,* Berlin, 1897\.

523:a. לֵבָב, 1\. 7: bef. לִבְבֵהֶן ins. ‘pl. c. sf.’

עד לבב השׁמים *unto the midst of heaven* [Dt 4:11](https://ref.ly/Deut4:11) (so Sam but MT לב). **II.** usu. *of men:* **1\.** *the inner man* in contrast with the outer, כלה שׁארי ולבבי *my flesh and my heart* (*soul*) *doth fail* [Psalm 73:26](https://ref.ly/Ps73:26); > \[Str3826,3820] † \[לִבָּה] *sf.* לִבָּתֵח Ez [16:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:30) should be corrected to לִבְרִיתֵךְ (see Co). \> older view, as fem. of לֵבּ.

> \[Str6965,8347] לֵב קָמָי prob. \`late Atbash (cf. שֵׁשַׁךְ) for p. 1058 כשדים the orginal reading (LXX) [Je 51:1](https://ref.ly/Jer51:1).

> \[Str3823] † I. \[לבב] **vb. denom. Niph.** *Impf.* יִלָּבֵב get a mind; וְאִישׁ נָבוּב יִלָּבֵב וְעַיִר פֶּרֶא אָדָם יִוָּלֵד *shall an empty man get a mind or a wild ass's colt be born a man* (?) [Jb 11:12](https://ref.ly/Job11:12). **Pi.** encourage; *Pf.* לִבַּבְתִּנִי בְּאַחַד֯ מֵעֵינַיִךְ .…לִבַּבִתִּנִי Ct [4:9](https://ref.ly/Song4:9), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Song4:9) *thou hast encouraged me, thou hast encouraged me with one of thine eyes* Ew Gi Gr RV m (AV RV Ges Hi De Öt (cf. [5:6](https://ref.ly/Song5:6)) *ravished my heart,*—Pi. *priv*. Ges§ 52 h).

> \[Str3834] † \[לְבִבָה] **n.pl.** cakes (prob. *pancakes,* from shape ?) לְבִבוֺת [2 S 13:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:6), [13:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:8), [13:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:10).

> \[Str3823] † II. \[לבב] **vb. denom. Pi. make cakes.** *Impf.* תְּלַבֵּב [2 S 13:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:6), [13:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:8).

## לָבַט (id: BDB04769)

\[Str3832] \[לָבַט] **vb.** thrust down, out, or away (NH *id.;* Ar. لَبَطَ *strike the ground* with a person, i.e. throw one down; Syr. Pa. ܠܰܒܶܛ *incitavit, stimulavit*);—only **Niph.** *Impf. be thrust down, away,* i.e. ruined; עָם לאֹ יָבִין יִלָּבֵט [Ho 4:14](https://ref.ly/Hos4:14); אֱוִיל שְפָתַיִם יִלָּבֵט [Pr 10:8](https://ref.ly/Prov10:8), [10:10](https://ref.ly/Prov10:10).

> \[Str3833] לְבִי, לָבִיא לְבִיָּא v. sub לבא. p. 522

> \[Str3864] לֻבִים v. לוּבִים. p. 530

* **Associated Passages:** Proverbs 10:10; Hosea 4:14; Proverbs 10:8


## לָבֵן (id: BDB04772)

\[Str3835] † \[לָבֵן] **vb.** be white (on —ֵ v. BaNB 166; LagBN 33, cf. 53, 54 infers …לָבֹן from לְבֹנָה; NH *id.,* Pi. Hiph., and deriv.; Ph. לבן *white;* Ar. لَبَنٌ *milk;* لبن also *be white,* dial. of Yemen, MaltzanZMG xxvii, 1873, 247; appar. √ of foll. fourteen words, but this dub. esp. in case of II. III. לָבָן, לְבָנָא, לִבְנָה, II. לְבוֺנָה, I. II. לִבְנִי);—**Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. הִלְבִּינוּ [Jo 1:7](https://ref.ly/Joel1:7); *Impf.* 1 s. אַלְבִּין [Psalm 51:9](https://ref.ly/Ps51:9); 3 pl. יַלְבִּינוּ [Is 1:18](https://ref.ly/Isa1:18); *Inf. cstr.* וְלַלְבֵּן (\= וּלְהַלְבֵּן) [Dn 11:35](https://ref.ly/Dan11:35); —**1\.** *make white* \= purify (ethical) [Dn 11:35](https://ref.ly/Dan11:35) (no object expr., ‖צָרַף, בָּרֵר). **2\.** *shew whiteness, grow white,* of fig\-tree, stripped by locusts, [Jo 1:7](https://ref.ly/Joel1:7); fig. of moral purity, ´כַּשֶּׁלֶג יַלְב [Is 1:18](https://ref.ly/Isa1:18); ´מִשֶּׁלֶג אַלְב [Psalm 51:9](https://ref.ly/Ps51:9). **Hithp.** *Impf.* יִתְלַבְּנוּ [Dn 12:10](https://ref.ly/Dan12:10) *be purified* (ethical, ‖יִתְבָּֽרֲרוּ).—לָבַן, *make brick,* v. infr.

SyrVer and LöwNo.107\);—[Gn 30:37](https://ref.ly/Gen30:37) לַח וְלוּז וְעַרְמוֺן ´מַקַּל ל as marking places of idolatrous incense\-burning תַּחַת אַלּוֺן וְלִבְנֶה וְּאֵלָה [Ho 4:13](https://ref.ly/Hos4:13).

> 527:a. לבְנֶה: named fr. exuding milky (white) gum De Ges al.

> \[Str3843,3840] †לְבֵנָה **n.f.** brick, tile (NH *id.;* Aram. לְּבִינְתָּא, ܠܒܶܢܻܬܳܐ; Zinj. לבן (?) DHMSendsch.37\. 59; As. *libittu;* Ar. لِبْنٌ, لَبِنٌ, لَبِنٌ, لِبْنَةٌ, لَبِنَةٌ (loan\-words acc. to Frä4f.); acc. to Thes and most from *whiteness* of clay, or light colour of sun\-baked bricks; so NöZMG xi,1886,735 LagBN 139; \> others regard as As. loan\-word VOJi. 22ff.; in As. a deriv. fr. *labânu, throw down, prostrate,* is sought DlPr. 93f.(cf.HWB 369\));—´ל [Gn 11:3](https://ref.ly/Gen11:3) Ez [4:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:1); cstr. לִבְנַת [Ex 24:10](https://ref.ly/Exod24:10); pl. לְבֵנִים Gn [11:3](https://ref.ly/Gen11:3) \+ 7 t.; sf. מִלִּבְנֵיכֶם [Ex 5:19](https://ref.ly/Exod5:19); —**1\.** brick, as building\-material, [Gn 11:3](https://ref.ly/Gen11:3) (sg. coll.; elsewh. pl.) v:[3](https://ref.ly/Gen11:3); [Ex 1:14](https://ref.ly/Exod1:14); straw used in making [Ex 5:7](https://ref.ly/Exod5:7), cf. v:[8](https://ref.ly/Exod5:8), v:[16](https://ref.ly/Exod5:16), v:[18](https://ref.ly/Exod5:18), v:[19](https://ref.ly/Exod5:19), [Is 9:9](https://ref.ly/Isa9:9) (fig.; as inferior to גָּזִית *hewn stone*); incense burnt on [Is 65:3](https://ref.ly/Isa65:3) ( \= roof\-tiles ? cf. Che Di). **2\.** \= *tile*, on which plan of city could be engraved (חקק) Ez [4:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:1). **3\.** \= *pavement*, [Ex 24:10](https://ref.ly/Exod24:10) (cf. Di).

> \[Str3835] †\[לָבַן] **vb.denom.** make brick (as As. *labânu* fr.*libittu* DlHWB 370\);—**Qal** *Impf.* 1 pl. נִלְבְּנָה [Gn 11:3](https://ref.ly/Gen11:3) (c. acc. cogn.); *Inf. cstr.* לִלְבֹּן [Ex 5:7](https://ref.ly/Exod5:7) (c. acc. cogn.), v:[14](https://ref.ly/Exod5:14) (abs.)

> \[Str4404] †מַלְבֵּן **n.\[m.]** **1\.** brick\-mould; **2\.** quadrangle (on meaning  *brick\-mould*, and fig. something *rectangular*, v. HoffmZAW 1882, 53–72 Dr2 S 1231, cf. NH מַלְבֵּן; Ar. مِلْبَنٌ; Syr. ܡܰܠܒܢܳܐ);—**1\.** *brick\-mould*, [2 S 12:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:31) Qr (Kt, by error, מלכן), [Na 3:14](https://ref.ly/Nah3:14). **2\.** *quadrangle*, [Je 43:9](https://ref.ly/Jer43:9) (at Tahpanhes).

> \[Str4192] לַבֵּן in ´עַל־מוּת ל [Psalm 9:1](https://ref.ly/Ps9:1) of dubious meaning, v. Thes BaePsalmen, p.xvii.

> \[Str7884] לִבְנָ֑ת, v. שִׁיחוֺר לִבְנָ֑ת. p. 1009

## לָבֵשׁ (id: BDB04790)

\[Str3847] †לָבֵשׁ,לָבַשׁ **vb.** put on (a garment), wear, clothe, be clothed (NH *id.;* Aram. לָבֵישׁ, ܠܒܶܫ; As. *labâšu;* Ar.لَبِسَ ; Eth. ለብሰ፡);—**Qal** Pf. 3 ms. לָבֵשׁ [Psalm 93:1](https://ref.ly/Ps93:1), [93:1](https://ref.ly/Ps93:1), לָבַשׁ [Jb 7:5](https://ref.ly/Job7:5) \+ 2 t., וְלָבַשׁ (consec.) [Lv 6:3](https://ref.ly/Lev6:3) \+ 3 t.; sf. וּלְבָשֵׁם (consec.) [Lv 16:4](https://ref.ly/Lev16:4); 3 fs. לָֽבְשָׁה Ju [6:34](https://ref.ly/Judg6:34) \+ 2 t., etc. (Ez [42:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:14) rd. prob. וְלָֽבְשׁוּ Qr, for ילבשׁו Kt, but cl. perhaps interpol., v. Co); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִלְבַּשׁ [Dt 22:5](https://ref.ly/Deut22:5) \+; sf. וַיִּלְבָּשֵׁנִי [Jb 29:14](https://ref.ly/Job29:14) יִלְבָּשָׁם [Ex 29:30](https://ref.ly/Exod29:30); 1 s. sf. אֶלְבָּשֶָׁנָּה Ct [5:3](https://ref.ly/Song5:3); 3 fpl. תִּלְבַּשְׁןָ [2 S 13:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:18), etc.; *Imv.* ms. לְבַשׁ [1 K 22:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:30), etc.;*Inf. abs.* לָבוֺשׁ [Hg 1:6](https://ref.ly/Hag1:6); *cstr.* לִלְבּשׁ [Gn 28:20](https://ref.ly/Gen28:20), [Lv 21:10](https://ref.ly/Lev21:10);  *Pt. act.* pl. הַלּֽבְשִׁים [Zp 1:8](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:8); *pass.* abs. לָבוּשׁ [1 S 17:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:5), [Dn 10:5](https://ref.ly/Dan10:5); לָבֻשׁ Ez [9:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:2) \+ 3 t.; cstr. לְבוּשׁ [Dn 12:6](https://ref.ly/Dan12:6), [12:7](https://ref.ly/Dan12:7), לְבֻשׁ Ez [9:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:11) \+ 3 t.; pl. cstr. לְבֻשֵׁי Ez [23:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:6) \+ 2 t.;—**1\. a.** lit. *put on* (one's own) garment (acc.) [Gn 38:19](https://ref.ly/Gen38:19), [1 S 28:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:8), [2 S 14:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:2), [1 K 22:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:30) \= [2 Ch 18:29](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:29), [Ex 29:30](https://ref.ly/Exod29:30), [Lv 6:3](https://ref.ly/Lev6:3), [6:4](https://ref.ly/Lev6:4), [16:4](https://ref.ly/Lev16:4), [16:4](https://ref.ly/Lev16:4), [16:23](https://ref.ly/Lev16:23), [16:24](https://ref.ly/Lev16:24), [16:32](https://ref.ly/Lev16:32), [21:10](https://ref.ly/Lev21:10), [Dt 22:5](https://ref.ly/Deut22:5), Ez [44:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:17), [44:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:19), and so [42:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:14) Qr (v. supr.), [Jon 3:5](https://ref.ly/Jonah3:5), Ct [5:3](https://ref.ly/Song5:3), [Est 4:1](https://ref.ly/Esth4:1), [5:1](https://ref.ly/Esth5:1); c. acc. garment \+ עַל־בְּשָרוֺ [Lv 16:3](https://ref.ly/Lev16:3); of putting on armour [Je 46:4](https://ref.ly/Jer46:4); \= *wear* (more or less habitually), c. acc. of garment, [Is 4:1](https://ref.ly/Isa4:1), [Dt 22:11](https://ref.ly/Deut22:11), [Zp 1:8](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:8), [Zc 13:4](https://ref.ly/Zech13:4); of Jerus. under fig. of woman [Je 4:30](https://ref.ly/Jer4:30), of rulers under fig. of shepherds Ez [34:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:3), cf. כֵּן תִּלְבַּשְׁןָ [2 S 13:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:18) (no acc.) **b.** very oft. fig., *put on, be clothed with*, c. acc. of garment;—לָבַשׁ בְּשָרִי רִמָּה [Jb 7:5](https://ref.ly/Job7:5) *my flesh is clothed* (i.e. covered) *with worms;* of Jerus., *be clothed with* inhabitants [Is 49:18](https://ref.ly/Isa49:18); of pasture, with flocks [Psalm 65:14](https://ref.ly/Ps65:14); more oft. the garment is some abstract quality, e.g. righteousness, majesty, beauty, strength, etc.;—וַיִּלְבַּשׁ צְדָקָה כַּשִּׁרְיָן [Is 59:17](https://ref.ly/Isa59:17) he (´י) *hath put on righteousness as a breastplate*, cf. v:[17](https://ref.ly/Isa59:17) (vengeance), [Psalm 93:1](https://ref.ly/Ps93:1) (majesty), v:[1](https://ref.ly/Ps93:1) (strength), [104:1](https://ref.ly/Ps104:1) (honour and majesty), [Is 51:9](https://ref.ly/Isa51:9) (strength); also [Jb 40:10](https://ref.ly/Job40:10); said of men, [2 Ch 6:41](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:41) (salvation), [Psalm 132:9](https://ref.ly/Ps132:9) (righteousness), [Jb 29:14](https://ref.ly/Job29:14) (*id.*); of Zion [Is 52:1](https://ref.ly/Isa52:1), [52:1](https://ref.ly/Isa52:1); also in bad sense, clothed with terror, trembling, shame, etc., Ez [7:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:27), [26:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:16), [Jb 8:22](https://ref.ly/Job8:22), [Psalm 35:26](https://ref.ly/Ps35:26), [109:29](https://ref.ly/Ps109:29), cf. v:[18](https://ref.ly/Ps109:18) (cursing). **c.** lit. *put on*, obj. om., בֶּגֶד לִלְבּשׁ…נָתַן [Gn 28:20](https://ref.ly/Gen28:20), [Jb 27:17](https://ref.ly/Job27:17); *Inf. abs.* לָבוֺשׁ [Hg 1:6](https://ref.ly/Hag1:6) (there is) *a clothing*, but he has nothing for warmth. **d.** once c. בְּ,אֲשֶׁר לָבַשׁ בּוֺ…לְבוּשׁ [Est 6:8](https://ref.ly/Esth6:8). **e.** *Pt. pass., clothed with*, chiefly Ezek.;—c. acc. of garment, Ez [9:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:2), [9:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:3), [Zc 3:3](https://ref.ly/Zech3:3), [Pr 31:21](https://ref.ly/Prov31:21), [Dn 10:5](https://ref.ly/Dan10:5); of wearing armour [1 S 17:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:5); cstr. before garment, לְבֻשׁ הַבַּדִּים Ez [9:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:11), [10:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:2), [10:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:6), [10:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:7), cf. [23:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:6), [23:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:12), [38:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:4), [Dn 12:6](https://ref.ly/Dan12:6), [12:7](https://ref.ly/Dan12:7). **f.** fig.:—לָֽבְשָׁה אֶת־גִּדְעָוֺן ´וְרוּחַ י Ju [6:34](https://ref.ly/Judg6:34) *and the spirit of* ´י *clothed itself with Gideon*, i.e. (GFM) took possession of him (cf. ܠܒܺܝܫ *possessed*); so [1 Ch 12:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:18), [2 Ch 24:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:20); צֶדֶק לָבַשְׁתִּי וַיִּלְבָּשֵׁנִי [Jb 29:14](https://ref.ly/Job29:14) and it *clothed itself* in me, as it were, became incarnate in me. **Pu.** only *Pt.* pl. מְלֻבָּשִׁים *arrayed*, c. acc. of garment [1 K 22:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:10) \= [2 Ch 18:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:9), [2 Ch 5:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:12); abs. *in full apparel* [Ezr 3:10](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:10). **Hiph.** *Pf*. 3 ms. sf. הִלְבִּישַׁנִי [Is 61:10](https://ref.ly/Isa61:10); 3 fs. הִלְבִּישָׁה [Gn 27:16](https://ref.ly/Gen27:16); 2ms. וְהִלְבַּשְׁתָּ֫ [Ex 28:41](https://ref.ly/Exod28:41) \+ 3 t.; sf. וְהִלְבַּשְׁתָּם (consec.) [Ex 29:8](https://ref.ly/Exod29:8), [Nu 20:26](https://ref.ly/Num20:26); 1 s. sf. וְהִלְבַּשְׁתִּיו [Is 22:21](https://ref.ly/Isa22:21); 3 pl. הִלְבִּישׁוּ [2 Ch 28:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:15), וְהִלְבִּשׁוּ (consec.) [Est 6:9](https://ref.ly/Esth6:9); *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיַּלְבֵּשׁ [Gn 41:42](https://ref.ly/Gen41:42) \+ 5 t.; sf. וַיַּלְבִּשֵׁם [Gn 3:21](https://ref.ly/Gen3:21), [Lv 8:13](https://ref.ly/Lev8:13); 3 fs. תַּלְבִּישׁ [Pr 23:21](https://ref.ly/Prov23:21), etc.; *Inf. abs.* הַלְבֵּשׁ [Zc 3:4](https://ref.ly/Zech3:4); cstr. הַלְבִּישׁ [Est 4:4](https://ref.ly/Esth4:4); *Pt.* sf. הַמַּלְבִּֽשְׁכֶם [1 S 1:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:24); —*clothe, array with*. **1\.** usu. c. 2 acc., **a.** lit. וַיּלְבֵּשׁ אֹתוֺ בִּגְדֵי־שֵׁשׁ [Gn 41:42](https://ref.ly/Gen41:42), [2 S 1:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:24), [Is 22:21](https://ref.ly/Isa22:21), [Ex 28:41](https://ref.ly/Exod28:41), [29:5](https://ref.ly/Exod29:5), [29:8](https://ref.ly/Exod29:8), [40:13](https://ref.ly/Exod40:13), [40:14](https://ref.ly/Exod40:14), [Lv 8:7](https://ref.ly/Lev8:7), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Lev8:13), [Nu 20:26](https://ref.ly/Num20:26), [20:28](https://ref.ly/Num20:28), [Zc 3:4](https://ref.ly/Zech3:4), [3:5](https://ref.ly/Zech3:5); of clothing Jerus. under fig. of infant Ez [16:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:10); of putting armour on some one [1 S 17:38](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:38), [17:38](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:38). **b.** fig., [Jb 10:11](https://ref.ly/Job10:11) *thou hast clothed me with skin and flesh;* cf. [39:19](https://ref.ly/Job39:19) (quivering of horse's neck); [Is 50:3](https://ref.ly/Isa50:3), [61:10](https://ref.ly/Isa61:10), [Psalm 132:16](https://ref.ly/Ps132:16), [132:18](https://ref.ly/Ps132:18). **2\.** c. acc. garment only, [Gn 27:16](https://ref.ly/Gen27:16) ( \+ עַל־יָדָיו); fig., [Pr 23:21](https://ref.ly/Prov23:21). **3\.** c. acc. Pers. only, [Gn 3:21](https://ref.ly/Gen3:21), [27:15](https://ref.ly/Gen27:15), [2 Ch 28:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:15), [28:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:15), [Est 4:4](https://ref.ly/Esth4:4), [6:9](https://ref.ly/Esth6:9), [6:11](https://ref.ly/Esth6:11).

> \[Str3830] †לְבוּשׁ,לְבֻשׁ **n.m.**:[Jb 30:18](https://ref.ly/Job30:18) garment, clothing, raiment (on format. cf. Ges§ 84a. 12 LagBN 64:179\);—לְבוּשׁ abs. [2 K 10:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:22) \+ 9 t.; cstr. לְבוּשׁ [Est 4:2](https://ref.ly/Esth4:2) \+ 2 t., לְבֻשׁ [Is 14:19](https://ref.ly/Isa14:19); sf. לְבוּשִׁי [Jb 30:18](https://ref.ly/Job30:18) \+ 3 t.; לְבוּשְֶָׁ [Is 63:2](https://ref.ly/Isa63:2), [Pr 27:26](https://ref.ly/Prov27:26); לְבוּשׁוֺ [Is 63:1](https://ref.ly/Isa63:1), [Jb 41:5](https://ref.ly/Job41:5), לְבֻשׁוֺ [2 S 20:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:8) \+ 2 t.; לְבוּשָׁהּ [Psalm 45:14](https://ref.ly/Ps45:14) \+ 2 t.; לְבוּשְׁכֶן [2 S 1:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:24); לְבוּשָׁם [Je 10:9](https://ref.ly/Jer10:9); pl. sf. לְבֻשֵׁיהֶם [La 4:14](https://ref.ly/Lam4:14); —*garment, clothing*, [Gn 49:11](https://ref.ly/Gen49:11) (poem in J; ‖סוּתֹה), [Jb 24:7](https://ref.ly/Job24:7) (‖כְּסוּת), v:[10](https://ref.ly/Job24:10), [30:18](https://ref.ly/Job30:18), [31:19](https://ref.ly/Job31:19), [Pr 27:26](https://ref.ly/Prov27:26), [Psalm 22:19](https://ref.ly/Ps22:19), [La 4:14](https://ref.ly/Lam4:14); of women's raiment [2 S 1:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:24), [Pr 31:22](https://ref.ly/Prov31:22), also fig. וְהָדָר לְבוּשָׁהּ עֹז v:[25](https://ref.ly/Prov31:25); specif. of princess [Psalm 45:14](https://ref.ly/Ps45:14); of warrior's tunic [2 S 20:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:8), cf. [Is 63:1](https://ref.ly/Isa63:1), [63:2](https://ref.ly/Isa63:2) (´י as warrior); raiment for worshippers [2 K 10:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:22); for mourners, לְבוּשִׁי שָק [Psalm 35:13](https://ref.ly/Ps35:13), cf. [69:12](https://ref.ly/Ps69:12); בִּלְבוּשׁ שָק [Est 4:2](https://ref.ly/Esth4:2); of royal apparel מַלְכוּת ´ל [Est 6:8](https://ref.ly/Esth6:8), [8:15](https://ref.ly/Esth8:15), cf. ## להב (id: BDB04799)

להב √ of foll. (cf. Ar. لَهِبَ be *thirsty* \[prob. *burn with thirst* Lane2674], II. *make* a fire *blaze fiercely,* cf. IV; v. *blaze fiercely* (of fire), cf. VIII; Aram. \[להב, ܠܗܒ] *burn*, in Shaph. Ishtaph. and deriv.; also לַהֲבָא, לַהֲבוּתָא *flame;* eth. ለሀበ፡; As. *la‘;abu, flame*, DlHWB 364\).

Ju [13:20](https://ref.ly/Judg13:20) \+ 5 t.; pl. לְהָבִים [Is 13:8](https://ref.ly/Isa13:8); cstr. לַהֲבֵי [Is 66:15](https://ref.ly/Isa66:15); —**1\.** *flame* of altar, Ju [13:20](https://ref.ly/Judg13:20), [13:20](https://ref.ly/Judg13:20) (הַמִּזְבֵּחַ ´ל)) אֵשׁ אוֺכְלָה ´ל, of ´י's is judgement [Is 29:6](https://ref.ly/Isa29:6), [30:30](https://ref.ly/Isa30:30), [66:15](https://ref.ly/Isa66:15) (in all ‖ forces of nature, whirlwind, thunder, earthquake, rain, hail, etc.); fig. פְּנֵיהֶם ´פְּנֵי ל [Is 13:8](https://ref.ly/Isa13:8) (i.e. hot with excitement, cf. Di); sim. [Jo 2:5](https://ref.ly/Joel2:5) אֵשׁ ´כְּקוֺל ל (of rush of locusts); fig. of breath of crocodile [Jb 41:13](https://ref.ly/Job41:13). **2\.** of *flashing point* of spear [Jb 39:23](https://ref.ly/Job39:23); *blade* of sword [Na 3:3](https://ref.ly/Nah3:3); in prose, Ju [3:22](https://ref.ly/Judg3:22), [3:22](https://ref.ly/Judg3:22).

[10:17](https://ref.ly/Isa10:17), Ob:[18](https://ref.ly/Obad1:18); ´שַׁלְה ´ל Ez [21:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:3); fig. of drought [Jo 1:19](https://ref.ly/Joel1:19), of locusts’ ravages [2:3](https://ref.ly/Joel2:3) (vb. לָהַט in both); of war [Nu 21:28](https://ref.ly/Num21:28), [Je 48:4](https://ref.ly/Jer48:4) (both vb. אָכַל); cf. ## להג (id: BDB04805)

להג √ of foll. (Ar. لَهِجَ *be devoted, attached* to a thing, *apply oneself assiduously to it*).

> \[Str3854] †לַ֫הַג **n.m.** (late) study, i.e. devotion to books; הַרְבֵּה יְגִעַת בָּשָר ´ל [Ec 12:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:12) (cf. Now).

## לָהָה (id: BDB04808)

\[Str3856] †\[לָהָה] **vb.** languish, faint (\= לָאָה) (Targum לְהִי, *id.*;—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 fs. apoc. וַתֵּ֫לַהּ אֶרֶץ [Gn 47:13](https://ref.ly/Gen47:13) (of famine).

> \[לִהְלֵהַּ] **vb.quadril.** amaze, startle (cf. Syr. Palp. ܠܰܗܠܶܗ *confudit, obstupefecit;* Ethpalp. *stupore percussus est;* ܠܘܽܗܠܳܗܳܐ *consternatio,* all in Lexx., PS1894)—**Hithpalp.** *Pt.* מִתְלַהְלֵהַּ as subst. \= *madman*, sq. הַיֹּרֶה זִקִים [Pr 26:18](https://ref.ly/Prov26:18) (v. Now).

* **Associated Passages:** Genesis 47:13; Proverbs 26:18


## לָהַט (id: BDB04810)

\[Str3857] †\[לָהַט] **vb.** blaze up, flame (NH *id.;* Syr. ܠܗܶܛ; As. *la‘âtu,* in der. forms);—**Qal** *Pt. act.* אֵשׁ לֽהֵט [Psalm 104:4](https://ref.ly/Ps104:4) a *flaming fire* (so Bae; אֵשׁ וָלַהַט BI Che, cf. Ol), made into ´י's servants; pl. לֽהֲטִים [57:5](https://ref.ly/Ps57:5) (fig. of enemies, cf. לְבָאִם va). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 fs. וְלִהַט [Mal 3:19](https://ref.ly/Mal3:19); 3 fs. לִהֲטָה [Jo 1:19](https://ref.ly/Joel1:19); *Impf.* 3 fs. תְּלַהֵט [Jo 2:3](https://ref.ly/Joel2:3) \+ 4 t.; וַתְּלַהֵט [Dt 32:22](https://ref.ly/Deut32:22); sf. וַתְּלַהֲטֵהוּ [Is 42:25](https://ref.ly/Isa42:25); —*set ablaze*, usu. c. acc.;—foundation of mts., [Dt 32:22](https://ref.ly/Deut32:22) (subj. אֵשׁ fig., of ´י's judgement, ‖קָדַח, יָקַד, אָכַל), cf. הָרִים ´תְּל [Psalm 83:15](https://ref.ly/Ps83:15) (in sim., subj. לֶהָבָה; ‖כְּאֵשׁ תִּבְעַ־רָ\+יעַר; of flame (לֶהָבָה); consuming trees [Jo 1:19](https://ref.ly/Joel1:19) ( of effects of drought; ‖אֵשׁ אָֽכְלָה); persons [Mal 3:19](https://ref.ly/Mal3:19) (subj. הַיּוֺם הַבָּא ‖בָּעַר); cf. [Psalm 97:3](https://ref.ly/Ps97:3) (subj. אֵשׁ), [106:18](https://ref.ly/Ps106:18) (subj. לֶהָבָה ‖וַתִּבְעַ־ר אֵשׁ; hyperb of ´י's wrath (אַפּוֺ וֱעֶזוּז מִלְחָמָה חֵמָה) consuming Jacob [Is 42:25](https://ref.ly/Isa42:25); of crocodile (hyperb.), נַפְשׁוֺ גֶּחָלִים תְלַהֵָט [Jb 41:13](https://ref.ly/Job41:13) *his breath setteth coals ablaze;* abs. [Jo 2:3](https://ref.ly/Joel2:3), fig. of devastation by locusts (subj. לֶהָבָה; ‖אָֽכְלָה אֵשׁ).

> \[Str3858] †לַ֫הַט **n.\[m.] flame**;—only cstr. הַמִּתְהַפֶּכֶת לַהַט הַחֶרֶב [Gn 3:24](https://ref.ly/Gen3:24) *the flame of the whirling sword*.

## לָהַם (id: BDB04813)

Lex.) p.242

## לוּ (id: BDB04817)

\[Str3863] †לוּ:l7 and לוּא († [1 S 14:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:30), [Is 48:18](https://ref.ly/Isa48:18), [63:19](https://ref.ly/Isa63:19)), also לֻא (Qr לוּ), † [2 S 18:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:12), [19:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:7) (Ar. لَوْ, Aram. ܠܘܰܝ, לְוַי, Mishn. לְוַי, As. *lû*, with opt. force, Dl:§§ 78 end, 93, 145; cf. Köii. 333\), **conj.** if, O that :—**1\.** *if* (stating a case which has not been, or is not likely to be, realized): **a.** sq. pf. (so mostly), [Dt 32:29](https://ref.ly/Deut32:29) לו חכמו ישכילו זאת *if* they had been wise (which they are not), they would understand this; [Mi 2:11](https://ref.ly/Mic2:11) (apod. וְהָיָה); Ju [8:19](https://ref.ly/Judg8:19), [13:23](https://ref.ly/Judg13:23) להמיתנו לא לקח לו ´חפץ י *if* J. had desired to slay us, he would not have taken, \&c.; [1 S 14:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:30), [2 S 19:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:7). **b.** sq. impf. Ez [14:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:15) *if* I were to send, \&c. (but rd. prob. אוֺ, cf. v:[17](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:17), v:[19](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:19)). **c.** sq. ptcp., [2 S 18:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:12) וְלֻא אָנֹכִי שֹׁקֵל and *though* I should be weighing 1000 pieces of silver upon my hand, I would not, \&c.; [Psalm 81:14–17](https://ref.ly/Ps81:14-Ps81:17) לו עמי שֹׁמֵעַ לי *if* my people were hearkening to me,…quickly would I bow down, \&c. **d.** sq. יֵשׁ [Job 16:4](https://ref.ly/Job16:4).—With the apod. omitted, [Gn 50:15](https://ref.ly/Gen50:15) לוּ יִשְטְמֵנוּ *if* Joseph were to hate us (how should we fare then ?).

 **2\.** *If only ...!* i.e. *O that ! would that !* (cf. εἰ γα, εἴθε) usu. sq. perf., as [Nu 14:2](https://ref.ly/Num14:2), [14:2](https://ref.ly/Num14:2) לוּ מַתְנוּ *if only* we had died in the land of Egypt! [20:3](https://ref.ly/Num20:3), [Jos 7:7](https://ref.ly/Josh7:7) וְלוּ הוֺאַלְנוּ וַנֵּשֶׁב [Is 48:18](https://ref.ly/Isa48:18), [63:19](https://ref.ly/Isa63:19); sq. יֵשׁ [Nu 22:29](https://ref.ly/Num22:29); sq. impf. [Gn 17:18](https://ref.ly/Gen17:18) לו יחיה *O that* Ishmael might live before thee! [Jb 6:2](https://ref.ly/Job6:2); sq. juss. [Gn 30:34](https://ref.ly/Gen30:34) כדברך לו יהי; sq. imv. [23:13](https://ref.ly/Gen23:13) אם אתה לוּ שׁמעני if thou—*O that* thou wouldst hear me! (\+ prob. v:[5](https://ref.ly/Gen23:5) לוּ שׁמעני :לאמר for ´שׁ :לאמרֹ לוֺ, and similarly v:[15](https://ref.ly/Gen23:15)).—Rd. also prob. לֻא for Mas. לאֹ Ju [21:22](https://ref.ly/Judg21:22) (with כִּי עַתָּה), [1 S 13:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:13), [20:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:14), [20:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:14), [Jb 9:33](https://ref.ly/Job9:33) (sq. יֵשׁ); and perh. [14:4](https://ref.ly/Job14:4) (Ew Kö). 

> \[Str3884] †לוּלֵא [Gn 43:10](https://ref.ly/Gen43:10), Ju [14:18](https://ref.ly/Judg14:18), [2 S 2:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:27), [Psalm 2:13](https://ref.ly/Ps2:13), elsewhere לוּלֵי:10 if not, unless (fr. לוּ *if*, and לֵא, by dissim. (Köii. 236, 489\) for לאֹ *not;* cf. Ar. لَوْلَا), the neg. of לוּ, and used similarly:—**a.** sq. pf., Ju [14:18](https://ref.ly/Judg14:18) לא מצאתם לולא …חרשׁתם *unless* ye had ploughed with my heifer, ye would not have found out my riddle, [1 S 25:34](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:34) (second כי resumptive: כִּי **1 d**), [Psalm 106:23](https://ref.ly/Ps106:23); with apod, introd. by כִּי עַתָּה [Gn 31:42](https://ref.ly/Gen31:42), [43:10](https://ref.ly/Gen43:10); by אָז [2 S 2:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:27) (כי resumptive); by כִּמְעַט [Is 1:9](https://ref.ly/Isa1:9); with an aposiop. [Psalm 27:13](https://ref.ly/Ps27:13) *if I had not believed ...!* **b.** sq. impf. [Dt 32:27](https://ref.ly/Deut32:27) לולי אגור …אמרתי I should have said, \&c .…*except* I dreaded, \&c. **c.** sq. ptcp., [2 K 3:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:14). **d.** without a verb, [Psalm 94:17](https://ref.ly/Ps94:17) (apod. כמעט), [119:92](https://ref.ly/Ps119:92) (apod. אָז). In the later language, [Psalm 124:1](https://ref.ly/Ps124:1), [124:2](https://ref.ly/Ps124:2) \-שֶׁ ´לוּלֵי י (apod. אֲזַי *except that ...* (cf. Aram., ܐܠܘ ܠܐܕ [Psalm 106:23](https://ref.ly/Ps106:23), דִּי …אִילּוּלֵי [Psalm 27:13](https://ref.ly/Ps27:13) Targum).—Rd. also לוּלֵי for אוּלַי in [Nu 22:33](https://ref.ly/Num22:33) (apod. כִּי עַתָּה). See further on לוּ and לוּלֵא Dr§§ 139–145 Köiii. 487f; 565\.

> \[Str3926] †לְמוֺ poet. for לְ, [Job 27:14](https://ref.ly/Job27:14), [29:21](https://ref.ly/Job29:21), [38:40](https://ref.ly/Job38:40), [40:4](https://ref.ly/Job40:4), like כְּמוֺ for כְּ,בְמוֺ for בְּ: see מוֺ.

Africa, W. of Egypt;—[Na 3:9](https://ref.ly/Nah3:9) ( \+ פּוּט), [2 Ch 12:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr12:3) (LXX Λιβυες; \+ מִצְרַיִם, סֻכִּיִּים, כּוּשִׁים), [16:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:8) LXX Λιβυες; (\+ כּוּוִים); לֻבִַים [Dn 11:43](https://ref.ly/Dan11:43) (v. Baer; c. מִצְרַיִם, כֻּשִַׁים), Theod. Λιβυων; prob. \= לְהָבִים (q. v.) [Gn 10:13](https://ref.ly/Gen10:13) \= [1 Ch 1:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:11) A LXXL Λαβιειμ; read לוּבים perh. also [Je 46:9](https://ref.ly/Jer46:9) (for HCT לוּדִים, cf. StaJavan 5f.), LXX Λυδοι (פּוּט *ib.* \= Λιβυες); LXX Sm Co StaJavan 6 Berthol rd. לוּב Ez [30:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:5) (for HCT כּוּב, q. v.) See WMMAs. Eur. 115\.

Lud, Lydia,** As. *Luddu:*—אַרְפַּכְשַׁד וְלוּד וַאֲרָם [Gn 10:22](https://ref.ly/Gen10:22) \= [1 Ch 1:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:17) LXX Λουδ. **2\.** appar. a people in NE. Africa תַּרְשִׁישׁ פּוּל וְלוּד [Is 66:19](https://ref.ly/Isa66:19), LXX Λουδ; ´וּפוּט פָּרַס וְל Ez [27:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:10); כּוּשׁ וּפוּט וְלוּד [30:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:5) ( \+ לוּב q. v.), in both LXX Λ(ο)υδοι; also pl. לוּדִים a ‘son’ of Miṣraim, [Gn 10:13](https://ref.ly/Gen10:13) LXX Λυδιειμ (‖לְהָבִים) \= [1 Ch 1:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:11) (לוּדִי֯ים); [Je 46:9](https://ref.ly/Jer46:9) (‖פּוּט,כּוּשׁ) v. לוּב supr.—On this African לוּד v. Di:[Gn 10:13](https://ref.ly/Gen10:13), opp. StaJavan 5 ff. cf. WMMAs. Eur. 115\.

## I לָוָה (id: BDB04823)

\[Str3867] †I. \[לָוָה] join (intr.), be joined (NH Pi. לִוָּה (ליוה) trans., Hithp. intr.; Aram. לַוִּי *accompany;* so ܠܘܳܐ Baes 12 comp. Ar. وَلِىَ be *near*);—**Qal** (late) *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יִלְוֶנּוּ Ec [8:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:13), *be joined to, attend* (of mirth). **Niph.** נִלְוָה [Psalm 83:9](https://ref.ly/Ps83:9), [Is 14:1](https://ref.ly/Isa14:1); 3 mpl. consec. וְנִלְווּ [Nu 18:4](https://ref.ly/Num18:4) \+ 2 t., \+ [Je 50:5](https://ref.ly/Jer50:5) (GesLbg. Bö Köi. 588 Gf Gie; \> *Imv.* Ki Ew§ 226c Ol:§ 264\); *Impf.* יִלָּוֶה [Gn 29:34](https://ref.ly/Gen29:34); יִלָּווּ [Nu 18:2](https://ref.ly/Num18:2); *Pt.* נִלְוָה [Is 56:3](https://ref.ly/Isa56:3) (pointed as Pf., rd. prob. נִלְוֶה); pl. נִלְוִים [Est 9:27](https://ref.ly/Esth9:27), [Is 56:6](https://ref.ly/Isa56:6); —*join oneself* or *be joined* unto, sq. עַל [Nu 18:2](https://ref.ly/Num18:2), [18:4](https://ref.ly/Num18:4) (מַטֵּה לֵנִי to Aaron, by word\-play), [Is 14:1](https://ref.ly/Isa14:1) (strangers to Isr.), [Dn 11:34](https://ref.ly/Dan11:34) (flatterers to the people), cf. [Est 9:27](https://ref.ly/Esth9:27); [Is 56:6](https://ref.ly/Isa56:6) (to ´י sq. אֶ־ל [Is 56:3](https://ref.ly/Isa56:3) (to ´י), [Zc 2:15](https://ref.ly/Zech2:15) (to ´י), [Je 50:5](https://ref.ly/Jer50:5) (to ´י), [Gn 29:34](https://ref.ly/Gen29:34) (J; husband to wife); sq. עִם [Psalm 83:9](https://ref.ly/Ps83:9) (Asshur with enemies of Isr.)

## II לָוָה (id: BDB04824)

\[Str3867] †II. \[לָוָה] **vb.** borrow (NH *id.;* cf. Ar. لَوَى *delay payment of debt* (cf. Baes 12 );—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 pl. לָוִ֫ינוּ [Ne 5:4](https://ref.ly/Neh5:4);  *Impf.* 2 ms. תִּלְוֶה [Dt 28:12](https://ref.ly/Deut28:12);  *Pt.* לֽוֶה [Pr 22:7](https://ref.ly/Prov22:7) \+ 2 t.;— *borrow*, לְאִישׁ לוֶֹה עֶבֶד מַלְוֶה [Pr 22:7](https://ref.ly/Prov22:7) *a borrower is slave to a man who lends;* abs. [Dt 28:12](https://ref.ly/Deut28:12), [Is 24:2](https://ref.ly/Isa24:2), [Psalm 37:21](https://ref.ly/Ps37:21); sq. כֶּסֶף [Ne 5:4](https://ref.ly/Neh5:4). **Hiph.** Pf. 2 ms. consec. וְהִלְוִיתָה [Dt 28:12](https://ref.ly/Deut28:12); *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יַלְוְ [Dt 28:44](https://ref.ly/Deut28:44); 2 ms. תַּלְוֶה [Ex 22:24](https://ref.ly/Exod22:24); sf. תַּלְוֶנּוּ [Dt 28:44](https://ref.ly/Deut28:44); Pt. מַלְוֶה [Psalm 37:26](https://ref.ly/Ps37:26) \+ 3 t., cstr. מַלְוֵה [Pr 19:17](https://ref.ly/Prov19:17); —*cause to borrow*, i.e. *lend to,* c. acc. Pers. \+ rei [Ex 22:24](https://ref.ly/Exod22:24) (E); c. acc. Pers. [Dt 28:12](https://ref.ly/Deut28:12), [28:44](https://ref.ly/Deut28:44), [28:44](https://ref.ly/Deut28:44); abs. [Psalm 37:26](https://ref.ly/Ps37:26), [112:5](https://ref.ly/Ps112:5) אִישׁ מַלְוֶה [Pr 22:77](https://ref.ly/Prov22:77) (v. **Qal** supr.); as subst. [Is 24:2](https://ref.ly/Isa24:2); ´מַלְוֵה י [Pr 19:17](https://ref.ly/Prov19:17) *a lender to* ´י is one shewing favour to the poor.

## III לוה (id: BDB04825)

III. לוה (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. لَوَى *turn, twist, wind;* As. *lamû, surround, encircle;* DlHWB 368 JenZMG xiiii (1889\), 201\).

> \[Str3880] †\[לִוְיָה] **n.f.** wreath;—cstr. לִוְיַת חֵן only fig. [Pr 1:9](https://ref.ly/Prov1:9) (‖עֲנָקִים) of instruction of parents, [4:9](https://ref.ly/Prov4:9) (‖עֲטֶרֶת תִּפְאֶרֶת) of work of Wisdom.

> \[Str3914] †\[ לֽיָה] **n.f.** wreath ? (mng. dubious, cf. StaZAW iii. 1883, 161; poss. \= לְוֺיָה);—pl. לֽיוֺת [1 K 7:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:29), [7:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:30), [7:36](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:36) of carved work on bases of lavers in Solomon's temple.

> \[Str3882] †לִוְיָתָן **n.m.**:[Jb 40:25](https://ref.ly/Job40:25) serpent, dragon, leviathan, poet. and rare (on format. fr. לוה c. fem. ת \+ ן—ָ v. Thes and cf. Ges§ 85, 54 Köii, p. 99 BaNB § 207c; LagBN 205 thinks foreign loan\-word);—*sea\-monster* \= crocodile [Jb 40:25](https://ref.ly/Job40:25); whale [Psalm 104:25](https://ref.ly/Ps104:25) (v. Che); *dragon* producing eclipses (mythol.) [Jb 3:8](https://ref.ly/Job3:8); fig. of Egypt as all\-engulfing [Psalm 74:14](https://ref.ly/Ps74:14) (‖תַּנִּינִים v:[13](https://ref.ly/Ps74:13)); cf. [Is 27:1](https://ref.ly/Isa27:1), [27:1](https://ref.ly/Isa27:1) (‖בָּרִחַ נָחָשׁ, עֲקַלָּתוֺן נָחָשׁ), v. Che Di and esp. (on ´ל in gen.) BartonTiamat, JAOS xv (1891\), 22 ff. GunkelSchöpf. u. Chaos 46\.

## לוּז (id: BDB04829)

\[Str3868] †\[לוּז] **vb.** (poet., mostly WsdLt) turn aside, depart (NH Niph., Hiph.; Ar. لَاذَ  *have recourse to, take refuge* in);—**Qal** *Impf.* מֵעֵיֶ\+ניְ אַל־יָלֻזוּ [Pr 3:21](https://ref.ly/Prov3:21) *let them not* (i.e. sound wisdom, etc., si vera 1\., cf. VB) *depart from thine eyes* (v. Hiph.). **Niph.** *Pt.* נָלוֺז [Pr 3:32](https://ref.ly/Prov3:32), [Is 30:12](https://ref.ly/Isa30:12); cstr. נְלוֺז [Pr 14:2](https://ref.ly/Prov14:2); pl. נְלוֺזִים [2:15](https://ref.ly/Prov2:15); —fig., *devious, crooked,* בְּמַעְגְּלֽתָם ´נ [Pr 2:15](https://ref.ly/Prov2:15) (‖עִקְּשִׁים); דְּרָכָיו ´נ [14:2](https://ref.ly/Prov14:2); as subst. of Pers. [3:32](https://ref.ly/Prov3:32); of course of action [Is 30:12](https://ref.ly/Isa30:12) (‖עשֶׁק), i.e. *crookedness \= craftiness, cunning,* cf. De Che Di. **Hiph.** *Impf.* אַל־יַלִּיזוּ מֵעֵינֶיְ [Pr 4:21](https://ref.ly/Prov4:21) (subj. דְּבָרַי *let them* \[my words] *not depart* (on form v. Ges§ 72 R. 9\) *from thine eyes* (strictly, *let them not practise, exhibit, deviation,* direct causat., Kö.i. 205\).

> \[Str3891] †\[לָזוּת] **n.f.** deviation, crookedness (fig.);—only cstr. לְזוּת שְפָתַיִם [Pr 4:24](https://ref.ly/Prov4:24) (on form cf. Ol:§ 219a Köii. 166, 474\).

> \[Str3869] †I. לוּז **n. \[m.]** almond\-tree, almond\-wood ( **NH** id.; Aram. לוּזָא, لُجٌّ *almond* (LöwNo. 319\), whence Ar. لَوْزٌ as loan\-wd. (Frä145 LagBN 157 f.), Eth. ለውዝ፡);—[Gn 30:37](https://ref.ly/Gen30:37) (J; \+ לִבְנֶה, עַרְמוֺן לַח) of rods stripped by Jacob.

> \[Str3870] †II. לוּז **n.pr.loc. 1\.** former name of Bethel [Gn 28:19](https://ref.ly/Gen28:19) (J), [35:6](https://ref.ly/Gen35:6) (E), [48:3](https://ref.ly/Gen48:3) (P), [Jos 18:13](https://ref.ly/Josh18:13), [18:13](https://ref.ly/Josh18:13) (P), Ju [1:23](https://ref.ly/Judg1:23) ; appar. distinct fr. B. מִבֵּית־אֵל לוּ֫זָה [Jos 16:2](https://ref.ly/Josh16:2) (JE; but ´ל here perh. explan. gloss, v.Di); LXX Λουζα, [Gn 28:19](https://ref.ly/Gen28:19) \[ουλαμ]μαυς **2\.** city in hands of Hittites Ju [1:26](https://ref.ly/Judg1:26) (on conject. as to site v. GFM); LXX Λουζα.

## לוּט (id: BDB04836)

\[Str3874] \[לוּט] **vb.** wrap closely, tightly, enwrap, envelope (Ar. لَاطَ *cleave, stick* to a thing; also trans. *make to stick*, or *adhere*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. לָאט [2 S 19:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:15) (so rd., for MT לָאַט, We Klo Dr Bu); *Pt. act.* לוֺט [Is 25:7](https://ref.ly/Isa25:7) (cf. Köi. 445\); *Pt. pass.* f. לוּטָה [1 S 21:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:10); —לוּטָה בַשִּמְלָה [1 S 21:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:10) it is *wrapped up in a garment* (of sword of Goliath); fig. of covering as sign of mourning, הַלּוֺט פְּנֵי־הַלּוֺט עַל־כָל־הָעַמִּים [Is 25:7](https://ref.ly/Isa25:7) *the surface of covering which covereth over all the peoples* (‖מַסֵּכָה הַנְּסוּכָה). **Hiph.** *Impf. envelope, wrap* וַּיָּ֫לָט פָּנָיוּ בְּאַדַּרְתּוֺ [1 K 19:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:13).

> \[Str3814,3909,3858] †לָּט,לָאט **n.\[m.]** secrecy, mystery ;—abs. לָט [1 S 18:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:22) \+ 2 t., לָאט Ju [4:21](https://ref.ly/Judg4:21); pl. sf. לָטֵיהֶם [Ex 7:22](https://ref.ly/Exod7:22) \+ 2 t.; לַהֲטֵיהֶם [Ex 7:11](https://ref.ly/Exod7:11); —alw.c. בְּ: **l.** בַּלָּט \= *secretly* [1 S 18:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:22), [24:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:5), [Ru 3:7](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:7); so בַּלָּאט Ju [4:21](https://ref.ly/Judg4:21). **2\.** בְּלָטֵיהֶם *with their mysteries* \= enchantments (i. e. of חַרְטֻמֵּי מִצְרַיִם) Ex [7:22](https://ref.ly/Exod7:22), [8:3](https://ref.ly/Exod8:3), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Exod8:14) \= בְּלַהֲטֵיהֶם [7:11](https://ref.ly/Exod7:11) (all P).

> \[Str3875] †I. לוֺט **n.m.** envelope, covering;—[Is 25:7](https://ref.ly/Isa25:7) v. sub vb. supr.

\[Str3885] I. לוּן, לִין **vb.** lodge, pass the night, fig. abide (NH לִינָה n. *lodging, spending the night;* cf. NöZMG xxxvii (1883\), 535 ff.; acc. to Thes kindred with לַיְלָה (q.v. infra), with change of ל and ן)—**Qal** *Pf.* לָן [Gn 32:22](https://ref.ly/Gen32:22), [2 S 12:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:16); 3 fs. וְ֫לָנֶה consec. (before monosyl. ) [Zc 5:4](https://ref.ly/Zech5:4); 1 pl. וְלַנּוּ consec. Ju [19:13](https://ref.ly/Judg19:13) (v. Ges§ 73\. R. 1 Köi. 508\); *Impf.* יָלִין [Ex 23:18](https://ref.ly/Exod23:18) \+ 13 t. \+ [2 S 17:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:8), but cf. **Hiph.**; וַיָּ֫לֶן [Gn 28:11](https://ref.ly/Gen28:11) \+ 4 t.; 3 fs. תָּלִין [Lv 19:13](https://ref.ly/Lev19:13) \+ 4 t.; תָּלַ֫ן [Jb 17:2](https://ref.ly/Job17:2) (v. Köi. 509\); 2 ms. juss. אַל־תָּ֫לֶן [2 S 17:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:16); תַָּלָן Ju [19:20](https://ref.ly/Judg19:20) (v. Köi.c.), etc.; *Imv*. לִין Ju [9:6](https://ref.ly/Judg9:6), [9:19](https://ref.ly/Judg9:19), etc.; *Inf. cstr.* לָלוּן [Gn 24:25](https://ref.ly/Gen24:25) \+ 5 t.; לָלִין [24:23](https://ref.ly/Gen24:23); *Pt.* pl. לֵנִים [Ne 13:21](https://ref.ly/Neh13:21); —**1\.** *lodge, pass the night:* **a.** lit., human subj., oft. c. prep. or adv. of place, [Gn 19:2](https://ref.ly/Gen19:2), [28:11](https://ref.ly/Gen28:11), Ju [19:13](https://ref.ly/Judg19:13), [19:15](https://ref.ly/Judg19:15), [2 S 17:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:16), [Jb 24:7](https://ref.ly/Job24:7) \+ oft. (on [2 S 17:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:8) v. **Hiph.**); c. ה loc., וְלָן וְשָׁכַב אַ֫רְצָה [2 S 12:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:16); c. ב loc. \+ ב temp. בַּלַּיְלָה הַהוּא [Gn 32:14](https://ref.ly/Gen32:14), [32:22](https://ref.ly/Gen32:22), [Jos 8:9](https://ref.ly/Josh8:9); c. הַלַּיְלָה (accus. temp. ), [Nu 22:8](https://ref.ly/Num22:8), [Jos 4:3](https://ref.ly/Josh4:3), [2 S 17:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:16), [19:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:8), [Ru 3:13](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:13); of the wicked [Psalm 59:16](https://ref.ly/Ps59:16), acc. to Hup De Pe al.; \< fr. II. לון LXX Vulgate Aq Che Bae al., cf. AV; sim. of temporary sojourn [Je 14:8](https://ref.ly/Jer14:8) (of ´י in Isr.) **b.** of animals : wild ox (רְאֵם) [Jb 39:9](https://ref.ly/Job39:9) (sq. עַל־אֲבוּסְֶָ ), porcupine (קִפֹּד) [Zp 2:14](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:14). **c.** inanim. subj. \= *remain all night :* of fat of sacrifice [Ex 23:18](https://ref.ly/Exod23:18) (E; sq. עַד־בֹּקֶר), passover meal [34:25](https://ref.ly/Exod34:25) (P; sq. לַבֹּקֶר), [Dt 16:4](https://ref.ly/Deut16:4) (sq. לַבֹּקֶר; all these without local designation), wages of hireling [Lv 19:13](https://ref.ly/Lev19:13) (H ; אֶת \= *with* \+ עַד־בֹּקֶר); of dead body [Dt 21:23](https://ref.ly/Deut21:23) (עַל loc.); dew [Jb 29:19](https://ref.ly/Job29:19) (ב loc.); bunch of myrrh Ct [1:13](https://ref.ly/Song1:13) בֵּין loc.) **d.** fig. יָלִין בֶָּכִי בָּעֶרֶב [Psalm 30:6](https://ref.ly/Ps30:6) *at evening weeping may come to lodge* (opp. וְלַבֹּקֶר רִנָּה). †**2\.** fig. *abide, remain*, subj. עַיִן ( \= *look upon*), c. ב [Jb 17:2](https://ref.ly/Job17:2); of error, [19:4](https://ref.ly/Job19:4) (c. prep. אֵת Pers.), strength [41:14](https://ref.ly/Job41:14) (of crocod.; c. ב); of righteousness, [Is 1:21](https://ref.ly/Isa1:21) (c. ב); תָּלִין נַפְשׁוֺ בְטוֺב [Psalm 25:13](https://ref.ly/Ps25:13); of hearkening ear [Pr 15:31](https://ref.ly/Prov15:31), c. בְּקֶרֶב; of thoughts [Je 4:14](https://ref.ly/Jer4:14), c. id.; abs., of man, *continue, endure*, [Psalm 49:13](https://ref.ly/Ps49:13), שָבֵעַ יָלִין [Pr 19:23](https://ref.ly/Prov19:23) *he shall continue satisfied*. **Hiph.** *Impf.* יָלִין *cause to rest, lodge*, sq. acc., [2 S 17:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:8) v. Köi. 509\. **Hithpalp.** *Impf.* יִתְלָֽ\+נן [Jb 39:28](https://ref.ly/Job39:28) *dwell, abide*, of eagle ; fig. יִתְלוָֺ\+נן בְּצֵל שַׁדַּי [Psalm 91:1](https://ref.ly/Ps91:1) of one trusting in ´י.

> \[Str4411] †מָלוֺן **n.m.** lodging\-place, inn, khan;—abs. ´מ [Gn 42:27](https://ref.ly/Gen42:27) \+ 5 t.; cstr. מְלוֺן [2 K 19:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:23), [Je 9:1](https://ref.ly/Jer9:1); —*lodging\-place, inn, khan (?)* [Gn 42:27](https://ref.ly/Gen42:27), [43:21](https://ref.ly/Gen43:21), [Ex 4:24](https://ref.ly/Exod4:24); אֹרְתִים ´מְ [Je 9:1](https://ref.ly/Jer9:1); \= camp (of Isr.) for a night, [Jos 4:3](https://ref.ly/Josh4:3) ( \+ לין), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Josh4:8); of Assyrians [Is 10:29](https://ref.ly/Isa10:29); fig. מְלוֺן קִצֹּה֯ [2 K 19:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:23), i.e. its (Lebanon's) remotest camping\-ground, hyperb. of Assyrian conquest; so rd. prob. in ‖ [Is 37:24](https://ref.ly/Isa37:24) for MT ´מְרוֺם ק, cf. Di Du Kit CheHpt.

> \[Str4412] †מְלוּנָה **n.f.** lodge, hut;—בְּמִקְשָׁה ´מ [Is 1:8](https://ref.ly/Isa1:8) (i.e. a watchman"s hut); sim. of frail, insecure structure בַּמְּלוּנָה וְהִתְנוֺדֲדָה [Is 24:20](https://ref.ly/Isa24:20) *and it* \[the earth] *shall shake* (or *totter*) *like a hut.*

I. לון1 a). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 2 mpl. הֲלִינֹתֶם [Nu 14:29](https://ref.ly/Num14:29); *Impf.* וַיָּ֫לֶן [Ex 17:3](https://ref.ly/Exod17:3); וַיַּלִּי֫נוּ [Ex 16:2](https://ref.ly/Exod16:2) Kt, [Nu 14:36](https://ref.ly/Num14:36) Qr; 2 mpl. תַּלִּי֫נוּ [Ex 16:7](https://ref.ly/Exod16:7) Qr, [Nu 16:11](https://ref.ly/Num16:11) Qr (on all vid. supr.); *Pt.* pl. מַלִּינִים [Ex 16:8](https://ref.ly/Exod16:8) \+ 3 t.;—*murmur* \= Niph., sq. על [Ex 17:3](https://ref.ly/Exod17:3) (E), elsewhere P: 16:8, [Nu 14:27](https://ref.ly/Num14:27), [14:27](https://ref.ly/Num14:27), [14:29](https://ref.ly/Num14:29), [17:20](https://ref.ly/Num17:5); *cause to murmur*, sq. acc. \+ עַל [Nu 14:36](https://ref.ly/Num14:36) Qr; (cf. also supra **Niph.**)

## לוּעַ (id: BDB04853)

\[Str3886] †**\[לוּעַ, or לָעַע]** **vb.** swallow, swallow down (Syr. ܠܰܥ, ܠܳܥ *lap* or *lick up* Ju [7:5–7](https://ref.ly/Judg7:5-Judg7:7));—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. וְלָע֫וּ consec. Ob:[16](https://ref.ly/Obad1:16) (Ges§ 67 R. 12\);—abs., fig. of nations drinking (cup of judgment).—Hi De *talk wildly,* vid. **II.**לוע; We Now נָעוּ *reel, totter* (cf. [Is 24:20](https://ref.ly/Isa24:20), [29:9](https://ref.ly/Isa29:9)).

> \[Str3930] †\[לֽעַ] **n.\[m.]** prob. **throat** (external) (cf. NH לוֺעַ *jaw, cheek,* Aram. לוֺעָא, ܠܘܥܐ *id.*);—וְשַמְתָּ֫ שַכִּין בְּלֽעְֶָ [Pr 23:2](https://ref.ly/Prov23:2) *and thou shalt put a knife to thy throat* (fig. for restraining oneself from indulgence in food).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 29:9; Isaiah 24:20; Proverbs 23:2


## II לוּע (id: BDB04855)

\[Str3886,3216] †II. \[לוּע, or לָעַע] **vb.** talk wildly (MT לָעוּ יָ֫לַע perh. better wd. Be לָעוּ֫, יִ֫לַע (√ לעה), cf. Thes and Ar. لَغَا, لَغِىَ *make mistakes in talking,* BaZMG xii. 1887, 605, 614\);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 mpl. לָעוּ [Jb 6:3](https://ref.ly/Job6:3), subj. דברים (v. Di); *Impf.* מוֺקֵשׁ אָדָם יָ֫לַע קֹדֶשׁ [Pr 20:25](https://ref.ly/Prov20:25) it is *a snare to a man* that *he should rashly cry, Holy !* (construction unusual, and perh. text, err., cf. Frankenberg; on form v. Ol:§ 243 a Anm; Böi. 296 Köi. 375 f. der. fr. לעע; Str., Hiph. fr. לעע).

## לחה (id: BDB04862)

לחה (√ of foll., poss. fr. *smoothness*, cf. Ar.لَحَى *peel off;* Syr. ܠܚܳܐ *strip off, erase*).

> \[Str3895] †I. לְחִי **n.m.**:Ct J. 10 jaw, cheek (NH *id.;* Aram. לוֺחָא; Ar. لَحْىٌ; on As. *laḫû* cf. DlHWB 375, but ح \= *ḫ ?*);—´ל abs. Ju [15:17](https://ref.ly/Judg15:17), [Mi 4:14](https://ref.ly/Mic4:14); cstr. Ju [15:15](https://ref.ly/Judg15:15) \+ 2 t.; לֶָחִי [1 K 22:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:24) \+ 3 t. \+ Ju [15:19](https://ref.ly/Judg15:19), [15:19](https://ref.ly/Judg15:19); *sf.* לֶחֱיוֺ [Jb 40:26](https://ref.ly/Job40:26); לֶחֱיָהּ [La 1:2](https://ref.ly/Lam1:2); du. לְחָיַיִּם [Dt 18:3](https://ref.ly/Deut18:3); cstr. לְחָיֵי [Is 30:28](https://ref.ly/Isa30:28); sf. לְחָיַי [Is 50:6](https://ref.ly/Isa50:6), cf. [Jb 16:10](https://ref.ly/Job16:10), etc.; לְחֵיהֶם [Ho 11:4](https://ref.ly/Hos11:4); —**1\.** *jaw, jawbone,* of animal, Ju [15:15](https://ref.ly/Judg15:15), [15:16](https://ref.ly/Judg15:16), [15:16](https://ref.ly/Judg15:16), [15:17](https://ref.ly/Judg15:17) (under jaw of ass), cf. n.pr.loc. רָכַת לֶָחִי v:[17](https://ref.ly/Judg15:17); in sim. עַלּ־לְחֵיהֶם כִּמְרִימֵי עֹל [Ho 11:4](https://ref.ly/Hos11:4) *like those lifting up a yoke from upon* (rd. מֵעַל ?) *their jaws* (Isr. under fig. of oxen); of sacrificial ox or sheep [Dt 18:3](https://ref.ly/Deut18:3); of crocodile [Jb 40:26](https://ref.ly/Job40:26); of Pharaoh under fig. of תַּנִּים Ez [29:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:4) (cf. v:[3](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:3)); cf. [38:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:4), [Is 30:28](https://ref.ly/Isa30:28). **2\.** *cheek*, of man [1 K 22:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:24) \= [2 Ch 18:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:23), [Jb 16:10](https://ref.ly/Job16:10), [Psalm 3:8](https://ref.ly/Ps3:8), Ct [5:13](https://ref.ly/Song5:13), [Is 50:6](https://ref.ly/Isa50:6), [La 3:30](https://ref.ly/Lam3:30), [Mi 4:14](https://ref.ly/Mic4:14); of woman Ct [1:10](https://ref.ly/Song1:10), [La 1:2](https://ref.ly/Lam1:2) (fig. of Jerusalem).

> \[Str3896] †II. לְחִי **n.pr.loc.** scene of an exploit of Samson;—´ל Ju [15:19](https://ref.ly/Judg15:19), [15:19](https://ref.ly/Judg15:19) (LXX Σιαγων), לֶ֔חִי v:[14](https://ref.ly/Judg15:14); c. art. בַּלֶּחי v:[9](https://ref.ly/Judg15:9) (LXX Λευει, LXXL Λεχει), cf. רָ֫מַת לֶָחִי v:[17](https://ref.ly/Judg15:17) \= *height of Leḥi* (expl. as fr. לְחִי *jawbone*, cf. GFM, LXX Ἀναίρεσις σιαγόνος also [2 S 23:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:11), where read לֶחְיָה *to Leḥi*, for לַחַיָּה, vid. II. חַיָּה, p. 31:2 supr.; (LXX θηρία; LXXL σιαγόνα). It must have lain in the Shephelah, near the border of the hill\-country of Judah (BuhlGeogr. 91\); site unknown; conjectures are cited and criticized by GFM: Ju [15:19](https://ref.ly/Judg15:19) q. v.

* **Associated Passages:** Judges 15:16; Ezekiel 38:4; Judges 15:14; Judges 15:15; Psalms 3:8; Micah 4:14; Isaiah 50:6; Isaiah 30:28; Ezekiel 29:4; Judges 15:9; Song of Songs 1:10; 2 Samuel 23:11; Hosea 11:4; Deuteronomy 18:3; 1 Kings 22:24; Judges 15:19; Job 16:10; Lamentations 3:30; Song of Songs 5:13; Job 40:26; Lamentations 1:2; Ezekiel 29:3; Judges 15:17; 2 Chronicles 18:23


## לחח (id: BDB04865)

לחח (√ of foll.; cf. Eth አልሕሐ፡. II. I. *moisten, cool;* ስኣሕልሐ፡ *be moistened* DILex. 30; NH לִחְלַח Pilp. *moisten thoroughly*, Aram. לַחְלַח *id.,* NH לַח *moist, fresh;* לֵיחָה *moisture, freshness*, etc.)

> \[Str3892] †לַח **adj.** moist, fresh, new;—´ל [Gn 30:37](https://ref.ly/Gen30:37), Ez [21:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:3); לָח[17:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:24); pl. לַחִים [Nu 6:3](https://ref.ly/Num6:3) \+ 2 t.;—**1\.** *moist, fresh,* of trees, fruit, etc.; לִבְנֶה לַח [Gn 30:37](https://ref.ly/Gen30:37) (J); ´עֵץ ל Ez [17:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:24), [21:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:3); grapes ´עֲנָבִים ל [Nu 6:3](https://ref.ly/Num6:3). **2\.** *new,* of cords, ´יְתָרִים ל Ju [16:7](https://ref.ly/Judg16:7), [16:8](https://ref.ly/Judg16:8), prob. made of *fresh* sinews of animal (cf. Syr. ܝܺܬܰܪ PS1652; GFMad loc.)

> \[Str3893] †\[לֵחַ] **n.m.** moisture, freshness (i.e. *force, vigour*);—sf. וְלֽא נָם לֵחהֹ [Dt 34:7](https://ref.ly/Deut34:7) *and his freshness had not fled;* rd. prob. also בְּלֵחוֺ for MT בְּלַחְמוֺ [Je 11:19](https://ref.ly/Jer11:19) (v. לֶחֶם ad fin.; on [Zp 1:17](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:17) v. לְחוּם).

> \[Str3895–96] לְחִי, לֶָחִי v. לחה

* **Associated Passages:** Zephaniah 1:17; Deuteronomy 34:7; Ezekiel 17:24; Judges 16:8; Jeremiah 11:19; Judges 16:7; Genesis 30:37; Ezekiel 21:3; Numbers 6:3
* **Associated Articles:** לחה (ID: `BDB04862@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## לחַךְ (id: BDB04869)

\[Str3897] †לחַךְ **vb.** lick (NH *id.;* so Aram. לְחַךְ ܠܚܰܟ; Ar. لَحِكَ; \- **Qal** *Inf. cstr.* כִּלְחֹךְ [Nu 22:4](https://ref.ly/Num22:4) (E), sq. acc.; of ox licking up grass. **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 fs. לִחֵָכָה [1 K 18:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:38); *Impf.* 3 mpl. יְלַחֲכוּ [Nu 22:4](https://ref.ly/Num22:4), [Mi 7:17](https://ref.ly/Mic7:17); יְלַחֵָכוּ [Psalm 72:9](https://ref.ly/Ps72:9), [Is 43:23](https://ref.ly/Isa43:23); *lick up* (sq. acc.), fire fr. heaven the water in trench [1 K 18:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:38); of Isr. consuming produce of land [Nu 22:3](https://ref.ly/Num22:3)(E); esp. עָפָר ´ל *lick the dust*, sign of humiliation [Mi 7:17](https://ref.ly/Mic7:17), [Psalm 72:9](https://ref.ly/Ps72:9), [Is 49:23](https://ref.ly/Isa49:23).

\[Str3898] I. \[לָחַם]:171 **vb.**fight, do battle (NH Hithp.; ואלתחם MI11.15\.19 perh. \= *order the battle,* cf.
