GFMad loc.

> \[Str3894] †לְחוּם **n.\[m.]** perh. intestines, bowels (mng. and √ not wholly certain, cf. NöZMG xl. 886, 721; from לחם \= *press together,* acc. to DlProl. 193\);—only sf.: וּלְחֻמַם כַּגְּלָלִים [Zp 1:17](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:17) their blood shall be poured out like water,*and their bowels like dung* Hi\-St (ct. [2 S 20:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:10)), Dl1. c. We (‘ihr Mark’), RothsteinKauAT Di:[Jb 20:23](https://ref.ly/Job20:23); Gr חֵילָם cf. v:[12](https://ref.ly/Job20:12) ( to wh. Now incl.); Now suggests also וְלֵחָם *and their moisture (freshness, vigour,* cf. [Dt 34:7](https://ref.ly/Deut34:7)); \> al. who render *flesh,* LXX τὰς σάρκας (cf. Ar. لَحْمٌ *flesh, meat*, v. sub לֶחֶם); וְיַמְטֵר עָלֵימוֺ בִּלְחוּמוֺ [Jb 20:23](https://ref.ly/Job20:23) to fill his belly, God sendeth into him his burning anger,  *and raineth* it *upon him, into his* (very) *bowels* Hi Dl1.c. BaeKauAT (cf. Di, and *into his flesh* De, v. supr.); \> disting. from [Zp 1:17](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:17) Di (in transl.) Da Hoffm Bu *his food* (as obj. of ימטר; rd. then לַחְמוֺ); LXX ὀδύνας, whence Me Sgfr חֲבָלִים , SchwallyZAW x, 1890, 179 בַּלָּהָה; Nö1.c. *wrath,* cf. Syr. ܠܚܡ *threaten*.

## II לָחַם (id: BDB04874)

\[Str3898] †II. \[לָחַם] **vb.** use as food, eat, poet. (so Thes Buhl, cf. Frä30 GuidiDella Sede 33; this would explain diff. between לֶחֶם and لَحْمٌ; yet verb rare and chiefly late);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. לָֽחֲמוּ [Pr 4:17](https://ref.ly/Prov4:17); *Impf.* 2 ms. תִּלְחַם [Pr 23:6](https://ref.ly/Prov23:6), 1 S. אֶלְחַם [Psalm 141:4](https://ref.ly/Ps141:4); *Imv.* mpl. לַחֲמוּ [Pr 9:5](https://ref.ly/Prov9:5); *Inf. cstr.* לְחוֺם [Pr 23:1](https://ref.ly/Prov23:1);  *Pt. pass.* pl. cstr. לְחֻמֵי [Dt 32:24](https://ref.ly/Deut32:24); *eat* (mostly Pr), abs. [Pr 23:1](https://ref.ly/Prov23:1); sq. acc. cogn. לֶחֶם [4:17](https://ref.ly/Prov4:17), [23:6](https://ref.ly/Prov23:6); sq. בְּלַחְמִי [9:5](https://ref.ly/Prov9:5); sq. בְּמַנְעַמֵּיהֶם [Psalm 141:4](https://ref.ly/Ps141:4) *eat of their dainties;* לְחֻמֵי רֶשֶׁף [Dt 32:24](https://ref.ly/Deut32:24) (poem) *eaten up with the Fire\-bolt*, fig. of pestilence (Dr).

II. עֶצָב; אוֺנִים ´ל [Ho 9:4](https://ref.ly/Hos9:4), vid אָוֶן. p.20 a; אֲנָשִׁים ´ל Ez [24:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:17), [24:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:22) of food offered to mourners, cf. [Je 16:7](https://ref.ly/Jer16:7) (where ins. ´ל LXX Gf Gie); שַׁלַּח לחמך על־פני המים [Ec 11:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:1) usually interpr. of benevolent giving (cf. Now); De al. of sea\-commerce in bread\-stuffs, cf. [Pr 31:14](https://ref.ly/Prov31:14). **b.** fig. לַחְמֵנוּ הֵם [Nu 14:9](https://ref.ly/Num14:9) (JE) *our food are they,* i.e. we will devour, destroy, them (cf. [Dt 7:16](https://ref.ly/Deut7:16)); ´דִּמְעָתִּי ל [Psalm 42:4](https://ref.ly/Ps42:4) cf. [80:6](https://ref.ly/Ps80:6) (vid. אכל p. 37:b supr.); ´ל of evil deeds of wicked [Jb 20:14](https://ref.ly/Job20:14); עֵץ בְּלַחְמוֺ [Je 11:19](https://ref.ly/Jer11:19) appar.fig.of destroying the *prophet and his house,* but read prob. בְּלֵחוֺ *in its freshness* (i.e. untimely), so Hi Gf Che Gie (vid. לֵחַ sub לחח supra).

> \[Str3902] †לִחֲמִי **n.pr.m.** brother of Goliath of Gath (q.v.) slain byElhanan, acc. to [1 Ch 20:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:5) לחמי את; LXX Ελεμεε, Λεεμει, Λοομι); orig. reading prob. (as in ‖ [2 S 21:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:19)) בֵּית הַלַּחְמִי q.v. p. 112:a supra.

> \[Str3903] †לַחְמָס **n.pr.loc.** in Judah (32 Codd. לחמם);—[Jos 15:40](https://ref.ly/Josh15:40) LXX Μαχες, A LXXL Λαμ(μ)ας; perh. \=  *El\-laḥm,* c. 13 miles WNW. from Hebron, Knad loc. PEFMem.iii. 261 BuhlGeogr. 192\.

## לָחַץ (id: BDB04878)

\[Str3905] †לָחַץ **vb.** squeeze, press, fig. oppress (Ar. لَحَّصَ; Chr.\-Pal. ܠܚܨ SchwallyIdioticon 48 (with which NöM74 comp. Mand. הלאצא, *torture,* NS. ܚܵܠܸܨ *press together, urge, exert oneself)*; see also rare deriv. in Syr. PS1932);—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ל Ju [4:3](https://ref.ly/Judg4:3) \+ 2 t. etc.; *Impf.* sf. יִלְחָצֵנִי [Psalm 56:2](https://ref.ly/Ps56:2); 3 fs. וַתִּלְחַץ [Nu 22:25](https://ref.ly/Num22:25), etc.; *Pt.* pl. לֽחֲצִים [Ex 3:9](https://ref.ly/Exod3:9) \+ 2 t., etc.— **1\.** *squeeze, press:* sq. acc. \+ אֶל Ju [1:34](https://ref.ly/Judg1:34), [Nu 22:25b](https://ref.ly/Num22:25) (J); sq. acc. \+ בְּ instr. [2 K 6:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:32) shut the door *and press him* (back, out) *with the door.* **2\.** *oppress:* sq. acc. Pers. [Ex 22:20](https://ref.ly/Exod22:20) ((‖הוֺנָה), [23:29](https://ref.ly/Exod23:29) (both E), [Psalm 56:2](https://ref.ly/Ps56:2); of people [Ex 3:9](https://ref.ly/Exod3:9) (E; \+ acc. cogn.), Ju [2:18](https://ref.ly/Judg2:18), [4:3](https://ref.ly/Judg4:3), [6:9](https://ref.ly/Judg6:9), [10:12](https://ref.ly/Judg10:12), [1 S 10:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:18), [Am 6:14](https://ref.ly/Amos6:14), [2 K 13:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:4), [13:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:22), [Je 30:20](https://ref.ly/Jer30:20), [Psalm 106:42](https://ref.ly/Ps106:42); abs. ## לָחַשׁ (id: BDB04880)

\[Str3907] †\[לָחַשׁ] **vb.** whisper, charm (NH *id.;* Aram. לְחַשׁ ܠܚܰܫ; Eth. ለሐሰ; *whisper*, v. RSJPh xiv. 1885, 122, possibly originally serpent\-charming);—**Pi.** *Pt.* pl. קוֺל מְלַחֲשִׁים [Psalm 58:6](https://ref.ly/Ps58:6) *voice of whisperers* (i.e. *serpent\-charmers;* ‖חוֺבֵר תֲבָרִים). **Hithp.** *whisper together*, *Impf.* יִתְלַחֲשׁוּ c. עַל [Psalm 41:8](https://ref.ly/Ps41:8); *Pt.* pl. מִתְלַחֲשִׁים [2 S 12:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:19).

> \[Str3908] †לַ֫חַשׁ **n.\[m.]** a whispering, charming;—´ל [Is 26:16](https://ref.ly/Isa26:16); לָחַשׁ [Ec 10:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:11), [Is 3:3](https://ref.ly/Isa3:3), [Je 8:17](https://ref.ly/Jer8:17); pl. לְחָשִׁים [Is 3:20](https://ref.ly/Isa3:20).—**1\.** *serpent\-charming* [Ec 10:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:11); נְבוֺן לָחַשׁ [Is 3:3](https://ref.ly/Isa3:3) (prob. more general). **2\.** *charms, amulets*, worn by women, [Is 3:20](https://ref.ly/Isa3:20). **3\.** *whisper of prayer*, צָקוּן לַחַשׁ [Is 26:16](https://ref.ly/Isa26:16) *they pour forth a whisper of prayer* (but Koppe Bö Gr Di Du rd. צְקוֺן לַחַשׁ ‘*Zauberzwang,’ compulsion of (by) magic*).

> \[Str3873] †לוֺחֵשׁ **n.pr.m.** (\= *whisperer*) only c. art ´הַלּ, a chief of the people [Ne 3:12](https://ref.ly/Neh3:12), [10:25](https://ref.ly/Neh10:25).

> \[Str3909] לָט v. sub לוּט. p. 532

> \[Str3910] †לֽט **n.\[m.]** myrrh, or *‘ladanum’* (Gk.), an aromatic gum exuded by leaves of *cistus\-rose, cistus villosus*, TristrFFP, 235, and esp. Nat. Hist. Bib., 458 ff.; RiHWB 897, cf. LöwNo. 79 Di:[Gn 37:25](https://ref.ly/Gen37:25) NowArch. i. 64 (Sab. לדן, Sab. Denkm.:84; As. *ladunu* (fr. Damascus) cf. SchrCOT [Gn 37:25](https://ref.ly/Gen37:25), MBAk. 1881, pp. 413 ff.; Ar لَادَنٌ, whence Gk. λήδανον, λάδανον Hdtiii. 112\);—וּצְרִי וָלֽט נְכאֹת [Gn 37:25](https://ref.ly/Gen37:25), merchandise of Ishmaelite caravan, cf. [43:11](https://ref.ly/Gen43:11), present to Joseph (both J; LXX στακτή).

[1 S 13:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:20), obj. מַחֲרֶשֶׁת, אֵת, קַרְדֹּם, מַחֲרֵשָׁה; fig. עֵינַיִם לִי ´ל [Jb 16:9](https://ref.ly/Job16:9) my foe *whets his eye for me* **Pu.** *Pt.* תַּעַר מְלֻטָּשׁ [Psalm 52:4](https://ref.ly/Ps52:4) *a sharpened razor* (in sim. of evil tongue).

## לִיץ (id: BDB04893)

\[Str3887] † \[לִיץ] **vb.** soorn (NH *id.;* vb. not in cognates; cf. Ph. מלץ *interpreter* CIS22; CIS44; CIS88; Ar. لَاصَ is *turn aside* (intrans.); hence perh. prop. *speak indirectly* or *obliquely,* FlDe [Pr. 1:6](https://ref.ly/Prov1:6));—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 m. לַצְתָּ [Pr 9:12](https://ref.ly/Prov9:12); *Impf.* יָלִיץ [3:34](https://ref.ly/Prov3:34) \+ 2 t.; *Pt.* לֵץ [9:7](https://ref.ly/Prov9:7) \+ 11 t.; pl. לֵצִים [Psalm 1:1](https://ref.ly/Ps1:1) \+ 3 t.;—*scorn*, וְלַצְתָּ לְבַדְּ תִשָּא [Pr 9:12](https://ref.ly/Prov9:12) *if thou scornest thou alone shalt bear it* (opp. חָכַמְתָּ), c. acc. [14:9](https://ref.ly/Prov14:9), [19:28](https://ref.ly/Prov19:28); c. לְ, לַלֵּצִים הוּא־יָלִיץ *scorners he* (*God*) *scorneth* [3:34](https://ref.ly/Prov3:34); הַלֵּץ the scorner is proud and haughty [21:24](https://ref.ly/Prov21:24), delights in scorning [1:22](https://ref.ly/Prov1:22), is incapable of discipline [9:7](https://ref.ly/Prov9:7), reproof [9:8](https://ref.ly/Prov9:8), [15:12](https://ref.ly/Prov15:12), or rebuke [13:1](https://ref.ly/Prov13:1), cannot find wisdom [14:6](https://ref.ly/Prov14:6); is an abomination [24:9](https://ref.ly/Prov24:9), should be avoided [Psalm 1:1](https://ref.ly/Ps1:1); smitten and punished for the benefit of the simple [Pr 19:25](https://ref.ly/Prov19:25), [21:11](https://ref.ly/Prov21:11) and banished for the removal of contention [22:10](https://ref.ly/Prov22:10); judgment is prepared for him [19:29](https://ref.ly/Prov19:29); ‖עָרִיץ [Is 29:20](https://ref.ly/Isa29:20); wine is a scorner [Pr 20:1](https://ref.ly/Prov20:1).—**Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 mpl. sf. הֱלִיצֻנִי [Psalm 119:51](https://ref.ly/Ps119:51); *Pt.* מֵלִיץ [Gn 42:23](https://ref.ly/Gen42:23), [Jb 33:23](https://ref.ly/Job33:23); pl. cstr. מְלִיצֵי [2 Ch 32:31](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:31); sf. מְלִיצַי [Jb 16:20](https://ref.ly/Job16:20); מְלִיצֶיְ [Is 43:27](https://ref.ly/Isa43:27); —**1\.** *deride* [Jb 16:20](https://ref.ly/Job16:20), [Psalm 119:51](https://ref.ly/Ps119:51). **2\.** Pt. *interpreter* [Gn 42:23](https://ref.ly/Gen42:23) (E); fig. of intermediaries between God and man, [Jb 33:28](https://ref.ly/Job33:28), [Is 43:27](https://ref.ly/Isa43:27); *ambassador* [2 Ch 32:31](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:31). **Poïlel** *Pt.* pl. לֽעֲצִים (מְ dropped Köii. 479\) *scorners* [Ho 7:5](https://ref.ly/Hos7:5). **Hithp.** *Impf.* תִּתְלוֺצָצוּ *act as a scorner, shew oneself a mocker*, [Is 28:22](https://ref.ly/Isa28:22).

> \[Str3944] † לָצוֺן **n.\[m.]** scorning, [Pr 1:22](https://ref.ly/Prov1:22); אַנְשֵׁי לָצוֺן *men of scorning, scorners* \= לֵצִים [29:8](https://ref.ly/Prov29:8), [Is 28:14](https://ref.ly/Isa28:14).

> \[Str4426] † מְלִיצָה **n.\[f.]** satire, mocking\-poem Hb [2:6](https://ref.ly/Hab2:6), also [Pr 1:6](https://ref.ly/Prov1:6) (al. here *figure, enigma*).

* **Associated Passages:** Habakkuk 2:6; Hosea 7:5; Isaiah 28:14; Psalms 1:1; Proverbs 24:9; Job 33:23; Proverbs 13:1; Proverbs 20:1; Proverbs 21:11; Proverbs 1:6; Proverbs 19:25; Proverbs 15:12; Isaiah 29:20; Proverbs 29:8; Proverbs 9:12; Proverbs 21:24; Proverbs 22:10; Isaiah 28:22; Proverbs 9:8; Proverbs 3:34; 2 Chronicles 32:31; Job 33:28; Psalms 119:51; Job 16:20; Genesis 42:23; Proverbs 19:28; Isaiah 43:27; Proverbs 14:9; Proverbs 19:29; Proverbs 9:7; Proverbs 14:6; Proverbs 1:22


## לישׁ (id: BDB04896)

לישׁ (√ of foll., Thes לושׁ; perhaps \= *be strong* (Thes) cf. Ar. لَوْثٌ, لَيْثٌ, strength; لَاثَ (med. י) III. v. *be strong,* etc., appar. denom. \= *be lion\-like*).

> \[Str3918] † I. לַ֫יִשׁ **n.m.:[Jb 4:11](https://ref.ly/Job4:11)** lion (Ar. لَيْثٌ, Targum לֵיתָא; As. *nesûu, lion* is comp. by Hal (cf. HptKAT 2, 510\) BaZA iii. 1888, 60\);—*lion* [Jb 4:11](https://ref.ly/Job4:11), [Is 30:6](https://ref.ly/Isa30:6) (‖לָבִיא), גִּבּוֹר ´ל בַּבְּהֵמָה [Pr 30:30](https://ref.ly/Prov30:30).

> \[Str3919] † II. לַ֫יִשׁ **n.pr.loc.** (on meaning *lion*, cf. RS Sem. i. 156 (om. 2nd ed.));—former name of Dan, in extreme north of Canaan, Ju [18:7](https://ref.ly/Judg18:7), [18:14](https://ref.ly/Judg18:14), [18:27](https://ref.ly/Judg18:27), [18:29](https://ref.ly/Judg18:29) (v. דָּן p. 192 b); LXX Λαισα; \= לָשֶׁם q.v.; cf. BuhlGeogr. § 124\.

> \[Str3919,3889] † III. לַ֫יִשׁ **n.pr.m.** father of Michal's second husband, [1 S 25:44](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:44) \+ [2 S 3:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:15) Qr (Kt לושׁ), LXX A Λμεις, Σελλης, LXXL Ιωας, Σελλειμ.

> \[Str3919] † לַ֫יְשָׁה **n.pr.loc.** N. of Jerus. [Is 10:30](https://ref.ly/Isa10:30) (perh. akin to III. לַיִשׁ, v. Di); site unknown; v. KasterenZPV xiii. 101 conj. *ï Isawiye,* village two miles NE. fr. Jerus. (PEFMap; Mem. iii. 27 RobBR i. 437 cf. BuhlGeogr; 175\), LXX ἐν Σα, Λαισα.

> \[Str1980] לְ \= לְכָה *Imv.* of הָלַח q.v. p. 229

* **Associated Passages:** Judges 18:29; Judges 18:27; Judges 18:14; Job 4:11; 2 Samuel 3:15; Proverbs 30:30; Judges 18:7; Isaiah 10:30; Isaiah 30:6; 1 Samuel 25:44

(but Ju [1:18](https://ref.ly/Judg1:18) del. as gloss Bu Kit GFM al.); land [Jos 10:42](https://ref.ly/Josh10:42) (D), [Dn 11:18](https://ref.ly/Dan11:18); water\-courses (fords), Ju [3:28](https://ref.ly/Judg3:28), [7:24](https://ref.ly/Judg7:24), [7:24](https://ref.ly/Judg7:24), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Judg12:8); obj. men (usu. princes, kings) in battle Ju [7:25](https://ref.ly/Judg7:25), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Judg8:12), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Judg8:14), [Jos 11:12](https://ref.ly/Josh11:12), [11:17](https://ref.ly/Josh11:17) (D), [2 Ch 22:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:9), [33:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:11); captives and spoils [2 S 8:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:4) \= [1 Ch 18:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:4); of Saul's seizing the kingdom (הַמְּלוּכָה), i.e. acquiring it actually by force of arms [1 S 14:47](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:47); also of Samson's *catching* the foxes Ju [15:4](https://ref.ly/Judg15:4); of a lion *catching* his prey [Am 3:4](https://ref.ly/Amos3:4); of a trap, snare, לָכוֺד יִלְכּוֺד [3:5](https://ref.ly/Amos3:5). † **2\.** fig. of entrapping men [Je 5:26](https://ref.ly/Jer5:26), [Psalm 35:8](https://ref.ly/Ps35:8), cf. ## לָעַב (id: BDB04921)

\[Str3931] †\[לָעַב] **vb.** jest (NH Hiph. *id.;* Targum לְעַב Ethpa. *id.;* Syr. ܠܥܰܒ Ethpa. *mock, delight oneself, be greedy;* Ar. لَعِبَ *play, sport, jest*);—only **Hiph.** *Pt.* ´וַיִּהְיוּ מַלְעִבִים בְּ [2 Ch 36:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:16) *and they were* (continually) *making jest at* the messengers of God.

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Chronicles 36:16


## לָעַג (id: BDB04922)

\[Str3932] †\[לָעַג] **vb.** mock, deride; also (Niph.) stammer, poet. (NH Hiph. *id.;* Targum לְעֵג Aph. *id.;* ܠܥܶܓ *stutter* (cf. NH לִגְלֵג *mock*, Targum לַגְלֵג *id.;* Syr. ܠܰܓܠܶܓ *stutter*, Ar. لَجْلَجَ *id.*));—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. לָֽעֲגָה [2 K 19:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:21), [Is 37:22](https://ref.ly/Isa37:22); *Impf.* יִלְעַ־ג [Jb 22:19](https://ref.ly/Job22:19), [Psalm 2:4](https://ref.ly/Ps2:4) יִלְעָג [Jb 9:23](https://ref.ly/Job9:23); 3 fs. תִּלְעַג [Pr 30:17](https://ref.ly/Prov30:17); 2 ms. תִּלְעַג [Psalm 59:9](https://ref.ly/Ps59:9), ´וַתִּל [Jb 11:3](https://ref.ly/Job11:3); 1 S. אֶלְעַג [Pr 1:26](https://ref.ly/Prov1:26); 3 mpl. יִלְעֲג־וּ [Psalm 80:7](https://ref.ly/Ps80:7); *Pt.* לֽעֵג [Pr 17:5](https://ref.ly/Prov17:5), [Je 20:7](https://ref.ly/Jer20:7); —*mock, deride, have in derision,* of Jerus. triumphing over enemy (c. לְ Pers. vel rei) [2 K 19:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:21) \= [Is 37:22](https://ref.ly/Isa37:22) (‖בזה); of the innocent [Jb 22:19](https://ref.ly/Job22:19) (‖שמח); of God (c. לְ) [9:23](https://ref.ly/Job9:23), [Psalm 2:4](https://ref.ly/Ps2:4), [59:9](https://ref.ly/Ps59:9); abs. of wisdom [Pr 1:26](https://ref.ly/Prov1:26) (‖שחק); of wicked (c. לְ) [Pr 17:5](https://ref.ly/Prov17:5) (‖שמח), [30:17](https://ref.ly/Prov30:17) (‖בוז), [Je 20:7](https://ref.ly/Jer20:7) (‖שחק); so also perh. [Psalm 80:7](https://ref.ly/Ps80:7) (read לָנוּ for לָמוֺ with LXX SyrVer Vulgate), cf. (abs.) [Jb 11:3](https://ref.ly/Job11:3). **Niph.** *Pt.* נִלְעַג לָשׁוֺן…עַם [Is 33:19](https://ref.ly/Isa33:19) *a people stammering of tongue* (i.e. barbarians, cf. va; but rd. נעלג \[√ עלג] GrMonatsschr. 1884, 45; ‖עִמְקֵי שָפָה). **Hiph.** (late; cf. NH) *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיַּלְעֵג [Ne 3:33](https://ref.ly/Neh3:33); 2 ms. תַּלְעִיג [Jb 21:3](https://ref.ly/Job21:3); 3 mpl. יַלְעִגוּ [Psalm 22:8](https://ref.ly/Ps22:8), וַיַּלְעִגוּ [Ne 2:19](https://ref.ly/Neh2:19); *Pt.* מַלְעִגִים [2 Ch 30:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:10); —*mock, deride,* always in bad sense: c. לְ Pers. [Ne 2:19](https://ref.ly/Neh2:19) (‖בזה), [Psalm 22:8](https://ref.ly/Ps22:8); c. עַלם Pers. [Ne 3:33](https://ref.ly/Neh3:33); c. בְּ Pers. [2 Ch 30:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:10) (‖השחיק); abs. [Jb 21:3](https://ref.ly/Job21:3).—So also יִלְעִיג (or יִלְעַג) [Pr 18:1](https://ref.ly/Prov18:1) for MT יִתְגַּלַּע Grl. c (v. גלע, p. 166 supra).

> \[Str3933] †לַ֫עַג **n.\[m.]** mocking, derision, (barbarous) stammering;—abs. ´ל [Jb 34:7](https://ref.ly/Job34:7) \+ 2 t. \+ [Psalm 123:4](https://ref.ly/Ps123:4) (´הַל MT cstr. c. art.—so KöSynt. § 303e—but prob. insert לְ foll. ## לָעַז (id: BDB04928)

\[Str3937] †\[לָעַז] **vb.** talk indistinctly, unintelligibly (NH *id.,* in deriv. (לַעַז *foreign language*, לָעוּז *foreigner*), also *murmur, remonstrate;* Syr. ܠܥܶܙ *talk indistinctly;* Ar. لَغَزَ *distort;* IV. *talk obscurely, ambiguously*);—only **Qal** *Pt.* עַם לֽעֵז [Psalm 114:1](https://ref.ly/Ps114:1) *a people talking unintelligibly* (‖מִצְרַיִם).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 114:1


## לָעַט (id: BDB04929)

\[Str3938] †\[לָעַט] **vb.** swallow (greedily) (NH *id.,* Hiph. *stuff* cattle with food; Syr. ܠܘܽܥܳܛܳܐ *jaw;* cf. Ar. لَغَطَ *speak confusedly, utter indistinct sounds*);—only **Hiph.** *Imv.* ms. sf. causat, הַלְעִיטֵנִי [Gn 25:30](https://ref.ly/Gen25:30) (J) *let me swallow*, sq. מִן rei.

> \[Str3939] †לַעֲנָה **n.f.** wormwood (NH *id.;* Ar. لَعْنٌ is *curse* (vb. لَعَنَ *id.*), so Nab. לצנת (vb. לען) cf. CookGloss.);—only fig. of bitter things:—of perverted justice, [Am 5:7](https://ref.ly/Amos5:7), [6:12](https://ref.ly/Amos6:12) (‖ראֹשׁ; of result of idolatry, [Dt 29:17](https://ref.ly/Deut29:17) (‖ *id.*); of ´י's chastisement [Je 9:14](https://ref.ly/Jer9:14) (‖מֵי־ראֹשׁ), [23:15](https://ref.ly/Jer23:15) (‖ *id.*), [La 3:15](https://ref.ly/Lam3:15) (‖מְרוֺרִים), v:[19](https://ref.ly/Lam3:19) (‖ראֹשׁ); sim. of bitter (מָרָה) result of intercourse with strange woman [Pr 5:4](https://ref.ly/Prov5:4).—On *wormwood (Artemisia absinthium,* etc., Gk. ἀψίθιον) cf. Löw80f. 401, 421 TristrNat, Hist. Bib. 493\.

> \[Str3886] לָעַע v. לוע

* **Associated Passages:** Genesis 25:30; Lamentations 3:15; Jeremiah 23:15; Deuteronomy 29:17; Amos 6:12; Amos 5:7; Proverbs 5:4; Jeremiah 9:14; Lamentations 3:19
* **Associated Articles:** לוּעַ (ID: `BDB04853@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## לפד (id: BDB04932)

לפד (√ of following; meaning unknown).

> \[Str3940] †לַפּיד **n.m.**:Ju [15:4](https://ref.ly/Judg15:4) torch (NH *id.*);—´ל abs. Ju [15:4](https://ref.ly/Judg15:4), [Is 62:1](https://ref.ly/Isa62:1); cstr. [Gn 15:17](https://ref.ly/Gen15:17), [Zc 12:6](https://ref.ly/Zech12:6); pl. לַפִּידִים Ju [15:5](https://ref.ly/Judg15:5) \+ 2 t., לַפִּידִם [Ex 20:18](https://ref.ly/Exod20:18), לַפִּדִים Ju [7:16](https://ref.ly/Judg7:16) \+ 3 t.; cstr. לַפִּידֵי [Dn 10:6](https://ref.ly/Dan10:6):—*torch*, Ju [7:16](https://ref.ly/Judg7:16), Ju [7:20](https://ref.ly/Judg7:20), [15:4](https://ref.ly/Judg15:4), [15:4](https://ref.ly/Judg15:4), [15:5](https://ref.ly/Judg15:5) (kindled, ´הִבְעִיר אֵשׁ בְּ); לַפִּיד אֵשׁ [Gn 15:17](https://ref.ly/Gen15:17) ( \+ עָשָׁן, תַּנּוּר; JE), [Zc 12:6](https://ref.ly/Zech12:6) (sim. of conquering power of \[chiefs of] Judah ; ‖כִּיּוֺר אֵשׁ), לַפִּידֵי אֵשׁ [Dn 10:6](https://ref.ly/Dan10:6) (sim. of eyes of angel in vision; ‖וּפָנָיו כְּמַרְאֵה בָרָק); sim. of flashes (reflected from) darting chariots [Na 2:15](https://ref.ly/Nah2:15) (‖בְּרָקִים); ´ל of lightning\-flashes [Ex 20:18](https://ref.ly/Exod20:18) (E; \+ בְּוֺלֽת *thunder\-peals*); of flashing water\-drops expelled by snortings of crocodile [Jb 41:11](https://ref.ly/Job41:11) (‖כִּידוֺדֵי אֵשׁ); of flame between cherubim Ez [1:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:13) (‖גַּחֲלֵ־י אֵשׁ, אֵשׁ); sim. of Zion's glorious deliverance [Is 62:1](https://ref.ly/Isa62:1) (‖נֹגַהּ).—לַפִּיד [Jb 12:5](https://ref.ly/Job12:5) v. sub פִּיד.

## לָפַת (id: BDB04937)

\[Str3943] †\[לָפַת] **vb.** twist, turn, grasp twisting motion (Ar. لَفَتَ *twist, wring;* As. *lapâtu, overthrow,*, DlHWB 382\);—**Qal** *Impf.* וַיִּלְפֹּת Ju [16:29](https://ref.ly/Judg16:29) sq. acc., Samson *grasped* the two middle columns. **Niph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיִּלָּפֵת [Ru 3:8](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:8) the man started up *and twisted himself;* 3 mpl. יִלָּֽפְתוּ [Jb 6:18](https://ref.ly/Job6:18) the paths of their way \[i.e. of the streams] *turn aside (twist, wind*, with lessening force), so AV Hi De Da Bu \> Ew Ol Di Siegf Du, who read Qal יִלְפְּתוּ or Pi. יְלַפְּתוּ, making ארחות, \= *caravans,* subj.

> \[Str3887] לֵץ, לָצוֺן v. sub ליץ. p.539

> \[Str3946] †לַקּוּם **n.pr.loc.** northern border\-town of Naphtali, [Jos 19:33](https://ref.ly/Josh19:33) (P), LXX Δωδαμ, LXXL Λακουμ. so also LagOnom 275, 24; site unknown.

* **Associated Passages:** Judges 16:29; Joshua 19:33; Ruth 3:8; Job 6:18

[Gn 8:9](https://ref.ly/Gen8:9) (J), [Ex 4:17](https://ref.ly/Exod4:17), [12:22](https://ref.ly/Exod12:22) (JE), [Dt 15:17](https://ref.ly/Deut15:17) \+ oft.; [Gn 40:11](https://ref.ly/Gen40:11) (E; of plucking grapes. [2 S 4:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:6) read סֹקְלָהfor לֽקְחֵי, v. חִטָּה, p. 334 supra, and סקל). This easily passes into **2\.** *take and carry along* with oneself, כֶּסֶף קְחוּ בְיֶדְכֶם [Gn 43:12](https://ref.ly/Gen43:12) (J), [Jos 9:11](https://ref.ly/Josh9:11) (JE), [1 K 14:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:3), [2 K 5:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:5), [8:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:8), [8:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:9), [9:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:1) all [Pr 7:20](https://ref.ly/Prov7:20) (all c. בְּיַד); [Gn 43:15](https://ref.ly/Gen43:15), [Jos 9:4](https://ref.ly/Josh9:4) (both JE), \+. **3\. a.** *take* from, or out of, sq. act. rei vel Pers., \+ מִן loc.: [Gn 2:22](https://ref.ly/Gen2:22) (rib from man), [3:22](https://ref.ly/Gen3:22) (man from ground; both J) ; [Dt 19:12](https://ref.ly/Deut19:12), [1 S 17:49](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:49), [1 K 17:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:19) \+; וַיִּקַּ ח אֶת־הַחֶרֶב מֵעַל יֶרֶךְ יִמִינוֺ Ju [3:21](https://ref.ly/Judg3:21) (i.e. *drew* it); c. ב instr. בְּמֶלְקָחַיִם מֵעַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ ´ל [Is 6:6](https://ref.ly/Isa6:6); sq. מִן partit. [2 S 12:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:4). **b.** *take, carry away:* Ez [3:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:14) *sprit lifited me up and carried me away;* fig.of passion carrying one away [Jb 15:12](https://ref.ly/Job15:12). **c.** *take away* from, so as to deprive of, sq. acc. [Gn 42:36](https://ref.ly/Gen42:36) (E); sq. acc.\+ מִן, [Gn 44:29](https://ref.ly/Gen44:29) (מֵעִם; J), [1 K 11:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:34), [Psalm 51:13](https://ref.ly/Ps51:13); לֽקֵחַ אֶ־ת ´י אֲדֹנֶיְ מֵעַל ראֹשְֶָׁ *is about to take away thy lord from over thy head*, so v:[5](https://ref.ly/Ps51:5); cf. abs. אֵינֶנּוּ אֹתוֺ אלהים ´כִּי־ל [Gn 5:24](https://ref.ly/Gen5:24) (P); more oft. without מִן, [Gn 27:36](https://ref.ly/Gen27:36), [27:36](https://ref.ly/Gen27:36), [30:15](https://ref.ly/Gen30:15), [30:15](https://ref.ly/Gen30:15), [31:1](https://ref.ly/Gen31:1) (all J), Ju [11:13](https://ref.ly/Judg11:13), [11:15](https://ref.ly/Judg11:15), [15:6](https://ref.ly/Judg15:6), [18:24](https://ref.ly/Judg18:24), [1 S 12:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:3), [12:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:3), [2 S 12:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:4), [1 K 11:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:34), [Jb 12:20](https://ref.ly/Job12:20); abs. [Jb 1:21](https://ref.ly/Job1:21); אֶתֶּן־לְךָ מֶלֶךְ בְּאַפִּי וְאֶקַּח בְּעֶבְרָתִי [Ho 13:11](https://ref.ly/Hos13:11); of the sword, taking away its victims Ez [33:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:4), [33:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:6); fig. of taking away understanding [Ho 4:11](https://ref.ly/Hos4:11). **d.** esp. *take away* life [1 K 19:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:10), [19:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:14), [Psalm 31:14](https://ref.ly/Ps31:14), [Pr 1:19](https://ref.ly/Prov1:19), [Jon 4:3](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:3). **4\.** *take* to or for a person: **a.** for oneself, sq. acc. \+ לְ, \= *appropriate* to oneself [Dt 7:25](https://ref.ly/Deut7:25), [Nu 8:16](https://ref.ly/Num8:16) (P), [Dt 22:7](https://ref.ly/Deut22:7), [2 K 12:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:6); c. לְ as dat. comm., [Zc 11:7](https://ref.ly/Zech11:7); אֶת־שְׁנֵי יְלָדַי לוֺ לַעֲבָדִים ´ל [2 K 4:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:1) *to take my two sons for himself for slaves;* so (without לְ reflex.), תִּקָּחֶנּוּ לְעֶבֶד עוֺלָם [Jb 40:28](https://ref.ly/Job40:28) *wilt thou take him as slave for ever?* **b.** \= *procure, get,* sq. acc. \+ לְ, קְחוּ לָכֶם תֶּבֶן[Ex 5:11](https://ref.ly/Exod5:11) (J) *get you straw;* לֽקֵחַ לוֺ קָלוֺן [Pr 9:7](https://ref.ly/Prov9:7) *getteth to himself shame;* so oft., esp. קַח לְ, etc., [Gn 6:21](https://ref.ly/Gen6:21), [12:19](https://ref.ly/Gen12:19), [16:21](https://ref.ly/Gen16:21), [45:19](https://ref.ly/Gen45:19) \+; without לְ, [Ne 5:2](https://ref.ly/Neh5:2) *let us procure corn*, and eat and live; of acquiring a field [Pr 31:16](https://ref.ly/Prov31:16). **c.** *take possession of*, sq. acc., of land [Dt 29:7](https://ref.ly/Deut29:7), [Jos 11:16](https://ref.ly/Josh11:16), [11:23](https://ref.ly/Josh11:23) (all D), [2 Ch 16:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:5); cf. פְּקֻדָּתוֺ יִקַּח אַחֵר [Psalm 109:8](https://ref.ly/Ps109:8) *his office let another take;* fig. הַלַּיְלָה הַהוּא יִקָּחֵהוּ אֹפֶל [Jb 3:6](https://ref.ly/Job3:6) *that night—let darkness take possession of it!* **d.** \= *select, choose,* ´י ´וְאֶתְכֶם ל[Dt 4:20](https://ref.ly/Deut4:20), cf. v:[34](https://ref.ly/Deut4:34) ( \+ לוֺ), [1 K 11:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:37); of men [Dt 1:15](https://ref.ly/Deut1:15), [Jos 3:12](https://ref.ly/Josh3:12) (JE; \+ לָכֶם); \+ מִן [Dt 1:23](https://ref.ly/Deut1:23), [Jos 4:2](https://ref.ly/Josh4:2) (JE). **e.** esp. *take* in marriage: (I) for another, esp. a son, c. לְ, וְלָקַחְתָּ֫ אִשָּׁה לִבְנִי, [Gn 24:4](https://ref.ly/Gen24:4) *and thou shalt take a wife for my son*, so v:[7](https://ref.ly/Gen24:7), v:[38](https://ref.ly/Gen24:38), v:[40](https://ref.ly/Gen24:40), v:[48](https://ref.ly/Gen24:48) (all J), [21:21](https://ref.ly/Gen21:21) (E), [Je 29:6b](https://ref.ly/Jer29:6); (2\) more oft. for oneself, usu. c. לְ reflex. (sts. \+ לְאִשָּׁה), [Gn 4:19](https://ref.ly/Gen4:19), [6:2](https://ref.ly/Gen6:2), [11:29](https://ref.ly/Gen11:29), [12:19](https://ref.ly/Gen12:19) (all J), \+oft.; without לְ [Gn 20:2](https://ref.ly/Gen20:2), [20:3](https://ref.ly/Gen20:3) (E), etc. **f.** \= *receive, accept,* esp. a bribe, gift, ransom, etc., [1 S 8:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:3), [12:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:3) c, [12:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:4) (both sq. מִן), [Am 5:12](https://ref.ly/Amos5:12), [2 K 5:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:15), [5:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:16), [5:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:20), [5:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:23), [5:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:26), [5:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:26), [Psalm 15:5](https://ref.ly/Ps15:5); interest\-money Ez [18:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:13), [18:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:17), [22:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:12); inheritance [Jos 13:8](https://ref.ly/Josh13:8), [18:7](https://ref.ly/Josh18:7)(both D), [Nu 34:14](https://ref.ly/Num34:14), [34:14](https://ref.ly/Num34:14), [34:15](https://ref.ly/Num34:15) (P); of earth, receiving Abel's blood [Gn 4:11](https://ref.ly/Gen4:11)(J); chastisement [Is 40:2](https://ref.ly/Isa40:2); of *perceiving* a sound, [Jb 4:11](https://ref.ly/Job4:11) *mine ear received a whisper of it; receive* mentally: וְלָקַח בְּעָלָיו [Ex 22:10](https://ref.ly/Exod22:10) (E) i.e. shall accept the oath as satisfactory; בָּרֵח לָקַחְתִּי [Nu 23:20](https://ref.ly/Num23:20) (JE), i.e. *I have received* (commandment by revelation) *to bless; receive* instruction [Pr 24:32](https://ref.ly/Prov24:32), [Je 2:30](https://ref.ly/Jer2:30) \+; entanglements of temptation [Pr 22:25](https://ref.ly/Prov22:25). **g.** *take*, as preliminary to further action: [Gn 31:34](https://ref.ly/Gen31:34)(E), *she took them* and put them; Ju [3:25](https://ref.ly/Judg3:25) *take* key and open ; [Jos 2:4](https://ref.ly/Josh2:4) *she took* the two men and hid them; [1 K 18:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:4) Obadiah *took* 100 prophets and hid them; [19:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:21) Elisha *took* the yoke of oxen and slew them, cf. [2 S 17:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:19), [18:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:18); sts. wholly redundant, קְחוּ וַעֲשוּ עֲגָלָה חֲדָשָׁה [1 S 6:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:7) *take and prepare a new cart,* etc.; somewhat different is הַלּֽקְחִים לְשׁוֺנָם וַיִּנְאֲמוּ נְאֻם [Je 23:31](https://ref.ly/Jer23:31), i.e. *they take* (\= make use of) their own tongues, and talk oracles. **5\.** *take* up, upon \=*put* upon, sq. עַל: וַתִּקַּת תָּמָר אֵפֶר עַל־ראֹשָׁהּ [2 S 13:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:19) *and Tamar put ashes upon her head;* וַיִּקָּחֶהָ עַל־הַחֲמוֺר Ju [19:28](https://ref.ly/Judg19:28); cf. יִפְרשֹׁ כְּנָפָיו יִקָּחֵהוּ [Dt 32:11](https://ref.ly/Deut32:11) *he spreadeth his wings, he taketh him up.* **6\.** \= *fetch:* קְחִי־נָא לִי מְעַט־מַיִם [1 K 17:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:10) *fetch me, pray, a little water,* cf. v:[11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:11), v:[11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:11), [2 K 2:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:20), [4:41](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:41); וְקַח־לִי מִשָּׁם שְׁנֵי גְּדָיֵי עִוִּים [Gn 27:9](https://ref.ly/Gen27:9) *and fetch me thence two kids,* cf. v:[13](https://ref.ly/Gen27:13) (J); \+ בְּיָדִי [1 S 21:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:9); an offering [Lv 12:8](https://ref.ly/Lev12:8) (P; ‖הֵבִיא v:[6](https://ref.ly/Lev12:6)); c. human obj. oft. nearly \= *summon,* לָקֹב אֹיְבַי לְקַחְתִּיְ [Nu 33:11](https://ref.ly/Num33:11) (JE) *to curse mine enemies did I summon thee*, Ju [11:5](https://ref.ly/Judg11:5), [1 S 16:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:11); שְׁלַח וְקַח אֹתוֺ אֵלַי [1 S 20:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:31) *send and bring him unto me;* [2 K 3:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:15), [6:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:13) \+; of ´י, summoning his people from exile, מִשָּׁם יְקָּחְֶָ [Dt 30:4](https://ref.ly/Deut30:4). **7\.** *take\=lead, conduct* (with or without contact): וַיִּקַּח אֶת־שְׁנֵי בָנָיו עִמּוֺ [Gn 48:1](https://ref.ly/Gen48:1); גַּם בְּקַרְכֶם קְחוּ [Ex 12:32](https://ref.ly/Exod12:32) (both E); וְאֶת־עַמּוֺ לָקַח עִמּוֺ [Ex 14:6](https://ref.ly/Exod14:6) (J) *and his people he took with him;* cf. v:[7](https://ref.ly/Exod14:7) (J), [Jos 8:1](https://ref.ly/Josh8:1) (JE), Ju [4:6](https://ref.ly/Judg4:6); אֶקָּֽחְֲ אֶל־מָקוֺם אַחֵר [Nu 23:27](https://ref.ly/Num23:27) *I will take thee unto another place,* cf. v:[14](https://ref.ly/Num23:14), v:[28](https://ref.ly/Num23:28) (all JE); ´י subj., וָאֶקַּח אֶת־אֲבִיכֶם [Jos 24:3](https://ref.ly/Josh24:3) (E) *and I took your father* Abraham from beyond the river; לַמָּוֶת הַצֵּל לְקֻחִים *rescue those led to death.* **8\.** *take \= capture, seize:* לָקַחְתִּי בְחָזְקָה [1 S 2:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:16) *I will take by force*, sc. flesh ; of spoils, ´לאֹ ל בֶּצַע כֶּסֶף Ju [5:19](https://ref.ly/Judg5:19) *gain of silver they took not;* of capture of ark [1 S 5:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:1); bronze [2 S 8:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:3), etc.; territory [Gn 48:22](https://ref.ly/Gen48:22) (E), [Dt 3:14](https://ref.ly/Deut3:14); cities [Nu 21:25](https://ref.ly/Num21:25) (JE),.[Dt 3:14](https://ref.ly/Deut3:14), [Jos 11:19](https://ref.ly/Josh11:19) (D), [1 S 7:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:14), [2 S 8:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:1); of capture of hippopotamus [Jb 40:24](https://ref.ly/Job40:24); of seductions of woman, וְאַל־תִּקָּֽחְֲ בְּעַפְעַפֶּיהָ [Pr 6:25](https://ref.ly/Prov6:25) *and let her not capture thee with her eyelids.* **9\.** *take \= carry off:* **a.** as booty [Gn 14:11](https://ref.ly/Gen14:11), [1 S 27:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:9), [30:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:16), [30:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:18), [30:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:19), [30:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:20), [1 K 14:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:26), [14:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:26). **b.** as prisoners [Gn 14:12](https://ref.ly/Gen14:12), [2 K 18:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:32) \= [Is 36:17](https://ref.ly/Isa36:17), [2 K 23:34](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:34) \= [2 Ch 36:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:4). **10\.** in phr. *take* vengeance: וְנִקְחָה נִקְמָתֵנוּ מִמֶּנּוּ [Je 20:10](https://ref.ly/Jer20:10) *and we will take our vengeance upon him;* abs. נָקָם אֶקַּח [Is 47:3](https://ref.ly/Isa47:3) *vengeance will I take*.

 †**Niph.** *Pf.* נִלְקַח [1 S 4:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:22); נִלְקָח [4:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:11), Ez [33:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:6); 3 fs. נִלְקָחָה [1 S 4:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:17); *Impf.* 3 fs. וַתִּלָּקַח [Est 2:8](https://ref.ly/Esth2:8), [2:16](https://ref.ly/Esth2:16); 1 S. אֶלָּקַח [2 K 2:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:9) ; *Inf. cstr.* הִלָּקַח [1 S 4:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:19), [4:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:21), sf. הִלָּֽקְחוֺ [1 S 21:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:7) ;—**1\.** *be captured*, of ark [1 S 4:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:11), [4:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:17), [4:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:19), [4:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:21), [4:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:22). **2\.** *be taken away, removed*, of shewbread [1 S 21:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:7) ; of removal by death Ez [33:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:6) ; of translation of Elijah [2 K 2:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:9) (c. מֵעִם Pers.) **3\.** *be taken, brought* unto (אֶ־ל), only [Est 2:8](https://ref.ly/Esth2:8), [2:16](https://ref.ly/Esth2:16). † **Pu.** *Pf.* לֻקַּח [Gn 3:23](https://ref.ly/Gen3:23) \+, 3 fs. לְֻקַָחָ־ה [2:23](https://ref.ly/Gen2:23), 2 ms. לֻקָּחְתָּ [3:19](https://ref.ly/Gen3:19), etc. ;—**1\.** *be taken* from, out of ( מִן of source ) [Gn 2:23](https://ref.ly/Gen2:23), [3:19](https://ref.ly/Gen3:19), [3:23](https://ref.ly/Gen3:23) (all J); cf. וְלֻקַּח מֵהֶם קְלָלָה [Je 29:22](https://ref.ly/Jer29:22) *and there shall be taken from them* ( derived from their case ) *a curse\-formula.* **2\.** \= *be stolen* from, Ju [17:2](https://ref.ly/Judg17:2) ( ל Pers.) **3\.** *be taken captive,* [Je 48:46](https://ref.ly/Jer48:46), cf. [Is 52:5](https://ref.ly/Isa52:5). **4\.** *be taken away, removed* ( \= **Niph. 2** ), of Elijah's translation [2 K 2:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:10) (c. מֵאֵת Pers.), of death [Is 53:8](https://ref.ly/Isa53:8) (c. מִן, prob. instr.) † **Hoph.** *Impf.* יֻקַּח [Gn 18:4](https://ref.ly/Gen18:4) \+, etc.;—**1\.** *be taken, brought* unto [Gn 12:15](https://ref.ly/Gen12:15) (J; sq. בֵּית *to the house of*), [18:4](https://ref.ly/Gen18:4) (abs.) **2\.** *be taken* out of [Jb 28:2](https://ref.ly/Job28:2) (מִן of source ), from, of Ez [15:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek15:3) (מִן partit.) **3\.** *be taken away* from [Is 49:24](https://ref.ly/Isa49:24) (מִן Pers.); abs. v:[25](https://ref.ly/Isa49:25). † **Hithp.** *Pt.* fs. אֵשׁ מִתְלַקַּ֫חַת lit. fire *taking hold of itself*, of lightning [Ex 9:24](https://ref.ly/Exod9:24) (JE), Ez [1:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:4), exact signif. dub.; *in close succession, incessant* Di Sm, *infolding itself*, i.e. a mass of fire, Thes RobGes A VVB SS, \> of *quivering* flashes Buhl, of *forked* or *zigzag* lightning Da. 

> \[Str3948] † לֶ֫קַח **n.m.**:[Pr. 4:2](https://ref.ly/Prov4:2) learning, teaching ( concrete; prob. *what is received* );—´ל abs. [Is 29:24](https://ref.ly/Isa29:24) \+ 5 t.; sf. לִקְחִי [Dt 32:3](https://ref.ly/Deut32:3), [Jb 11:4](https://ref.ly/Job11:4) ; לִקְחָהּ [Pr 7:21](https://ref.ly/Prov7:21); —**1\.** *instruction*, subjective, as personal acquirement; יִלְמְדוּ לֶָקַח [Is 29:24](https://ref.ly/Isa29:24), they *shall learn instruction* (‖בִּינָה); וְיוֺסֶף לֶָקַח [Pr 1:5](https://ref.ly/Prov1:5) a wise man will hear *and will increase* (his) *learning*, so [9:9](https://ref.ly/Prov9:9). **2\.** *teaching*, obj., as thing taught, יַעֲרֹף כַּמָּטָר לִקְחִי [Dt 32:2](https://ref.ly/Deut32:2) (‖אִמְרָתִי), [Jb 11:4](https://ref.ly/Job11:4) ; טוֺב נָתַתִּי לָכֶם ´ל [Pr 4:2](https://ref.ly/Prov4:2) ; also \= *teaching\-power, persuasiveness*, ´מֶתֶק שְפָתַיִם יוֺסִיף ל [Pr 16:21](https://ref.ly/Prov16:21) *sweetness of lips increaseth persuasiveness* (v. מתק), וְעַל שְפָתָיו יוֺסִיף לֶקַח v:[23](https://ref.ly/Prov16:23); in bad sense, of seductive words of adulteress, ´בְּרֹב ל [Pr 7:21](https://ref.ly/Prov7:21) (‖בְּחֵלֶק שְפָתֶיהָ).

> \[Str3949] † לִקְחִי **n.pr.m.** a Manassite, [1 Ch 7:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:19), LXX Λακεειμ, A Λακεια, LXXL Λοκεειμ.

> \[Str4455] † I. מַלְקוֺחַ **n.m.**:[Is 49:25](https://ref.ly/Isa49:25) booty, prey (cf. לָקַח **9**);—´הֲיֻקַּח מִגִּבּוֺר מ [Is 49:24](https://ref.ly/Isa49:24) (‖שְׁבִי), cf. v:[25](https://ref.ly/Isa49:25) (‖ *id.*); [Nu 31:26](https://ref.ly/Num31:26) (‖ *id.*), [31:11](https://ref.ly/Num31:11) (‖שָׁלָל), v:[12](https://ref.ly/Num31:12) (‖ id. \+ שְׁבִי, i.e. here, only, disting. fr. captives), v:[32](https://ref.ly/Num31:32) (‖יֶתֶר הַבּ֔ז), v:[27](https://ref.ly/Num31:27).

> \[Str4455] † II. \[מַלְקוֺחַ] **n.\[m.] jaw** (as *taking, seizing* food);—du. sf. וּלְשׁוֺנִי מֻדְבָּק מַלְקוֺחָי [Psalm 22:16](https://ref.ly/Ps22:16) *and my tongue is made to cleave to my jaws*, i.e. my gums.

> \[Str4457] † מֶלְקָחַ֫יִם, \[מַלְקָחַיִם] **n.\[m.] du.** tongs, snuffers;—**1\.** tongs used at altar of temple, for lifting coal, in Is.'s vision, [Is 6:6](https://ref.ly/Isa6:6). **2\.** lamp *snuffers*, in temple [1 K 7:49](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:49) \= [2 Ch 4:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:21); in tabern. sf. ( ref. to lamp\-stand, מְנוֺרָה) מַלְקָחֶיהָ [Ex 25:38](https://ref.ly/Exod25:38), [37:23](https://ref.ly/Exod37:23), [Nu 4:9](https://ref.ly/Num4:9) (all P).

> \[Str4727] † \[מִקָּח **n.\[m.]** a taking, receiving;—cstr. מִקַּח־שֹׁחַד [2 Ch 19:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:7) *a taking of a bribe*.

> \[Str4728] † \[מִקָּחָה] **n.f.** ware (prop. thing *received* sc. in trade; cf. לָקַח in NH); only pl. הַמִּקָּחוֺת [Ne 10:32](https://ref.ly/Neh10:32) *(their) wares.*

## לָקַט (id: BDB04948)

\[Str3950] † \[לָקַט] **vb.** pick or gather up, specif. glean ( NH *id.* and נָקַט; Ar. لَقَطَ *pick up* (as a bird, grains, etc.); Aram. לְקַט, and נְקַט; ܠܩܰܛ; Mand. לגט (NöM 54, also on Talm. נקט); cf. As. *lakâtu* DlHWB 385 (but ט \= *t*?)) ;—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. לָֽקְטוּ [Ex 16:22](https://ref.ly/Exod16:22), [Nu 11:8](https://ref.ly/Num11:8), ´וְל consec. [Ex 16:4](https://ref.ly/Exod16:4), לָקָטוּ [Ex 16:18](https://ref.ly/Exod16:18); *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִלְקְטוּ [Ex 16:5](https://ref.ly/Exod16:5), יִלְקֹטָוּן [Psalm 104:28](https://ref.ly/Ps104:28), וַיִּלְקְטוּ [Ex 16:17](https://ref.ly/Exod16:17), [16:21](https://ref.ly/Exod16:21); 2 mpl. sf. תִּלְקְטֻהוּ [Ex 16:26](https://ref.ly/Exod16:26); *Imv.* mpl. לִקְטוּ [Gn 31:46](https://ref.ly/Gen31:46), [Ex 16:16](https://ref.ly/Exod16:16); *Inf. cstr.* לִלְקֹט [Ex 16:27](https://ref.ly/Exod16:27) \+ 2 t. ;—**1\.** *pick up, gather,* c. acc., stones [Gn 31:46](https://ref.ly/Gen31:46) (J), lilies Ct [6:2](https://ref.ly/Song6:2); usu. the manna: [Ex 16:4](https://ref.ly/Exod16:4), [16:5](https://ref.ly/Exod16:5), [16:26](https://ref.ly/Exod16:26) (J), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Exod16:21), v:[22](https://ref.ly/Exod16:22) (P); sq. מִן partit. v:[16](https://ref.ly/Exod16:16) (P); so abs. v:[27](https://ref.ly/Exod16:27), [Nu 11:8](https://ref.ly/Num11:8) ( both JE ), [Ex 16:17](https://ref.ly/Exod16:17), [16:18](https://ref.ly/Exod16:18) (P); of animals gathering food תִּתֵּן לָהֶם יִלְקֹטָוּן [Psalm 104:28](https://ref.ly/Ps104:28). **2\.** specif. *glean*, abs., [Ru 2:8](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:8) (c. בְּ loc.; usu. Pi. in this sense ). **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 fs. וְלִקְּטָה consec. [Ru 2:16](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:16), לִקֵָּטָה [Ru 2:17](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:17), [2:18](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:18); 2 fs. לִקַּטְתְּ [Ru 2:19](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:19); *Impf.* וַיְלַקִּט [Gn 47:14](https://ref.ly/Gen47:14) \+ 2 t.; 3 fs. תְּלַקִּט [Ru 2:15](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:15), ´וַתּ [Ru 2:3](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:3), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:17); 2 ms. תְּלַקִּט [Lev 19:9](https://ref.ly/Lev19:9) \+ 2 t.; 1 S. אֲלַקֳּטָה [Ru 2:2](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:2), [2:7](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:7) (cf. BaNB 152, 489\); *Inf. cstr.* לְלַקִּט [Ru 2:15](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:15) \+ 2 t.; *Pt.* מְלַקִּט [Is 17:5](https://ref.ly/Isa17:5), pl. מְלַקְּטִים Ju [1:7](https://ref.ly/Judg1:7), [Je 7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18) ;—**1\.** *gather, gather up*, fallen grapes, [Lv 19:10](https://ref.ly/Lev19:10) (H), arrows [1 S 20:38](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:38), (fire\-)wood [Je 7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18); abs. (food from ground) Ju [1:7](https://ref.ly/Judg1:7) (cf. GFM); \= *pick, pick off,* [2 K 4:39](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:39), [4:39](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:39) (\+ מִן of source ). **2\.** fig. *collect* (money) [Gn 47:14](https://ref.ly/Gen47:14) (J). **3\.** specif. *glean*, after the reapers, usu. abs. [Ru 2:3](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:3), [2:7](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:7) (‖אָסַף), v:[17](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:17) ( all c. בְּ loc.), v:[15](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:15) ( בֵּין loc.), v:[19](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:19) (adv. loc.), also v:[2](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:2) (where בְּ may be loc. or partit., cf. [Is 17:5](https://ref.ly/Isa17:5) (שִׁבֳּלִים, sim. of devastation of land; ‖אָסַף קָצִיר), [Ru 2:17](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:17), [2:18](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:18); c. acc. cogn. לֶקֶט [Lv 19:9](https://ref.ly/Lev19:9) \= [23:22](https://ref.ly/Lev23:22) (H). **Pu.** *Impf.* 2 mpl. תְּלֻקְּטוּ [Is 27:12](https://ref.ly/Isa27:12) *ye shall be picked up* (of Isr. under fig. of grains or berries, after the threshing process of ´י's judgment). **Hithp.** *Impf.* וַיִּתְלַקְּטוּ Ju [11:3](https://ref.ly/Judg11:3) and *there collected themselves* worthless men unto (אֶל) Jephthah (*were raked together* GFM).

## לָקַק (id: BDB04951)

\[Str3952] † \[לָקַק] **vb.** lap, lick (NH *id.,* Pi.; Ar. لَقَّ *lick, lap* of dog (Wahrm));—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. לָֽקְקוּ [1 K 21:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:19); *Impf.* 3 ms. יָלֽק Ju [7:5](https://ref.ly/Judg7:5), [7:5](https://ref.ly/Judg7:5); 3 mpl. יָלֽ֫קּוּ [1 K 21:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:19), וַיָּלֽ֫קּוּ [1 K 22:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:38) ;—*lap,* יָלֽק בִּלְשׁוֺנוֺ מִ־ן הַמַּיִם Ju [7:5](https://ref.ly/Judg7:5), like of dog v:[5](https://ref.ly/Judg7:5) (abs.); of dogs *lapping up* blood (acc.), [1 K 21:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:19), [21:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:19), [22:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:38). **Pi.** *Pt.* pl. c. art. הַמְַלַקְקִים Ju [7:6](https://ref.ly/Judg7:6) (בְּיָדָם אֶל־פִּיהֶם is erron. gloss, v. GFM), v:[7](https://ref.ly/Judg7:7); in both \= *lap, lap up* (\= **Oal** v:[5](https://ref.ly/Judg7:5)).

* **Associated Passages:** Judges 7:5; Judges 7:6; 1 Kings 22:38; Judges 7:7; 1 Kings 21:19


## לקשׁ (id: BDB04952)

לקשׁ (√ of foll.; NH לקשׁ *be late* (Hiph. and deriv.); Aram. ܠܰܩܶܫ *make* or *do late,* לְקִישָׁא, ܠܩܺܝܫܳܝܳܐ *late;* vulg. Ar. لَقَسَ *be late* (Hauran), Wetzst in De:[Job 24:6](https://ref.ly/Job24:6), so II. Wahrm ; لَقِّيسَ *late*, DozySuppl. ii. 543\).

> \[Str3954] † לֶ֫קֶשׁ **n.\[m.]** prob. after\-growth, after\-math , i.e. spring\-crop (cf. GASm12 Proph. i. 109\);—only (2 t.) [Am 7:1](https://ref.ly/Amos7:1), בִּתְחִלַּת עֲלוֺת הַלָּקֶשׁ וְהִנֵּה :לֶקֶשׁ אַחַר נִּזֵּי הַמֶּלֶח.—\> Other interpr. are: *late\-rain growth* (fr. מַלְקוֺשׁ q.v.), HoffmZAW iii. (1883\) 116; *leaf\-and\-herb\-gathering* in spring, We after KleinZPV iv. 1881, 83; Anderlindviii. 1886, 62\.

> \[Str4456] † מַלְקוֺשׁ **n.m.**:[Je 3:3](https://ref.ly/Jer3:3) latter\-rain, spring\-rain (i.e. showers of March–April; important, as strengthening and maturing crops) ; ´מ [Ho 6:3](https://ref.ly/Hos6:3) \+ 7 t. ;—related to גֶּשֶׁם as particular to general, opp. יוֺרֶה (*former rain,* q.v.): [Ho 6:3](https://ref.ly/Hos6:3), [Je 5:24](https://ref.ly/Jer5:24); so opp. מוֺרֶה [Jo 2:23](https://ref.ly/Joel2:23); particular under מָטָר [Dt 11:14](https://ref.ly/Deut11:14) (opp. יוֺרֶה), [Zc 10:1](https://ref.ly/Zech10:1) (\+ מְטַר־גֶּשֶׁם), [Jb 29:23](https://ref.ly/Job29:23); ‖רְבִבִים [Je 3:3](https://ref.ly/Jer3:3); ´עָב מ [Pr 16:15](https://ref.ly/Prov16:15) *spring\-rain\-cloud* (sim. of king's favour).

> \[Str3953] † \[לָקַשׁ] **vb.** only **Pi.** 3 mpl., prob. denom. fr. לֶקֶשׁ \= take the aftermath, i.e. *take everything* (Wetzst in DeHiob 26:6\) (\> Buhl II. לקשׁ, cf. Ar. لَقَثَ *schnell zusammenraffen* \[cf. Lane2668]) ;—*despoil;* :וְכֶרֶם רָשָׁע יְלַקֵּֽשׁוּ [Jb 24:6](https://ref.ly/Job24:6) *and the vineyard of the wicked* (but rd. prob. עָשִׁיר *the rich* Bu Du) *they despoil* (‖קָצַר).

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 3:3; Hosea 6:3; Amos 7:1; Proverbs 16:15; Deuteronomy 11:14; Job 29:23; Zechariah 10:1; Job 24:6; Joel 2:23; Jeremiah 5:24

## לשׁן (id: BDB04962)

לשׁן (√ of foll.; Thes conj. orig. mng. *lick* (cf. لسّ, لسب, لسم), so Hup\-Now:[Psalm 32:4](https://ref.ly/Ps32:4)).

> \[Str3956] †לָשׁוֺן **n.m.**:[Jos 7:29](https://ref.ly/Josh7:29) and (more oft.) **f.**:[Is 28:11](https://ref.ly/Isa28:11) tongue (NH *id.;* also As. *lišânu;* Ar. لِسَانٌ; Eth. ልስኣን፡; Aram. לִשָּׁן, לִישָּׁן, ܠܶܫܳܢܳܐ (all *li\-*); Zinj. לשן DHMSendsch. 59\);—abs. ´ל [Ex 4:10](https://ref.ly/Exod4:10) \+; הַלָּשֹׁן [Jos 15:2](https://ref.ly/Josh15:2); cstr. לְשׁוֺן [Jos 7:21](https://ref.ly/Josh7:21) \+; sf. לְשׁוֺנִי [2 S 23:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:2); לְשֹׁנוֺ [Ex 11:7](https://ref.ly/Exod11:7) \+ 2 t.; לְשׁוֺנוֺ Ju [7:5](https://ref.ly/Judg7:5), [Est 1:22](https://ref.ly/Esth1:22); לְשׁוֺנְ [Psalm 34:14](https://ref.ly/Ps34:14); לְשׁוֺנְכֶם [Is 59:3](https://ref.ly/Isa59:3), etc.; pl. לְשֹׁנוֺת abs. [Psalm 31:21](https://ref.ly/Ps31:21), [Is 66:18](https://ref.ly/Isa66:18); cstr. [Zc 8:23](https://ref.ly/Zech8:23); sf. לְשֹׁנֹתָם [Gn 10:20](https://ref.ly/Gen10:20), [10:31](https://ref.ly/Gen10:31); —**1\.** *tongue* of men, **a.** used in lapping Ju [7:5](https://ref.ly/Judg7:5); cleaving to the palate (חֵק), in thirst [La 4:4](https://ref.ly/Lam4:4), so as to produce dumbness [Jb 29:10](https://ref.ly/Job29:10) (‖קוֺל נֶחְבָּא), [Psalm 137:6](https://ref.ly/Ps137:6), Ez [3:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:26) (‖נֶאֱלַם); cleaving to jaws (מַלְקוֺחַ) [Psalm 22:16](https://ref.ly/Ps22:16); בַּצָּמָא נָשָׁתָּה ´ל [Is 41:17](https://ref.ly/Isa41:17) *their tongue for thirst is parched;* תִּמַּק בְּפִיהֶם ´לְ [Zc 14:12](https://ref.ly/Zech14:12) (of ´י's judgment); a choice morsel is held ´תַּחַת ל [Jb 20:12](https://ref.ly/Job20:12) (fig. of mischief), cf.
